 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
FOREWORD by the minister

 Our national curriculum is the culmination of our efforts over a period of seventeen
 years to transform the curriculum bequeathed to us by apartheid. From the start of
 democracy we have built our curriculum on the values that inspired our Constitution
 (Act 108 of 1996). The Preamble to the Constitution states that the aims of the
 Constitution are to:

 • heal the divisions of the past and establish a society based on democratic
 values, social justice and fundamental human rights;

 • improve the quality of life of all citizens and free the potential of each person;

 • lay the foundations for a democratic and open society in which government is
 based on the will of the people and every citizen is equally protected by law;
 and

• build a united and democratic South Africa able to take its rightful place as a sovereign state in the family of
 nations.

Education and the curriculum have an important role to play in realising these aims.

In 1997 we introduced outcomes-based education to overcome the curricular divisions of the past, but the experience
of implementation prompted a review in 2000. This led to the first curriculum revision: the Revised National Curriculum
Statement Grades R-9 and the National Curriculum Statement Grades 10-12 (2002).

Ongoing implementation challenges resulted in another review in 2009 and we revised the Revised National
Curriculum Statement (2002) to produce this document.

From 2012 the two 2002 curricula, for Grades R-9 and Grades 10-12 respectively, are combined in a single document
and will simply be known as the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12. The National Curriculum Statement for
Grades R-12 builds on the previous curriculum but also updates it and aims to provide clearer specification of what
is to be taught and learnt on a term-by-term basis.

The National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12 accordingly replaces the Subject Statements, Learning Programme
Guidelines and Subject Assessment Guidelines with the

(a) Curriculum and Assessment Policy Statements (CAPS) for all approved subjects listed in this document;

(b) National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of the National Curriculum Statement
 Grades R-12; and

(c) National Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12.

MRS ANGIE MOTSHEKGA, MP
MINISTER OF BASIC EDUCATION

YEKHARIKHULAMU NEKUHLOLA

1.1 Sendlalelolwati

Sitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 (i-NCS) sinika inchubomgomo yekharikhulamu nekuhlola
etikolweni.

Kute kwentiwe ncono kusebenta kwaso leSitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe sichitjiyelwe, kwentelwa kutsi
letichibiyelo ticale kusebenta ngaBhimbidvwane 2012. Kwakhiwe umculu munye weNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu
neKuhlola kwaleso naleso sifundvo lotawusebenta esikhundleni seTitatimende Tetifundvo, Ticondziso Tetinhlelo
Tekufundza neTicondziso Tekuhlolwa Kwetifundvo kumaBanga R-12.

1.2 Sibutsetelo

(a) LeSitatimende seKharikhulamu Savelonkhe emaBanga R-12 (Bhimbidvwane 2012) simele sitatimende
 senchubomgomo yekufundza nekufundzisa etikolweni taseNingizimu Afrika, kantsi sicuketse loku
 lokulandzelako:

 (i) Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola leso naleso sifundvo lesisemtsetfweni;

 (ii) Umculu wenchubomgomo i-National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of
 the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12 kanye

 (iii) Nemculu wenchubomgomo, i-National Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12 (Bhimbidvwane 2012).

 (b) LeSitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 (Bhimbidvwane 2012) singena esikhundleni
 saleTitatimende teKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe letimbili lekungito letisebenta nyalo, letatiwa ngekutsi:

 yaHulumende nombolo 23406 mhlaka - 31 Inkhwekhweti 2002, kanye ne-

 Nombolo 25545 mhlaka - 6 Imphala 2003 kanye nakuGazethi yaHulumende Nombolo 27594 mhlaka -
 17 Inkhwekhweti 2005.

(c) Letitatimende tekharikhulamu yavelonkhe letibekwe ngalokucacile endzinyaneni (b) (i) na (ii) ngenhla tifaka
 ekhatsi letinchubomgomo letilandzelako lokutawuya ngekuya kwandze kutikhweshisela eceleni Sitatimende
 seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 (Bhimbidvwane 2012) emkhatsini waleminyaka 2012-2014:

 (i) Titatimende teTinkhundla teKufundza,/teTifundvo, Ticondziso Tetinhlelo Tekufundza neTicondziso
 Tekuhlolwa kweTifundvo temaBanga R-9 nemaBanga R-12 ;

 General Education and Training Band, leyashicilelwa emculwini weSatiso saHulumende Nombolo 124
 kuGazethi yaHulumende Nombolo 29626 yashicilelwa mhlaka 12 Indlovana 2007.

1.3 Tinhlosojikelele teKharikhulamu yaseNingizimu Afrika

(a) Sitatimende seKharikhulamu Yavelonkhe emaBanga R-12 sinika inchazelo yaloko lokumele kufundvwe
 etikolweni taseNingizimu Afrika ngalokuphatselene nelwati, emakhono nemagugu ekuphila. Lekharikhulamu
 itawucinisekisa kutsi bafundzi batfola babuye basebentise lwati nemakhono ngetindlela letitawubonakala
 etimphilweni tabo. Ngaloko-ke lekharikhulamu igcugcutela lwati ngesimongcondvo semmango wangakubo,
 ibe icikelela loko lokumcoka lokwentiwa emhlabeni jikelele.

(b) Sitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 siphumelelisa letinhloso letilandzelako:

 • kuhlomisa bafundzi ngelwati, emakhono nemagugu ekuphila lafanele, kute banetiseke baphindze babambe
 lichaza emmangweni njengetakhamuti telive lelikhululekile. Loku kutawentiwa ngaphandle kwekunaka
 simonhlalo setemnotfo, buve, bulili, simo semtimba, nobe likhono lekuhlakanipha labanalo;

 • kutsi batfole imfundvo lephakeme;

 • kulungiselela bafundzi kutsi bakwati kumela timo letehlukile kuleto tasetikolweni temfundvo lephakeme
 naleto tasemsebentini; kanye

 • nekunika bacashi imininingwane leyenele mayelana nemakhono ebafundzi.

(c) Sitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 senganyelwe ngulemigomo lelandzelako:

 • Ingucuko kutenhlalo: kucinisekisa kutsi kungafani kwemfundvo lokwakwentiwa esikhatsini lesengca
 kuyalungiswa, nekutsi tonkhe takhamuti titfola ematfuba emfundvo lalinganako kuto tonkhe tigaba.

 4 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 • Kufundza gekutimisela nangalokujulile: kugcugcutela tindlela tekufundza ngekutimisela nangalokujulile
 kunekutsi bafundze ngekuhlabelela ngaphandle kwekuvisisa loko labafundziswa kona.

 • Lwati lolusetulu nemakhono lasetulu: linanincane lemacophelo elwati nemakhono lekumele azuzwe kulelo
 nalelo Banga emisiwe abuye abekwa ezingeni lelisetulu lekungenteka kutsi azuzwe bafundzi kuto tonkhe
 tifundvo.

 • Inchubekelembili: lokucuketfwe kanye nesimongcondvo saleso naleso sifundvo kukhombisa
 inchubekelembili kusuka kulokulula kufika kulokulukhuni.

 • Emalungelo ebantfu, khukhulelangoco, bulungiswa betemvelo netenhlalo: kungeniswa kwemitsetfomgomo
 nenchubo yebulungiswa betenhlalo netemvelo kanye nemalungelo ebantfu njengekubeka
 kweMtsetfosisekelo waseNingizimu Afrika. Sitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12
 (Jikelele) sibonisa luvelo esimeni sekwehlukahlukana njengebuphuya, kungalingani, buve, bulili, lulwimi,
 budzala, kukhubateka, naletinye timo.

 • Kuhlonipha lwati lwendzabuko: kwemukela umlandvo lonotsile nemagugu emdzabu alelive lokungito tintfo
 letibalulekile ekuphumeleliseni emagugu ekuphila lamiswe kuMtsetfosisekelo; kanye ne-

 • Kwetsembeka, lizinga lelifanele, nelikhono lekwenta: kunika imfundvo lenelizinga lelicatsanisekako
 neyalamanye emave ngebubanti nangekujula.

(d) Sitatimende seKharikhulamu yaVelonkhe emaBanga R-12 sihlose kukhicita bafundzi labakhona ku-

 • bona nekusombulula tinkinga bese batsatsa tincumo ngekutakhela imicabango lejulile

 • sebenta ngekubambisana nalabanye njengelilunga lelicembu, lenhlangano nelemmango ngemphumelelo;

 • hlelembisa, nekutiphatsa ngalokunetibopho kuko konkhe labakwentako ngendlela lenemphumelelo;

 • gcogca, hlatiya, hlela nekuhlola lwati ngalokujulile

 • chumana ngemphumelelo ngekusebentisa tibonwa, timphawu ne /nobe bugagu bekusebentisa lulwimi
 ngetindlela letinyenti;

 • sebentisa isayensi nebuchwepheshe ngemphumelelo nangalokujulile bakhombise kutibophelela
 kutendzawo netemphilo yalabanye; kanye neku-

 • khombisa kuvisisa umhlaba njengeluchungechunge lwetehlakalo ngekubona kutsi timongcondvo
 tekusonjululwa kwetinkinga atihambi todvwa.

(e) Khukhulelangoco kufanele kube ngiyo incenye lemcoka kulomkhakha, ngekuhlela nekufundzisa kuleso naleso
 sikolo. Loku kungenteka kuphela uma bonkhe bafundzisi bangaba nekuvisisa ngalokujulile indlela yekubona
 tihibe tekufundza kanye nendlela yekutivikela kanye nendlela yekuhlelela kwehlukahlukana.

 Lokuhamba embili ekulawuleni khukhulelangoco kwenta siciniseko sekutsi tihibe tibonwe, kuliwe nato, kuto
 tonkhe tinhlaka tekwesekela ummango wesikolo, lokufaka ekhatsi bafundzisi, Emacembu Ekwesekela Latinte
 Etigodzini (District-based), Emacembu laSesigabeni Sekwesekela Tikhungo, batali, kusetjentiswe Tikolo
 Letikhetsekile kutsi tibe Tikhungo letinguMtfombo welusito. Nangabe bafundzisi bafuna kulwa netihibe eklasini
 bafanele basebentise emasu lehlukene ekwehlukanisa ikharikhulamu, lafana nalawo latfolakala emculwini
 weLitiko Letemfundvo Lesisekelo lowatiwa ngekutsi i-Guidelines for Inclusive Teaching and Learning (2010).

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
SIGABA 2: KWETFLWA KWELULWIMI

SIGABA SABOKHEWANE

LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA KUMABANGA 1-3

2.1 SINGENISO

Esigabeni saboKhewane, emakhono labalulekile eluLwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kukharikhulamu ahlukaniswe
ngalendlela:

 Kulalela nekukhuluma
 Kucabanga neKunoma kanye neSakhi neKusetjentiswa
 Kufundza nemisindvo kweLulwimi lokuhlanganiswe nawo onkhe emakhono lamane
 elulwimi (kulalela, kukhuluma, kufundza nekubhala)
 Kubhala nekufundzisa kubhala ngesandla

Lokucuketfwe (lwati, imicondvo kanye nemakhono) eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
kuhlelwe ngetigamu eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola, kusetjentiswa letihloko
letilandzelako. LeSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola sitawunika bothishela loku:

• singeniso lesicuketse imihlahlandlela yekusebentisa umculu wesiGaba saboKhewane

• lokucuketfwe, imicondvo kanye nemakhono latawufundziswa esigamini ngasinye

• imihlahlandlela yekwabiwa kwesikhatsi

• tidzingo temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile kanye netiphakamiso teluhlolo lolungakahleleki

• luhla lwetinsita letiphakanyisiwe telibanga ngalinye

2.2 KUFUNDZA LULWIMI Lwekwengeta

Bafundzi beta esikolweni bati lulwimi lwabo lwasekhaya. Basuke sebakwati kulukhuluma ngalokushelelako, kantsi
basuke sebati emagama lamanyenti. Kukwati kufundza nekubhala eBangeni1 kwakhela etukwesisekelo selulwimi
lolukhulunywako. Ngaleyo ndlela, kuba malula kutsi ukwati kufundza nekubhala ngelulwimi lwakho.

Bafundzi nabacala kufundza lulwimi Lwekwengeta eBangeni 1, kumele bakhe sisekelo lesicinile sekukwati kukhuluma
lulwimi lwabo. Kumele beve emagama lamanyenti eSiswati labatawukwati kuwevisisa ngendlela lasetjentiswe ngayo.
Kulalela thishela nakafundza tindzaba etincwadzini letinemifanekiso (Tincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza) kuyindlela
lenhle yekwenta loku njengobe iphindze yesekele kutfutfukiswa kwekucalisa kufundza kwemfundzi. Ngesikhatsi
bafundzi bakhula, badzinga kunikwa ematfuba lamanyenti ekukhuluma lulwimi ngetindlela letilula. Kwenta njalo
kutakwakha sisekelo sekukwati kufundza nekubhala kubafundzi labaseBangeni 2 ne 3.

ENingizimu Afrika, bafundzi labanyenti bacala eBangeni 4 kusebentisa lulwimi Lwekwengeta, Siswati, njengeLulwimi
Lwekufundza neKufundzisa (LoLT). Loku kusho kutsi kumele babe sebanelwati lolusezingeni lelisetulu lweSiswati
nabacedza liBanga 3, kantsi kumele futsi babe sebakwati kufundza nekubhala kahle ngeSiswati. Kungako
inchubekelembili yabo yekukwati kufundza nekubhala kumele inonophiswe emaBangeni 2 ne 3.

 8 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
Bafundzi bayakwati kwendlulisela emakhono lamanyenti ekukwati kufundza nekubhala bawasusela elulwimini lwabo
lwasekhaya. Kwenta sibonelo, uma bafundzi bafundziswa kubhala kahle ngesandla elulwimini lwabo lwasekhaya,
bangakwati kusebentisa lelikhono lasebanalo nasebabhala Siswati. Uma bafundza imisindvo elulwimini lwabo
lwasekhaya, asisabi khona sidzingo sekuphindze bafundze ngebudlelwane emkhatsini wemsindvo nekupelwa kwayo
elulwimini lweSiswati. Lapha basuke sebadzinga kuphela kusebentisa lwati lwabo lweSiswati kanye nekufundza
ngalobudlelwane lobukhona emkhatsini wemisindvo nekupelwa kwayo lobehlukile elulwimini lweSiswati.

Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola lusebentisa litfuba
lemakhono ekukwati kufundza nekubhala umfundzi lasavele anawo elulwimini lwakhe lwasekhaya. Kwenta sibonelo,
imisebenti lenjengekufundza basitwa nguthishela leyetfulwa eBangeni 1 eLulwimini Lwasekhaya eSitatimendeni
seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola yetfulwa eBangeni 2 eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola. Loku ngulesikubita ‘kufundza lulwimi lwesibili’-
lokusho kutfutfukisa sisekelo lesicinile sekukwati kufundza nekubhala eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kanye nekwakhela
etukwalesisekelo eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola.

2.3 TIMONGCONDVO LETEHLUKENE TEKUFUNDZA LULWIMI

Etikolweni lapho bafundzi batawusebentisa khona lulwimi lwabo Lwekwengeta, Siswati njengelulwimi lwekufundza
nekufundzisa (LoLT) kusukela eBangeni 4, kubalulekile kutsi kufundza kunikwe sikhatsi lesenele sekufundza Siswati
esiGabeni saboKhewane. Kepha ngete kwaba njalo etikolweni lapho kusetjentiswa lolo lulwimi njengelulwimi
lwekufundza nekufundzisa (LoLT) kuwo onkhe emabanga. Kuleto tikolo, linyenti lebafundzi labafundza Siswati
neAfrikaans njengelulwimi lwasekhaya, abakhulumi letilwimi njengelulwimi lwasekhaya, kungako kumele lomsebenti
wekufundza lulwimi unikwe sikhatsi lesenele.

2.4 KWABIWA KWESIKHATSI

Lokwabiwa kwesikhatsi lokulandzelako kwetilwimi kutawucala kusebenta nga- 2012. Esigabeni saboKhewane
sikhatsi sitawulawulwa kuhleleka kwelulwimi lolusetjentiswa kuleso sikolo. Tikolo tingakhetsa kusebentisa linanikhulu
lesikhatsi nobe linanincane lesikhatsi sekufundziswa kweLulwimi Lwasekhaya kanye neLulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta kuye ngetidzingo tebafundzi. Linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwasekhaya neseLulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta sikhonjiswe ngekufakwa kubakaki kulelithebula lelingentasi.

2.6 KWETFULA LULWIMI LWEKUCALA Lwekwengeta

Thishela nakangenisa Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta eBangeni 1, kumele abe nendlela lelula yekuletsa
etingcondvweni tebafundzi umcondvo ‘welulwimi lwesibili. Indlela lenhle yekwenta loku kusebentisa iphaphethi
lenganikwa ligama ngelulwimi lwekwengeta, kwenta sibonelo, Iphaphethi lenguSibusiso. Esifundvweni sekucala,
thishela wetfula iphaphethi atjele bafundzi kutsi Sibusiso akakwati kukhuluma lulwimi lwabo lwasekhaya; ukwati
kukhuluma Siswati kuphela, ngako kutawumele bakhulume naye ngeSiswati. Sibusiso utawuhlala njalo aluphawu
lwasendlini yekufundzela Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta. Thishela angetfula iphaphethi yesibili (sib. Iphaphethi
lenguSibongile) kute atewukhombisa kukhulumisana emkhatsini wabo basebentisa lulwimi lwekwengeta (sib.
kubingelela).

 10 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
2.7 KULALELA Nekukhuluma

Kufundza lulwimi lwekwengeta cishe kuyefana nekufundza lulwimi lwasekhaya ngaphandle nje kwekutsi kona
kwenteka kamuva emphilweni yebafundzi. Eminyakeni yekucala yekuphila kwabo, bafundzi beva emagama
lamanyenti lalula elulwimi lasetjentiswe etimeni letitsite, lokuyintfo leyenta kutsi bakwati kutfola kancane kancane
luhlelo lwelulwimi kanye nelwatimagama lelulwimi lwabo lwasekhaya. Bacala ngekukhipha ligama linye nobe mabili,
lokumagama labawasebentisela kuveta tinchazelo netinhloso letehlukahlukene. Bakhona kuvisisa lulwimi lolushubile
kunekutsi balukhulume.

Kubalulekile kutsi bothishela bakukhumbule loku ngesikhatsi bafundzi bafundza lulwimi lwekwengeta. EBangeni
1, bafundzi kumele bembulelwe elulwimini lolukhulunywako ngekufundvwa kwetindzaba kanye nangeticondziso
labanikwa tona endlini yekufundzela. Kulalela tindzaba naticocwa yindlela lenhle kakhulu yekutsi bafundzi bafundze
lulwimi lwekwengeta. Thishela kumele ente loku:

• khetsa indzaba lelula nalenetakhi lesiphindzaphindza emagama, lokuyintfo leyenta kube khona kuvuselela
 kabusha kwesilulumagama kanye neluhlelo lwelulwimi

• kwenta lulwimi lwakhe luhlale lulula, akhulume angasheshisi, akhulume abe nguye angalingisi.

• kusebentisa kunyakatiswa kwemtimba, titfombe kanye netintfo letiphatsekako letitawesekela kuvisiseka
 kwendzaba

• kukhuluma indzaba tikhatsi letinyentana, aye ngekubandzakanya bafundzi ngekutsi nabo bahlanganyele
 kusho loko lokushiwo endzabeni

Lenye indlela yekwembulela bafundzi elulwimini lwekwengeta kulalela tindzaba (nobe ematheksthi lacuketse
emaciniso) letifundvwa nguthishela. Thishela ufundza encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza, lokuyincwadzi lenkhulu
lenemifanekiso lebhalwe ngemagama lamakhulu labatawabona bafundzi ngesikhatsi thishela afundza. Loku kubitwa
‘Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela’. Lobunye buhle Bekufundza Ngekuhlanganyela ngukutsi njengoba kwenta umsebenti
lomuhle wekulalela, kuphindze kutfutfukise likhono lemfundzi lekucalisa kufundza nekubhala. Kwenta sibonelo,
bafundzi batfola umcondvo walokubhaliwe (sib. kutsi sicala kufundza incwadzi ngaphambili sigcine ngemuva; nekutsi
sicala kufundza kusancele siye kusekudla sicale etulu siye entasi kwelikhasi), ngaleso sikhatsi bafundza bacala
kubona emagama lambalwa elulwimini lwekwengeta (sib. yena, bona). Bafundzi kumele batetayete umsebenti
weKufundza Ngekuhlanganyela njengobe kungumsebenti labatowenta etifundvweni teLulwimi Lwasekhaya lwabo.

Lenye indlela lenhle yekwembulela bafundzi elulwimini lwekwengeta kubanika ticondziso lokumele batente
ngemitimba yabo; kwenta sibonelo, thishela utsi, ‘Wota lapha, Thabo, ’ akhombisa ngekunyakatisa umtimba, bese
Thabo uyeta. Lendlela, leyatiwa njengendlela yekuphendvula ngemtimba, inebuhle bekutsi thishela ukhona kubona
ngekushesha kutsi Thabo uyakuvisisa yini loko lakushoko nobe cha bese uyakwati thishela kunika imphendvulo-
lokungaba yimphendvulo letsi ‘Wente kahle, Thabo,’ nobe aphindze sona leso sicondziso angasheshisi asigcizelele
ngekunyakatisa umtimba ngemandla. Lulwimi lwasendlini yekufundzela (sib. Wota embili utewuhlala phasi emadini)
lunika bafundzi ematfuba lamanyenti etindlela temvelo tekwetfula indlela yekuphendvula ngemtimba. Imilolotelo
leneminyakato, nayo iyindlela lenhle yekuhlanganisa lulwimi kanye nemsebenti lotsintsa umtimba ngendlela
letawesekela kuvisiswa kanye nekukhunjulwa kwelulwimi.

Buhle baletindlela letintsatfu letichazwe lapha ngenhla (kulalela tindzaba, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kanye
Nekuphendvula ngekunyakatisa umtimba) ngukutsi tonkhe letindlela tigcile ekufundzisweni kwelulwimi ngekulalela
sifundvo sekuvisisa. Kubafundzi labancane loku kususa ingcindzetelo yekukhuluma, kwehlise neluvalo kubuye

CAPS 11
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
kubente bagcile ekuvisiseni lulwimi. Nanobe kunjalo, kute bafundzi babe basebentisi belulwimi labanemakhono
elulwimini, kumele batetayete kukhuluma lulwimi.

Ekucaleni, lulwimi lolukhulunywa bafundzi kutakuba ngulelo loluhleleke ngandlelatsite-tingoma letifundvwa ngenhloko,
imilolotelo netinkondlo letineminyakato kanye nalolunye lulwimi loluhleleke ngandlelatsite lolufundvwe njengeticejana
netaga, kwenta sibonelo, ‘Sawubona, unjani?’ ‘Ngiyaphila, wena unjani?’ Njengoba bafundzi bacala kuvisisa lulwimi
lwekwengeta kancane kancane, kumele bacale kukhuluma, bacale ngeligama linye nobe mabili. Kwenta sibonelo,
nabaphendvula umbuto lobutwe nguthishela, ‘Ingabe uyitsandzile indzaba?’ Imphendvulo yemfundzi ‘Yebo’ nobe
‘Cha.’

Ekucaleni, lulwimi lolucalisako lolukhulunywa bafundzi kumele lube lwesekelwe (lukhonjiswe ngekwenta lubuye
lwesekelwe). Kwenta sibonelo, bafundzi bangacala kulingisa tindzaba thishela labacocele nobe labafundzele tona,
basebentise inkhulumomphendvulwano. Bafundzi bangabuye bacoce tona letindzaba labaticocelwe babe basitwa
nguthishela. Thishela kumele acinisekise kutsi bonkhe bafundzi batfola ematfuba ekukhuluma Siswati. Njengobe
bafundzi batakube bachubekela embili ngekushesha lokungafani, thishela kumele ahlele ematfuba ekukhuluma
(sib. ngemibuto layibutako) ngendlela letakuba sezingeni lemfundzi ngamunye. Ngesikhatsi bafundzi bachubeka
nemabanga, thishela kumele alindzele kutsi bafundzi bakwati kukhuluma kakhudlwana kantsi nemagama
labawakhulumako kumele aye ngekwandza.

Njengobe bafundzi bakhombisa inchubekelembili ekufundzeni Siswati, kumele basale betfulelwa tinhlobo letinyenti
tematheksthi. EBangeni 1, batawuba nelwati lolunyenti lwetindzaba letihlukene bese bacala kubona kwakheka
kanye netimphawu tendzaba lelandzisako (kwetfulwa balingisi, kuchazwa simonhlalo, kuvela inkinga ibuye
isonjululwe; indzaba lelandzisako ivame kucocwa kusetjentiswa sikhatsi lesengcile). EBangeni 2 Lulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola, kwetfulwa kulandza ngemlomo (sib.
Itolo, siye edolobheni. Sicale sayotsenga kudla esitolo lesikhulu sekudla. Sase siya emtapolwati, njll) kantsi eBangeni
3 kuhlanganiswa nekulandza ngekubhala. Kulandza luhlobo lwetheksthi lolubalulekile ngobe kwakha budlelwane
emkhatsini welulwimi lolukhulunywako kanye nelulwimi lolubhalwako. Esikhatsini lesinyenti sisebentisa kulandza
ngemlomo (sib. kucocela bantfu ngaloko lesikwentile), kodvwa siphindze sikubhale phasi. EBangeni 3, bafundzi
babuye betfulelwe indlela yekulandzelanisa kahle itheksthi yemlomo ( ticondziso letinjengetindlela tekupheka) kanye
nemibiko leyetfula lwati (sib. Tindlovu tilwane letinkhulu. Tihlala tihamba tingumhlabi, njll). tibonelo taletinhlobo
tematheksthi tinikwe eSigabeni 4 ekupheleni kwalomculu kwabuye kwachazwa takhi kanye netimphawu tato.

Imisebenti yekulalela / yekukhuluma leyentiwa emalanga onkhe, kanye/kabili ngeliviki leyakhelwe
etukwetingcikitsi letitsite

Sikhatsi lesenele kumele sibekelwe kulalela nekukhuluma eBangeni 1. Loku kubese kuyancishiswa emaBangeni 2
ne 3 lapho kusuke sekungeniswa khona kufundza nekubhala eLulwimini Lwesibili Lwekwengeta. Kumele kugcilwe
kakhulu emakhonweni ekulalela nekukhuluma kuso sonkhe siGaba saboKhewane.

Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola, kulalela nekukhuluma
kuhlelwe ngalendlela:

• luhla lwemisebenti lokumele yentiwe emalanga onkhe esikhatsini lesiliviki. Kukhetfwa kanye nelinani
 lemisebenti lokumele yentiwe lilanga ngalinye kweyeme kuthishela kanye nasesikhatsini lanaso; loku
 kutakwehluka kuye ngekutsi usebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi nobe linanikhulu lesikhatsi lesabelwe Lulwimi
 Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.

• umsebenti logcile kulokutsite, ‘kulalela tindzaba leticocwako naletifundvwako’, lokuyintfo lefundziswa kanye
 nobe kabili ngeliviki, kuye ngeliBanga kanye nelinani lesikhatsi lesikhona.

 12 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
Lemisebenti ihlelwa ngetingcikitsi letitsite. Kuphakanyiswa kutsi thishela akhetse tingcikitsi letine ethemini ngayinye,
kulokunye tibe ngaphasi kwaloko nangabe kusetjentiswa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Thishela kumele akhetse tingcikitsi letihambisana nekufundziswa kwelulwimi lwekwengeta. Letingcikitsi kumele
kube kuleto labatetayele bafundzi, lokungenani kube nguleto letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya, tiphindze tinike
ematfuba lamanyenti ekufundzisa lulwimi ngendlela lolusetjentiswe ngayo (sib. letingcikitsi kumele tinike ematfuba
ekukhombisa nekusebentisa tintfo letikhona endlini yekufundzela). Letinye tingcikitsi tinikwe njengetibonelo, kodvwa
loku kutiphakamiso nje kuphela; atisito tingcikitsi letimisiwe.

Sizatfu sekusebentisa tingcikitsi kutsi kwentiwe lula kuvuselelwa kabusha njalo kwesilulumagama kanye netakhi
telulwimi etimeni letifanele. Kwenta sibonelo, emagama lahambisana nemtimba (buso, emehlo, tindlebe, imphumulo,
umlomo, imikhono, imilente, tinyawo) kanye netindzawo latfolakala kuto (Khomba _____ yakho./Lena yi _____ yami./
Leti ti _____ tami) kumele bacale ngekuweva kanyentana etingcikitsini lasetjentiswe kuto; bafundzi kumele banikwe
ematfuba ekuwasebentisa lamagama. Ngesikhatsi bafundzi bendlulela emaBangeni 2 ne 3, kutawudzingeka banikwe
ematfuba ekuwafundza nekuwabhala lamagama. Bafundzi batawukwati kukhumbula nekusebentisa silulumagama
kanye netakhi tawo nabaloku bawavuselela njalo.

2.8 KUFUNDZA NEKUBHALA

Ngenca yetizatfu letinikwe lapha ngenhla, eBangeni 1 kugcilwa kakhulu ekutfutfukiseni lulwimi lolukhulunywako,
lokungulapho bafundzi batakube bafundza kubhala nekufundza ngeLulwimi lwabo Lwasekhaya. Kepha, emaBangeni
2 newe- 3 kumele kugcilwe ekutfutfukiseni likhono lekukwati kufundza nekubhala eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Kubaluleke kakhulu loku kubafundzi labatakube basebentisa iEnglish njengelulwimi lwekufundza nekufundzisa (LoLT)
eBangeni 4. Kumele bakwati kufundza nekubhala kuletinye tifundvo labatentako, babuye basebentise tincwadzi
tekufundzisa teSiswati esiGabeni Lesisemkhatsini. Loku kutawudzinga emazinga laphakeme ekukwati kufundza
nekubhala, ikakhulukati silulumagama lesibanti elulwimini lweEnglish.

Kufundza nekubhala kuphindze kufake sandla ekutfutfukiseni lulwimi lwebafundzi lweSiswati. Kufundza kwembulela
bafundzi ekufundzeni kwabo lulwimi lwekwengeta. Lucwaningo luveta kutsi kutfutfuka kwesilulumagama sebafundzi
kweyeme ekufundzeni labakwentako. Kubhala kubalulekile ngobe kuphocelela bafundzi kutsi bacabange ngeluhlelo
lwelulwimi kanye nesipelingi. Loku kukhutsata bafundzi kutsi basebentise lulwimi, banonophise kufundza lulwimi
baphindze benyuse kufundza lokungenamaphutsa.

Kungako linanikhulu lesikhatsi libekelwa imisebenti yekufundza nekubhala Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola emaBangeni 2 ne 3. Lemisebenti yeKufundza
neKubhala ihlekele ngalendlela:

2.8.1 Kutetayeta imibhalo lesendzaweni lebakuyo

Kusukela eminyakeni yakudzala, bafundzi baseNingizimu Afrika bavetelwa ebaleni imibhalo kumbe emagama
lamanyenti labhalwe etindzaweni lebakuto kusetjentiswe lulwimi lweSiswati, kwenta sibonelo, timphawu (timphawu
temgwaco, timphawu tetitolo, njll) nemabhokisi lavale tintfo letitsite. Bothishela bangasebentisa loku njengesicalo
sekufundzisa bafundzi kukwati kufundza nekubhala elulwimini lwabo lwekwengeta, kwenta sibonelo, ngekuletsa
emabhokisi lavale tintfo labawetayele nobe tikhangisi endlini yekufundzela kute babone kutsi bafundzi bayawati yini
emagama aletintfo letingekhatsi kumbe letikhangiswako. Kusukela ethemini 3 eBangeni 1, uma bafundzi sebakwati
kufundza nekubhala ngeLulwimi lwabo Lwasekhaya, thishela angacala kulebula tintfo letisendlini yekufundzela
asebentisa Lulwimi Lwasekhaya kanye neSiswati. Lemisebenti yesekela kufundza lokwenteka kungakahlelwa;
akusiyo imisebenti legcile ekukwatini kufundza nekubhala kantsi akukameli inikwe linanikhulu lesikhatsi.

CAPS 13
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
2.8.2 Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela

Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kwetfulwa eBangeni 1 kuchubeke naso sonkhe siGaba saboKhewane. Lomsebenti
ungumsebenti lobalulekile logcile ekutfutfukiseni lulwimi kanye nekukwati kufundza nekubhala. Inhloso yekufundza
ngekwabelana emaBangeni 1 kuniketa bafundzi litfuba lekutetayeta lulwimi lwabo lwekwengeta ngendlela levakalako
nalesekelako. Lenye inhloso yekufundza ngekwabelana kutfutfukisa kukwati kufundza nekubhala kwebafundzi
lokusacalisako elulwimini lwabo lwekwengeta. Batfutfukisa imicondvo yemagama labhaliwe baphindze bacale
kubona emagama lambalwa labhalwe ngeSiswati. Kulelizinga, thishela kumele ente loku:

• Kukhetsa itheksthi lelula lekhulisiwe (sib. Incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza) lebhalwe ngemagama lambalwa
 kepha ibe nemifanekiso leminyenti lemihle. Indzaba kumele ibe netakhi lesicacile nalesilula (sib. Tingulube
 letintsatfu). Kuyasita kakhulu uma lulwimi luphindvwaphindvwa futsi kube nekucombeleka kwemagama
 latawulandzela (sib. Iphi indlovu? Isehlatsini; iphi indlovu? Isehlatsini, njll) Ematheksthi kumele aye ngekuba
 lukhuni nakuloku kuchubeka umnyaka.

• Kukhuluma nebafundzi ngetitfombe kute batekuvisisa silulumagama lesisetjentisiwe. Babute imibuto ngelulwimi
 lwabo lwasekhaya. Basite bachumanise indzaba nalokwenteka etimphilweni tabo.

• Kufundza itheksthi tikhatsi letinyentana asebentisa umuno nobe ‘indvukwana yekukhomba’ kute bafundzi
 bamlandzele ngalesikhatsi achubeka netheksthi

• Kubuta imibuto lephatselene nendzaba

• Kubandzakanya bafundzi kancane kancane ‘ekufundzeni’ indzaba

Njengobe bafundzi bendlulela emaBangeni 2 ne 3 ematheksthi kumele aye ngekuba lukhunyana. Thishela
ubakhombisa indlela yekufundza ngalokushelelako abuye asebentise itheksthi kutfutfukisa silulumagama, kuvisisa,
emakhono ekuhlahlela emagama, kuvisisa takhi setheksthi, luhlelo lwelulwimi kanye netimphawu tekufundza.

2.8.3 Kufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa Nguthishela

EBangeni 2, bafundzi bacala umsebenti lomusha elulwimini lwekwengeta: Kufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa
Nguthishela. Kepha batawube bawetayele umsebenti njengobe bebasolomane bawenta elulwimini lwabo
lwasekhaya kusukela ekucaleni kweliBanga 1. Kulomsebenti, thishela udzinga tincwadzi tekufundza letitsite letihlelwe
ngekwemazinga ebulukhuni bawo. Thishela kumele ahlele bafundzi ngemacembu lahlelwe ngekwemakhono
lanebafundzi laba-6 kuya kula-10 bese ukhetsa incwadzi yekufundza lelungele lizinga labo. Thishela usebenta
nelicembu ngalinye kanye ngeliviki lokutawutsatsa emaminithi la-15 ngesikhatsi lalamanye emacembu afundza
ngababili nobe afundza ngekutimela nobe enta imisebenti lehambisana netheksthi, kwenta sibonelo, imisebenti
yekubhala lelula lenjengekucedzela imisho nobe kubeka imisho ngendlela lelandzelana kahle. Inhloso yekufundza
basitwa nguthishela ngukutsi thishela akwati kutfola litfuba lekubukana nemfundzi ngamunye kute atfutfukise
emakhono abo esifundvo sekuvisisa kanye nawekuhlela emalunga emagama elulwimini lwabo lwekwengeta.

 14 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 Ticondziso tekwakha emacembu ngekwemakhono
 Kute ubeke bafundzi ngemacembu, kutawumele ubacaphele ngesikhatsi bafundza tincwadzi tekufundza. Khetsa incwadzi
 yekufundza locabanga kutsi umfundzi utawukwati kuyifundza, kodvwa kungabi ngulelula kakhulu-kumele kube khona
 bulukhunyana lobukhona umfundzi latawubukana nabo. Uma umfundzi akwati kufundza ngalokushelelako, aphimisa kahle,
 kusho kutsi itheksthi isezingeni lakhe lekufundza. Uma umfundzi atfola bumatima, khetsa lelula udzimate ufike kuleyo
 lesezingeni lakhe lekufundza. Uma sewuhlole bonkhe bafundzi, sewungababeka ngemacembu latawuya ngekwemakhono.
 Tinyatselo letitawulandzelwa esifundvweni sekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela
 I. Khetsa itheksthi lefanele:
 Tincwadzi tekufundza letihlelwe ngetigaba titawusetjentiswa esikhatsini lesinyenti ngesikhatsi bafundza ngemacembu.
 Kumele tibe sezingeni leliphasi kunalelo lematheksthi lasetjentiswe esifundvweni sekufundza ngekuhlanganyela. Fundza
 itheksthi kucala bese ubhala silulumagama nobe luhlelo lwelulwimi lolungaba yinkinga kubafundzi. Loku kunganika
 thishela sifundvo langagcila kuso.
 II. Singeniso:
 Ngenisa luhlobo lwencwadzi (sib. lengasiwo emaciniso nobe lengemaciniso) kanye nesihloko. Sita bafundzi kutsi bakwati
 kuchumanisa sihloko kanye nalabakufundze emphilweni. Lenkhulumo kumele igcile kuloko lokutawufundvwa kute
 bafundzi bafundze ngemphumelelo (emaminithi lama-2 kuya kula-3).
 III. Kukhuluma ngetitfombe
 Sebentisa titfombe kute ungenise bafundzi esihlokweni uphindze ukhulume nganobe ngusiphi silulumagama lesisha.
 Yetama loku kugcile kuleso sihloko uphindze ube mfishane (emaminithi lama-2 kuya kula-3)
 IV. Kufundza kwekucala:
 Umfundzi ngamunye ufundza itheksthi. Thishela ucaphela indlela bafundzi lebatiphatsa ngayo nabafundza bese ukhetsa
 sifundvo lesengetiwe latawugcila kuso lesiphatselene naloko lakubonile ngesikhatsi bafundza. Thishela utawuya
 kumfundzi ngamunye alalele umfundzi afundza incenye lencane yetheksthi ngekuphimisela. Thishela utabakhutsata
 bafundzi ngekutsi ababute imibuto lelandzelako:
 • Ngabe ulindzele kutsi utawufundza ngani kulencwadzi?
 • Ngabe loko kukunika umcondvo lovakalako yini?
 • Wente kahle! Ukhonile kulungisa emaphutsa akho. Loko kunika umcondvo lovakalako.
 • Ngabe yini letawuvakala kahle kulomusho?
 • Buka imifanekiso.
 • Kungaba ngiko kodvwa phindza ubuke luhlavu lolusekucaleni.
 Kungenteka ekucaleni kutawumele ubute lemibuto ngelulwimi lwasekhaya lwebafundzi. Nanobe kunjalo, sheshe ucale
 kubuta imibuto ngeSiswati. Bafundzi kumele kube sebayayati lemibuto njengobe bebasolomane benta lomsebenti
 elulwimini lwabo lwasekhaya kusukela ekucaleni kweliBanga 1.
 V. Kuvisisa
 Buta bafundzi imibuto lemayelana netheksthi kute ucinisekise kutsi bayevisisa yini.
 VI. Kufundza kwesibili nekufundza lokulandzelako
 Emva kwemalanga bafundzi bayawuphindze bafundze itheksthi ngababili nobe ngamunye. Lokubalulekile lapha
 lokutawugcilwa kuko, kutfutfukisa kufundza ngekushelela kanye nekunika bafundzi ematfuba ekutfutfukisa silulumagama,
 luhlelo lwelulwimi nekuvisisa kahle itheksthi. Kufundza ngekuphindzelela kwesekela kutfutfuka kwekufundza
 ngekushelela elulwimini lwekwengeta.

Kungenteka bothishela abakakwetayeli kusebentisa Kufundza Ngemacembu ikakhulukati eklasini leLulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta. Ngako, bangangenisa lendlela kancane kancane. Uma sebativa batetsemba ngekuyisebentisa
eLulwimini Lwasekhaya, bangacala kuyisebentisa eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta. Kwanyalo, bothishela
bangafundzisa liklasi lonkhe lapho bonkhe bafundzi banekhophi yetheksthi lefanako umfundzi ngamunye atfole
litfuba lekuyifundza. Bothishela kumele bachubeke nekucaphela indlela bafundzi labatiphatsa ngayo nabafundza
baphindze babasite kutfutfukisa likhono labo lekuvisisa kanye nemakhono ekuhlahlela emagama.

Akukho sikhatsi lesenele seKufundza Basitwa Nguthishela eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni
seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola ngendlela lesikhona ngayo eLulwimini Lwasekhaya eSitatimendeni
seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola. Bothishela labasebentisa linanikhulu lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta batawukwati kusebenta nelicembu ngalinye lelincane kanye ngeliviki emaminithi la-15. Kepha labo
labasebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta ngete bakwati kwenta loko. Kutawumele
bente umsebenti wekufundza kweliklasi lonkhe endzaweni yeKufundza Basitwa Nguthishela.

CAPS 15
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
2.8.4 Kufundza ngababili nekufundza ngekutimela

Kufundza ngababili nobe kufundza ngekutimela kunika bafundzi litfuba lekutilungiselela kufundza kubuye kukhutsate
kufundzela kutitfokotisa. Ekufundzeni ngababili, bafundzi lababili bafundze kanye kanye nobe banikane litfuba
lekufundza.

Bafundzi kumele basebentise lesikhatsi kwenta tintfo letimbili: 1) kuphindze bafundze incwadzi lefundvwako
Ngesikhatsi Sekufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa Nguthishela badzimate bakwati kufundza ngekushelela 2) kufundzela
kutitfokotisa etincwadzini letifundvwako ekhoneni lekufundza/. Itheksthi kumele ibe sezingeni lelingaphasi kwalelo
lelisetjentiselwe Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kanye neKufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa Nguthishela.

Kunika bafundzi ematfuba ekutifundzela tincwadzi kubuye kutfutfukise likhono labo lekufundza ngekushelela,
ingce nje uma tincwadzi lebatifundzako tilula ngendlela yekutsi bafundza ngaphandle kwekusitwa. Tincwadzi
letimfisha, letilula, letinetheksthi lecombeleka lula kanye netitfombe letinemibala lehlukene tibalulekile kakhulu.
Labanye bothishela bayatsandza kunika bafundzi umsebenti wekuyofundza emakhaya - kufundza lokuphindvwako
kwencwadzi yelicembu nobe tincwadzi letilula ‘letijabulisako’. Lendlela yekufundza leyentiwa emalanga onkhe idlala
indzima lebalulekile ekusiteni bafundzi kutsi bakwati kufundza.

2.8.5 Imisindvo

Sigaba sekucala sekufundza kuhlahlela lulwimi lolubhalwako kwenta loku ngemlomo-kufundza kwehlukanisa
imisindvo yelulwimi lehlukene (lwati lwemisindvo). Umfundzi kumele abuke imisindvo kanye nabonkhamisa (sib.
‘b’, ‘o’, ‘n’, ‘a’ nobe ‘v’, ‘e’, ‘l’, ‘a’) bese uhlanganisa imisindvo nabonkhamisa kute akhe emagama (sib. ‘bona’, ‘vela’)
(imisindvo). Umfundzi kumele evisise emagama ( ngekuvisisa) abuye ahlangane nawo esikhatsini lesinyenti
labhaliwe akwati kutibonela wona (ngalokutentakalelako). Kwekugcina, umfundzi kumele akwati kufundza emagama
lasemishweni ngekusheshisa abe awevisisa (ngekushelela). Kepha, letincenye tekufundza atenteki ngetinyatselo
letilandzelanako. Kwenta sibonelo, bafundzi bafundza kubona nekuvisisa emagama laphelele labhalwe endzaweni
labahlala kuyo kanye Nekufundza Ngekuhlanganyela ngesikhatsi basebancane. Nanobe kunjalo, luhlelo lwemisindvo
loluhlelekile lubalulekile ekufundzeni kufundza ngelulwimi lwakho lwasekhaya, kuhambisana nekufundza, kubhala
kanye nekulalela tindzaba natifundvwa.

Bafundzi nabacala kufundza nekubhala ngelulwimi lwekwengeta, basuke sebati kutsi imisindvo ihlahlelwa njani
elulwimini lwabo lwasekhaya. Basuke sebevisisa imicondvo yalokubhaliwe futsi sebanelwati lwangaphambili
lwebudlelwane bemisindvo nekupelwa kwayo. Labakudzingako eklasini lemisindvo eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta kutetayeta kusebentisa lolwati kufundza kuhlahlela itheksthi lebhalwe ngeEnglish (sib. kuhlanganisa
imisindvo lebayatiko nabonkhamisa kute bakhe emagama). Bafundzi kumele futsi bati lapho budlelwane bemisindvo
nesipelingi behluka khona elulwimini lwabo lwasekhaya kanye naselulwimini lwesibili lwekwengeta.

Kubalulekile kutsi eBangeni 1, bafundzi batfutfukise sisekelo lesicinile elulwimini lwabo lwekwengeta. Nakungenjalo,
angeke bawevisise emagama labawahlahlelako eSiswatini eBangeni 2 kantsi nemsebenti labawenta emisindvweni
utawuvele nje ube ‘kumemeta nje emagama labhaliwe’. Bafundzi nabo batawuzuza ekufundzeni kubona imisindvo
yeSiswati (lwati lwemisindvo) eBangeni 1. Loku kwenteka kancono ngetingoma nemilolotelo letibasita kutsi
bahlukanise imisindvo (sib. lo lo lo lo).

Thishela kumele akhumbule kutsi indzima yakhe iphatselene nekutsi ente bafundzi bati ngebudlelwane emkhatsini
wemisindvo nekupelwa kwayo elulwimini lwekwengeta, kungabi kufundza ngekwenta ngalokungenamaphutsa.
Imisindvo kumele yenteke ngemisebenti lemifisha, levamile naleyenteka kuso sonkhe siGaba saboKhewane.

 16 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
Imisebenti yemisindvo leyentiwa emalanga onkhe/njalo ngeliviki

Kumele kucikelelwe kakhulu imisindvo kuso sonkhe siGaba saboKhewane. Luhlelo lunikiwe eLulwimini Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta eSitatimendeni seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola. EBangeni 1, kutawugcilwa ekutfutfukiseni
lwati lwemisindvo. EmaBangeni 2 ne 3, kunikwe luhlelo lwemisindvo lolutakwakhela etukwaloko bafundzi lasebavele
bakwentile elulwimini lwabo lwasekhaya. Njengobe kunelinanincane lesikhatsi sekufundzisa imisindvo, bothishela
bakhutsatwa kutsi bahlanganise kufundziswa kwemisindvo kanye nemisebenti yeKulalela, Kukhuluma kanye
neKufundza Ngekuhlanganyela.

2.8.6 Kubona emagama

Bafundzi bafundza kubona emagama labawabonako (kubona nobe kusho emagama) ngekutsi bawabone njalo.
Emagama lavela kanyenti kutheksthi nobe emagama labawasebentisa njalo angafundvwa ngayo lendlela. Bafundzi
nabachubeka nekufundza ngelulwimi lwabo lwekucala lwekwengeta, loko kutakwenta kutsi bakwati kutfola emagama
lamanyenti labawabonako nalawo labawasebentisa njalo.

2.8.7 Kuvisisa

Bafundzi bavame kukwati kuhlatiya emagama elulwimini lwabo lwekwengeta, kepha bangakuvisisi loko
labakufundzako. Loku kuholela kuloku bantfu labakubita ‘kumemeta nje emagama labhaliwe’.Sizatfu lesikhulu sekutsi
bafundzi bangakwati kuvisisa itheksthi kutsi emakhono abo elulwimi abutsakatsaka. Abanaso silulumagama neluhlelo
lwelulwimi lolwenele kute batewukwati kwakha umcondvo waloko labakufundzako. Ngako-ke, thishela kumele akhe
silulumagama neluhlelo lwabo lwelulwimi ngekutsi abatayete kusebentisa lulwimi lweSiswati ezingeni lelifanele.
Emasu lanjengekwakha ‘emagama lafakwa elubondzeni’ eklasini kanye nekukhutsata bafundzi kutsi batibekele
sichazamagama sabo (nobe tincwadzi tesilulumagama) nawo ayasita. Kwenta bafundzi bafundze kakhulu elulwimini
lwabo lwekwengeta kuyindlela lenhle letawutfutfukisa silulumagama sabo. Nanobe kunjalo, lelisu litawusebenta
ingce uma ematheksthi asezingeni lelifanele lekufundza ngekutimela.

Lenye indlela lebalulekile yekutfutfukisa kuvisisa kwebafundzi nabafundza kubuta imibuto letakwenta bafundzi
batibandzakanye netheksthi. Thishela kumele acale ngemibuto lelula sib. Ngubani …?’ (sib. Ngubani lodle liphalishi?)
‘Ini …?’ (sib. Udle ini Sipho?) kanye nekutsi ‘Kuphi …?’ (sib. Uye kuyewulala kuphi Sipho?) Kancane kancane,
njengobe bafundzi betayela indlela yekubuta babuye batfutfukise lulwimi lolufanele lwekuphendvula imibuto, imibuto
leminyenti lelikhuni ingabutwa. Ngesikhatsi bafundzi sebaseBangeni 3, kumele babe sebakwati kuphendvula imibuto
letsi ‘Kungani …?’ (sib. ‘Kungani Sipho angamange aye esikolweni?).

 Kute utfole lolunye lwati mayelana nekutsi ungakufundzisa njani Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela, Kufundza Basitwa
 Nguthishela, Kufundza Ngababili kanye Nekufundza Ngekutimela, Imisindvo, kubona emagama kanye nekuvisisa buka ku:
 Department of Basic Education’s handbook, ‘Teaching Reading in the Early Grades’ (2008), lengatfolakala ngekuphenya ku:
 www.education.gov.za

2.8.8 Kubhala

Bafundzi bafundza emakhono ekwakha tinhlavu kanye nekubhala ngesandla eLulwimini lwabo Lwasekhaya.
Bangasebentisa lolwati nasebacala kubhala ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta ethemini 3 yeliBanga 1. Imisebenti
yekubhala eBangeni 1 ilula kakhulu njengobe bafundzi kumele bagcile ekubhaleni ngeLulwimi lwabo Lwasekhaya.

EBangeni 2, kugcilwa kakhulu ekubhaleni ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta. Kwenta sibonelo, kubhala kudzinga
kutsi basitwe, bafundzi babhala basebentisa tinhlaka temisho njengekutsi ‘Ngitsandza _______. /Angitsandzi
______.’ EBangeni 3, kubhala kucala kuba lukhuni. Ngekusitwa nguthishela, bafundzi kulindzeleke kutsi babhale

CAPS 17
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
isethi lelula yeticondziso babuye balandzise ngabo. Ngekubambisana nathishela, bafundzi batawubhala kanye
nathishela (Kubhala Ngekuhlanganyela), babhala indzaba lelula.

Emakhono ekubhala lamanyenti asuselwa eLulwimini Lwasekhaya. Kwenta sibonelo, bafundzi bafundza kutsi
ibhalwa njani itheksthi njengekucala ngekulandzisa eLulwimini lwabo Lwasekhaya, kantsi batsatsela kulolwati,
bese ngekuhamba kwesikhatsi bafundza kubhala ngekutsi balandzise eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Ngalokunjalo, bafundzi bafundza kutsi lwentiwa njani luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala, kuhlela bunjalo bembhalo kanye
nekushicilela umbhalo wabo (luhlelo lwekubhala) eLulwimini lwabo Lwasekhaya, bese basebentisa lamakhono
nababhala eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.

2.8.9 Takhi telulwimi nekusetjentiswa

Lwati lolukahle lwesilulumagama kanye neluhlelo lwelulwimi lunika sisekelo sekutfutfukisa emakhono (kulalela,
kukhuluma nekubhala) eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta. EBangeni 1 silulumagama kanye neluhlelo lwelulwimi
kufundvwa ngalokutentakalelako ngekubatayeta lulwimi lolukhulunywako. EmaBangeni 2 ne 3, bafundzi batawuphindze
batfole silulumagama kanye neluhlelo lwelulwimi ngekutsi bafundze Siswati. EBangeni 3, kunemisebenti letsite
legcile ekusetjentisweni kweLulwimi.

Silulumagama lesibekiwe selibanga ngalinye kanye neluhla lwemagama lavame kusetjentiswa esiSwati lunikiwe
eSigabeni 3 kulomculu. Kubalulekile kutsi bafundzi bafinyelele kuloko lokubekiwe uma ngabe batawulindzeleka kutsi
basebentise iEnglish njengelulwimi Lwekufundza Nekufundzisa (LoLT) eBangeni 4. Bothishela badzinga emasu
ekutfutfukisa silulumagama sebafundzi, kwenta sibonelo:

• emagama labhalwa elubondzeni kanye nemalebuli eklasini

• imidlalo yesilulumagama (sib. kwakha emagama)

• kufundza ngekutimela

• kugcina sichazamagama sakhe (tincwadzi tesilulumagama)

• kusebentisa tichazamagama letinemifanekiso (letinelulwimi lunye naletinetilwimi letimbili)

Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela betfule babuye bakhe kabusha silulumagama, bente lemisebenti
lephawulwe ngentasi
Caphela: Tingcikitsi letiniketiwe / netihloko timane titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela batatikhetsela tingcikitsi tabo
labatitsandzako kuye ngesimongcondvo netinsita labanato.Kumele bente sicinisekiso sekutsi banato tincwadzi
letinkhulu/ emaphosta, imilolotelo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo mbamba letitawuhambelana nengcikitsi
lekhetsiwe.
Kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kube kanyentana uma kungenteka, kumele bahlole bafundzi ngekusebentisa
Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki loluphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Umsebenti wemalanga onkhe (1 li -awa ngeliviki)
Munye nobe minyenti yalemisebenti lelandzelako malanga onkhe:
• Ucala kutfutfukisa lwatimagama lwetemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) ngekusebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko letifana ne
 ‘Timphahla’
• Uphendvula tibingelelo letilula kanye nemicimbi yekuvalelisa, basebentisa emabintana, sib. ’Sawubona’ ‘Unjani’
 ‘Ngiyaphila’
• Wenta ticelo letilula, sibonelo, ‘ Ngicela kuya endlini lencane.?’
• Ulandzela ticondziso tathishela abe ukhomba tintfo letitfolakala eklasini nobe etitfombeni, ‘sibonelo. Ngikhombe litafula.
• Uniketa emagama etintfo letisesitfombeni nobe eklasini abe alandzela imibuto yathishela, sibonelo. ‘Yini loko? ‘Sigcoko’
• Uphendvula ngekwenta ticondziso letilula temlomo, sib. ‘Khumula ticatfulo takho.’
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula, sibonelo, ‘Yini loko lobhala ngako?’ Ipenseli.
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongcondvo, sib. Bunyenti bemabito labalekako, liswidi- emaswidi.
• Uhlabela tingoma letilula, anyakate ngekusitwa nguthishela, sibonelo ‘Luphi lwandle, luseThekwini, lwentani na? Lugubha
 emagagazi.
• Uhlanganyela etingomeni nemilolotelo, ngekwenta iminyakato, Sibonelo. Kushu, Kushu, Kushu, sasuka sitimela.
• Udlala umdlalo welulwimi, sib, Umdlalo wekushaya tandla bese uniketa emabito, emabito etindzawo, emabito ebantfu,
 emabito etitselo, emabito etimoto, emabito ebafana, njll.
Imisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma lekugcilwe kuyo (15 emaminithi x 2 ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona nobe letifundziwe
Thishela ufundza nobe acoce indzaba kabili ngeliviki. Tindzaba leticocwako tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa lulwimi
lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati. Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisukele encwadzini lenkhulu nobe emaphosta
lanemidvwebo lebonakala kahle kubo bonkhe bantfwana.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso lacocelwe wona nobe lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu nobe
iphosta lenemifanekiso, sib. Likati nenja, ngekutijabulisa babuye bangenelele emakhorasi ngesikhatsi lesifanele
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula, sibonelo, ‘Belihlala kuphi likati?’ Neticondziso, sibonelo. ‘Hlala esitulweni.’
• Uphendvula imibuto yathishela ngekuniketa letinye tetintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni, sibonelo, ’Bangaki bantfu
 kulesitfombe?’

 24 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi:
Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama, sib. Imibala: kuluhlata, kumhlophe,
 kubovu njll.
• Ucala kuvisisa nekuba nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula letikusimongcondvo selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako, sibonelo. Indlela lephocako-‘Khumula lihembe, ’ tabito tebuniyo-‘Sicatfulo sami’, bunyenti bemabito etintfo
 letibalekako, liswidi-emaswidi njll.
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki
Kulalela neKukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
• Uphendvula ngekwenta ticondziso temlomo letilula
• Ukhomba tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni aphendvula ticondziso tathishela
Liviki 6-10
• Uniketa letinye tetintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 1:
Kulalela neKukhuluma (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa kwaletinye tetisekelo tesilulumagama ngekukhomba tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni abe
 aphendvula ticondziso tathishela

CAPS 25
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 1
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 15 emaminithi ngeliviki
Imphimiso nelwati lwemisindvo (1-5 emaminithi umsebenti ngamunye)
Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe, ihlanganiswe nekuLalela neKukhuluma nobe imisebenti yekufundza ngekuhlanganyela.
• Uhlukanisa imisho yetemlomo ngemagama ngekushayela ligama ngalinye tandla, sib. imisho lesuselwa endzabeni. Make
 ufike itolo.
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela, tfola emagama lanemvumelwano etindzabeni, etingomeni, nasemilolotelweni, sibonelo, ’Khushu,
 Khushu, Khushu sasuka sitimela. Khushu, Khushu, Khushu sasuka sitimela
• Ucala kutfola imisindvo lesekucaleni kwemagama, (sib. ’likati’ ‘l’, make ‘m’)
Kucala kufundza nekubhala ( 5-10 emaminithi kanye nobe kabili ngethemu )
Thishela utawuletsa emaphosta, nalokunye ekilasini kute kube nesimo sembhalo wetindzawo letihlukene sembukiso
wasekilasini. Ngetikhatsi tekuvakasha kwesikolo, thishela utawukhombisa bafundzi umbhalo lobhalwe etindzaweni
lebakuto.
• Kubona emagama latayelekile endzaweni lesiphila kuyo. (sib. kungenwa lapha/kuphunywa lapha/ kuvuliwe)
• Utfutfukisa lwati lwekufundza nekubhala,( sib.lwati lwembhalo) ngemsebenti wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi nelinanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15 emaminithi
ngeliviki)
 Nangabe thishela asebentisa linanikhulu eLuLwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele etfule incwadzi lenkhulu
yekufundza lensha (nobe iphosta nalamanye ematheksthi lakhulisiwe) kulowo nalowomsebenti wemalanga onkhe.
Nangabe asebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, utawusebentisa itheksthi lefanako
letawutsatsa emaviki 2-3 bese benta lomsebenti wentiwe kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki.
Thishela utawufundzela liklasi itheksthi, akhombise emagama babuye bacocisane ngetitfombe nangendzaba.
Utawuphindza afundze lendzaba emkhatsini weliviki, agcugcutele bafundzi kutsi nabo bafundze. Itheksthi
isetjentiselwa kungenisa silulumagama lesisha.
• Ulalela indzaba nobe itheksthi lengemaciniso ngalesikhatsi alandzela thishela abuye abuke netitfombe.
• Ucoca ngetitfombe asebentise Lulwimi Lwasekhaya lapho kudzingeke khona.
• Ubona tintfo etitfombeni, sib. ‘Ngikhombise sihlahla lesikhulu’, Khombisa bantfwana labadlalako’.
• Uphendvula leminye imibuto lelula asitwa titfombe, sib. Sikuphi situlo samkhulu? ‘
• Ufundza silulumagama setemlomo, sib.sihlahla, lomdzala, indvodza, umoya, njll.
• Emva kwekufundza kanyentana, ungenelela kumakhorasi lapho kufanele khona.
• Ulingisa indzaba asebentisa letinye tetinkhulumomphendvulwano.
• Udvweba sitfombe akhombise umcondvo lobalulekile losendzabeni.
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Imphimiso nelwati lwemisindvo: (Temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ucala kutfola imisindvo leyehlukene ekucaleni kwemagama.
• Utfola emagama lanemvumelwane endzabeni, etingomeni nasemilolotelweni
Kufundza: (Temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Ubona bantfu, tilwane naletinye tintfo, emifanekisweni yencwadzi lenkhulu (nobe lamanye ematheksthi lakhulisiwe
 lanemifanekiso )
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula yetemlomo lephatselene netindzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso.
• Udvweba sitfombe lesikhombisa umcondvo lobalulekile endzabeni nobe etheksthini lengemaciniso.
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 1:
Kufundza (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula yetemlomo laphatselene netindzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa silulumagama lesisendzabeni ngekukhombisa tintfo letisetitfombeni babe balandzela ticondziso
 tathishela , sib. ’Ngikhombise sitja lesikhulu’’ lesimanti/lesomile/lesishile, njll.

CAPS 27
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/ NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1 - 5.
Bothishela bakhetsa tigcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama, bente lemisebenti lengentasi.
Chaphela kutsi tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela kumele bakhetse tihloko tabo letifanelekile kuye
ngengcikitsi netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza/ emaphosta, imilolotelo,
tingoma, imidlalo netintfo mbamba letimayelana nengcikitsi lekhetsiwe.
Bothishela kumele bayente yonkhe lemisebenti, kube kanyentana uma kungenteka. kumele bahlole bafundzi ngekusebentisa
Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki loluphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5
Liviki 6 - 10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (1 li-awa ngeliviki)
Munye nobe minyenti yalemisebenti lelandzelako ngeliviki:
• Ucala kutfola silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko letifana nalesi: ‘kudla’
• Uphendvula tibingelelo netivaleliso, asebentise imisho lemabintana, sib. Utiva njani? Ngiphilile Hamba kahle? Ngiyabonga.
• Wenta ticelo letilula, sib. Ngicela emanti.
• Ukhomba tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni aphendvula ticondziso tathishela, sib. Ngikhombe libhanana, lilamula, njll
• Uphendvula imibuto yathishela ngekuniketa letinye tetintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni, sib. ‘Bangaki bantfu kulesitfombe?’
• Uphendvula ngekwenta ticondziso letilula temlomo, sib. ‘Khumula lijezi.’
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula, ‘sib. ‘Mangaki emalamula?’ Matsatfu’
• Uvisisa abuye acale kusebentisa tinhlelo telulwimi letilula letisukela kungcikitsi, sib. Sikhatsi sanyalo lesilula: ‘Ngihamba
 naMuzi’, njll.
• Uhlabelela tingoma letilula bese wenta umnyakato, sib. ‘Ngitsandza kuhleka, ngihleke, ngihleke’, njll
• Uhlanganyela emilolotelweni nasetingomeni, kwentiwa iminyakato
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi, sib. Imidlalo yekucombela munye utsatsa likhadigama alifihle bese ubuta loku: ‘kubhalweni
 kulelikhadi?’ njll
Imisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma lekugcilwe kuyo (15 emaminithi x2 ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba lococelwe tona nobe letifundziwe
Thishela ufundza nobe acoce indzaba kabili ngeliviki. Tindzaba leticocwako tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa lulwimi
lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati. Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisukele encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe
emaphosta lanemifanekiso letawubonakala kahle kubo bonkhe bantfwana.
Ulalela ngekutijabulisa tindzaba letimfisha nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso leticociwe nobe letifundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu
nobe emaphosta lanemifanekiso babuye bangenelele ngesikhatsi lesifanele , sib. Tinyoni letisihlanu, tihlete’mtsini, njll.
Uvisisa abuye aphendvule ticondziso nemibuto lelula, sib. Ngikhombe titselo, Tingaki tibhidvo kulesitfombe?
• Uphendvula imibuto yathishela ngekuniketa letinye tetintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni, sib. Yini loku lokulesitfombe?’
Kutfutfukisa lwati, silulumagama netakhiwo telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama, sib. Kulandzelanisa tintfo –
 emalanga eliviki
• Ucala kuvisisa nekuba nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongcondvo selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako, sibonelo. Sikhatsi sanyalo lesimalula, sib. Ngiya edolobheni. Indlela lephikisako, sib. Ngidlala ibhola- Angidlali
 ibhola.

 28 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kulalela neKukhuluma: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
• Uuniketa letinye tetintfo letiphatselene nengcikitsi esitfombeni nobe letiseklasini
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula
Liviki 6-10
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula
• Utetfula ngetindlela letilula asebentise emabintana lamafisha nesilulumagama lesifundziswe ngesikhatsi sethemu , sib.
 ‘Ngiyalitsandza liphopho. Angiwatsandzi emabhanana.’
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 2:
Kulalela neKukhuluma: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa kwalesinye silulumagama setemlomo lesifundziswe ngesikhatsi sethemu ngekwenta ticondziso
 letifana naleti, Dvweba emabhanana lasihlanu.
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula lenetimphendvulo letimfisha, sib. Mangaki emabhanana lakhona?
• Utetfula ngetindlela letilula asebentise emabintana lamafisha, sib. ‘Angiwatsandzi emabhanana.’

CAPS 29
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKAMISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 15 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/ NEMAKHONO
Imphimiso nelwati lwemisindvo (1-5 emaminithi)
Lemisebenti kumele ifishaniswe ibuye ihlanganiswe nekulalela nekukhuluma nobe imisebenti yekufundza
ngekuhlanganyela.
• Ufundza lishadi labonkhamisa (sib. a, e, i, o, u)
• Utfola lwati lwemsindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngamunye nabonkhamisa (sib: umsindvo“l” nankhamisa “a”
 kwenta “la”, umsindvo“l” nankhamisa “e” kwenta “le”, umsindvo “n” nankhamisa “a’ kwenta “na”, umsindvo
 “n” nankhamisa “e” kwenta “ne”(sib. la, le, li, lo, lu, ) (ba, be, bi, bo, bu) (ma, me, mi, mo, mu) lala, babe, bami,
 malume, njll
• Utfola lwati lwemisindvo elwimini Lwekwengeta ngekusebentisa imilolotelo netingoma (sib: Lolo lololo bindza
 mntfwanamake, make uyolima sitawudla umbila)
• Ubona imisindvo yekucala emagameni latayelekile (sib: babe “b”, make”m”)
• Bashaya tandla bashayela emagama lahlahlelwe ngendlela letayelekile (sib. Si-tu-lo’)
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela utfola emagama lanemvumelwane etindzabeni, etingomeni nasemilolotelweni (sib. ‘Gege’
 ‘lagege’, ‘Gege lagege’ )
Kucala kufundza nekubhala (5-10 emaminithi kanye nobe kabili ngethemu )
Thishela uletsa tinsita, emaphosta, njll, eklasini kute kuvetwe simo sembhalo wetindzawo letihlukene sembukiso
waseklasini. Uma kuneluhambo lwekuvakasha lwesikolo, thishela utawukhombisa bafundzi umbhalo lobhalwe
etindzaweni lebakuto.
• Ubona emagama latayelekile endzaweni lesiphila kuyo, sib. Mani, Sikolo
• Utfutfukisa lwati lwekufundza nekubhala,( sib.lwati lwembhalo) ngemsebenti wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Linanincane lesikhatsi 25-30 ngeliviki, linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15 emaminithi
ngeliviki).
Lapho bothishela basebentisa linanikhulu eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, betfula incwadzi lenkhulu
lensha nobe lenye yeticashunwa letindze ngeliviki ibuye yentiwe lemisebenti malanga onkhe. Lapho bothishela
basebentisa linanikhulu eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, basebentisa libhuku linye esikhatsini lesifanako
lesingangemaviki lamabili nobe lamatsatfu bese lomsebenti wentiwa kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki. Bafundzela
liklasi indzaba, bakhombe emagama babuye bakhulume ngetitfombe nesimondzawo. Bothishela baphindze
bafundze lendzaba emkhatsini weliviki, bakhutsate bafundzi kutsi bahlanganyele. Itheksthi isetjentiselwa kwetfula
silulumagama lesisha.
• ulalela indzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso abe alandzela thishela abuke netitfombe.
• Ukhuluma ngesitfombe kusetjentiswa Lulwimi Lwasekhaya uma kunesidzingo.
• Ubona tintfo letisesitfombeni (sib. Ngikhombise sikhwama. Khomba esicatfulweni)
• Uphendvula leminye imibuto lelula ngekusitwa titfombe, sib. Likuphi likhekhe?
• Ufundza lesinye silulumagama setemlomo, sib. sikhwama, sicatfulo, likhekhe
• Ngemuva kwekufundza kanyentana, ungenelela kumakhorasi lapho kufanele khona
• Ulingisa indzaba usebentise ikhulumomphendvulwano
• Udvweba sitfombe lesiphatselene nalokushiwo ngulendzaba

• Ufundza lishadi labonkhamisa (sib. a, e, i, o, u)
• Utfola lwati lwemsindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngamunye nabonkhamisa (sib: umsindvo“l” nankhamisa “a”

 “n” nankhamisa “e” kwenta “ne”(sib. la, le, li, lo, lu, ) (ba, be, bi, bo, bu) (ma, me, mi, mo, mu) lala, babe, bami,
 malume, njll
• Ubona imisindvo yekucala emagameni latayelekile (sib: babe “b”, make”m”)
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela utfola emagama lanemvumelwane etindzabeni, etingomeni nasemilolotelweni (sib. ‘Gege’
 ‘lagege’)
Kufundza: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Ubona bantfu, tilwane netintfo kumifanekiso lesencwadzini lenkhulu
• Uphendvula imibuto lephatselene nendzaba
• Udvweba sitfombe lesiphatselene nalokushiwo ngulendzaba
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile
Kufundza (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Bashaya tandla bashayela emagama lahlahlelwe ngendlela letayelekile
• Uphendvula imibuto yetemlomo lephatselene nendzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso’
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa silulumagama lesitfolakala endzabeni ngekukhomba tintfo letisetifombeni abe aphendvula ticondziso
 tathishela, sib. “Ngikhombe umngani wakho.’

CAPS 31
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: kute
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 15 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Kubhala ngekuhlanganyela (Linanikhulu lesikhatsi – kanye ngeliviki)

• Kubhala imibhalotihumusho ngekusitwa nguthishela ngemdvwebo wakhe abuye afundze lokubhaliwe.

Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)

 32 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 3
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 Li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE /LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1 - 5
Bothishela bakhetsa tigcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama, bente lemisebenti lengentasi.
Chaphela kutsi tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela kumele bakhetse tihloko tabo letifanelekile kuye
ngengcikitsi netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza/ emaphosta, imilolotelo,
tingoma, imidlalo netintfo mbamba letimayelana nengcikitsi lekhetsiwe.
Bothishela kumele bayente yonkhe lemisebenti, kube kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
ngekusebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki loluphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (1 li-awa ngeliviki)
Lemisebenti lelandzelako ingentiwa malanga onkhe kuye ngesikhatsi lesitfolakalako:
• Ucala kutfutfukisa temlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) silulumagama basebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko letinjenge ‘Tilwane’
• Baphendvula tibingelelo letilula netivaleliso, basebentisa emabintana, sibonelo, Sala kahle.Sitawubonana kusasa’
• Kwenta ticelo letilula, sib. Ngicela ungiphe incwadzi yekufundza’
• Ukhombisa tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni, sib. Ngikhombe tinkhomo letidlako.’
• Basho tintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni nobe ekilasini, balandzele ticondziso temibuto yathishela.’sib. Yini lena? ‘yinkhomo’
• Uphendvula ngekwenta ticondziso temlomo , sib. ‘dvweba sitfombe sendlu’
• Uphendvula imibuto lebutwa nguthishela, sib. ‘Unjani umbala walelijezi? Uluhlata satjani.
• Uvisisa acale kusebentisa takhi letilula teLulwimi kusimongcondvo , sib. kucala kusebentisa tiphawulo, njengaleti: ‘Umfana
 lomkhulu’, Intfombatane lencane.
• Ubona umuntfu, tilwane nobe intfo ngalokuchazwe ngemlomo, sib’. Indishi lencane ‘ ‘Nginemilente lemibili’. ‘Ngikhala ngitsi
 ‘hhi hhi’’ Ngingubani mine’
• Uhlabela tingoma letilula ente neminyakato, sib. Babe Ndlovu uhlala epulazini.
• Uhlanganyela ngekwenta imilolotelo netingoma, ente iminyakato, sib. ‘Nginetandla letimbili’, ‘Ngineminwe lemihlanu.’
• ‘Udlala imidlalo’, sib. Imidlalo yekucombela, - umfundzi munye utawulingisa umsindvo wesilwane, labanye bafundzi
 bacombela ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kutsi ngusiphi silwane lesikhala ngalendlela.
Imisebenti yeKulalela neKukhuluma lokugcilwe kuyo (15 emaminithi kabili ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba laticocelwe naletifundziwe
Thishela utawufundza indzaba nobe acoce indzaba kabili ngeliviki.
Tindzaba lebacocelwe tona tingalingiswa kusetjentiswa lulwimi lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati
Tindzaba letifundvwako kumele titsatselwe encwadzini lenkhulu nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso kute bonkhe
bafundzi bakwati kubona letitfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso lacociwe nobe lafundziwe asuselwa encwadzini lenkhulu
kumbe iphosta lenemifanekiso, sib. Emagundvwane lamatsatfu, Dukwane lelibovana, ngekutitfokotisa babuye bangenelele
emakhorasi ngesikhatsi lesifanele.
• Uvisisa alandzele teluleko, sib. Nguyiphi inkhomo? Mingaki imilente yayo?
• Kuphendvula imibuto lelula lephatselene nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha.Tingaki tinkhomo endzabeni? Tintsatfu.
• Uphendvula imibuto yathishela ngekusho tintfo letisesitfombeni, sib. Shano tintfo letisesitfombeni.Yini lena? Inkhomo.
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati netakhi teLulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla batawuchubeka naloku:
• Wakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama sib.tinombolo nebukhulu, njll.
• Ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi teLulwimi letilula tesimongcondvo lesifanelekile elulwimini
 lolukhulunywako, sib. kusebentisa tiphawulo letifana na‘kukhulu, kuncane, sib.bondzaweni, emfuleni, ekhaya, sib.tandziso,
 emini, ebusuku njll.

 34 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 3
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYIWE
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 1 Li-Awa 15 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI /N EMAKHONO
Imphimiso nelwati lwemsindvo (1-5 emaminithi umsebenti ngamunye)
Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe ibuye ihlanganiswe nemisebanti yekuLalela neKukhuluma kanye nekufundza
ngekuhlanganyela.
• Ushaya tandla kukhombisa emalunga emagameni latayelekile (sib. um/si/la, si/hla/hla)
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela utfola emagama lanemvumelwano endzabeni, tingoma nemilolotelo. (sib. Emakholwa angena
 ahleka abanga umsindvo).
• Bacala kubona imisindvo leyehlukene lesekucaleni kweligama (sib. ’g’gadza’ ‘sitsebe’s‘)
• Uva emagama lasebunyentini (litafula- ematafula)
Kucala kufundza nekubhala (5 – emaminithi kanye ngethemu )
Thishela utawuletsa tinsita ekilasini, njengemaphosta, njll, kute kuvetwe simo sembhalo wetindzawo letihlukene
sembukiso waseklasini. Ngesikhatsi seluhambo lwekuvakasha kwesikolo, thishela utawukhombisa bafundzi
umbhalo lobhalwe etindzaweni lebakuto.
• Ubona emagama latayelekile endzaweni lesiphila kuyo sib.luphawu lwesitaladi, luphawu lwesitolo, emagama lakhangisako,
 sib. i-Toyota, i -OMO, i-Nandos, njll.
• Utfutfukisa lwati lwekufundza nekubhala,( sib. Lwati lwembhalo) ngemsebenti wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Linanincane: 25-30 emaminithi ngeliviki, linanikhulu 1 li - awa 15 emaminithi)
Nangabe thishela asebentisa linanikhulu lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta wetfula incwadzi lenkhulu
nobe letinye tinhlobo temidvwebo lekhulisiwe ematheksthini, evikini ngalinye, lomsebenti wentiwa malanga onkhe.
Lapho thishela asebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi eLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta usebentisa libhuku lelifanako
emavikini la -2 nobe la-3, lomsebenti wentiwa kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki. Thishela ufundzela likilasi itheksthi,
akhombise emagama babuye bacoce ngesitfombe nangesimondzawo sendzaba. Thishela uphindze afundze indzaba
emkhatsini weliviki, abuye agcugcutele bafundzi kutsi bafundze. Itheksthi isetjentiselwa kwetfula silulumagama
lesisha.
• Ulalela indzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso ngalesikhatsi balandzela thishela babuye babuke titfombe.
• Ucoca ngetitfombe basebentisa Lulwimi Lwasekhaya lapho kufanele khona
• Ubona tintfo esitfombeni, sib. ‘Khombisa logwaja’ Khombisa litje’.
• Baphendvula imibuto lelula ngekusitwa titfombe, sib. Ngabe logwaja unemadlebe lamadze nobe lamafisha?
• Ufundza ngesilulumagama setemlomo, sib, silwane, logwaja, umsila, tindlebe.
• Emva kwekufundza kanyentana, ungenelela kumakhorasi, lapho kufanele khona.
• Balingisa indzaba basebentisa letinye tetinkhulumomphendvulwano.
• Udvweba sitfombe lesikhombisa umcondvo lobalulekile wendzaba.
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Imphimiso nelwati lwemsindvo:(temlomo na / nobe kwenta)
• Ushaya tandla kukhombisa emalunga emagameni latayelekile, (sib. um-go-dzi’, ’ti-tfu-ku-tfu-ku’)
• Kuva emagama lasebunyentini, (sib. Umuti-imiti, sitja-titja).
• Bacala kubona imisindvo leyehlukene lesekucaleni kweligama.
• Kufundza: (temlomo na / nobe kwenta)
• Ubona labanye bantfu, tilwane netintfo emifanekisweni lesencwadzini lenkhulu nobe leminye imidvwebo lekhulisiwe
 ematheksthini.
• Kuphendvula imibuto lelula yetemlomo lephatselene nendzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso
• Kudvweba titfombe letikhombisa umcondvo lobalulekile yendzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso.

CAPS 35
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile Umsebenti 3:
Kufundza (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ushaya tandla kukhombisa emalunga emagameni latayelekile
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula yetemlomo lephatselene nendzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa silulumagama lesisendzabeni ngekukhombisa tintfo letisesitfombeni kube kulandzelwa ticondziso
 tathishela, sib. khomba logwaja, Tindlovu, njll.

Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (1 li-awa)
Leminyeti yalemisebenti kumele yentiwe kuye ngekuvuma kwesikhatsi/ sikhatsi lesiniketiwe:
• Ucala kutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) usebentisa tingcikitsi/tihloko letifana naleti: Tintfo
 lengingatenta.’
• Uphendvula tibingelelo netivaleliso, kusetjentiswa imisho lemabintana, sib. ‘Hamba kahle.’ Sitawubonana Ngiyabonga.
• Kwenta ticelo letilula, sib. ‘Ngicela ungiphe lisobho.’
• Ukhomba tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni aphendvula ticondziso tathishela, sib. ‘Ngikhombe sitfombe lesineluswane.’
• Uphendvula imibuto yathishela ngekuniketa letinye tetintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni, sib. ‘Bentani labantfu kulesitfombe.’
• Uphendvula ngekwenta ticondziso letilula temlomo, sib. Dvweba litafula.’
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula lebutwa nguthishela, sib. ‘Uhlala nabani ekhaya?’, Unjani umbala welihembe lakho.’
• Uvisisa abuye acale kusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongcondvo, sib. Tindlela tesento: ‘Ngihamba naMuzi’, njll.
• Ubona umuntfu, silwane nobe intfo ngalokuchazwe ngemlomo lokulula, sib. Nginemacembe, neticu, ngiyini?
• Uhlabelela tingoma letilula ente nemnyakato, sib. ‘Sitinyoni, siyasuka siyahlala’, njll.
• Uhlanganyela kumilolotelo netingoma, kwentiwe iminyakato.
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi, sib. Imidlalo yekushiyelana umntfwana munye ugijima ayotsintsa lomunye, lotawugijima kakhulu
 utawufika avale lesikhala. Losele utawuchubeka atsintse lomunye, njll.
Imisebenti yeKulalela neKukhuluma lokugcilile(15 emaminithi x2 ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba lococelwe tona nobe letifundziwe
Thishela ufundza nobe acoce indzaba kabili ngeliviki. Tindzaba leticocwako tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa lulwimi
lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati. Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisukele encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe
kumaphosta lanemidvwebo lebonakala kahle kubo bonkhe bantfwana.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso lacociwe nobe letifundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza
nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo ngekutijabulisa babuye bangenelele kumakhorasi lapho kufanele khona sib. Sitinyoni tsine,
njll.
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule ticondziso.
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula lecondzile lemayelana nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha, sib. Nje umuntfu uyahleka?
 Uhleka nakwentenjani?
• Uniketa tintfo letisesitfombeni uphendvula imibuto yathishela, sib, Ngubani lomile kulesitfombe?
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
Ngebamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, kufake ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama, sib. Intfo longakhona kuyenta: Mine
 ngingagijima.
• Ucala kuvisisa nekuba nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongcondvo selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako, sibonelo. ‘Tindlela tesento’, ’Tiphawulo’, ‘Indlela lephikisako’

KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE

LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imphimiso nelwati Lwemsindvo (1-5 emaminithi umsebenti ngamunye)
Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe ibuye ihlanganiswe nemisebanti yekuLalela neKukhuluma kanye nekufundza
ngekuhlanganyela.
• Ushaya tandla kukhombisa emalunga emagameni latayelekile (sib. um-dzi-bi ; um-tse-le-la)
• Ngelusito lwathishela utfola emagama lanemvumelwano endzabeni, tingoma nemilolotelo (sib. Naba bomake batfwele
 imitfwalo, Naba bomake batfwele imitfwalo)
• Bacala kubona imisindvo leyehlukene lesekucaleni kweligama, (sib. Sihlahla, Likhuba )
• Ubona bunyenti emagameni ( sib. Litafula – ematafula, indlu - tindlu )
Kucala kufundza nekubhala (5 – emaminithi kanye ngethemu )
Thishela utawuletsa tinsita eklasini, njengemaphosta, njll kute kuvetwe simo sembhalo wetindzawo letihlukene
sembukiso waseklasini. Ngesikhatsi seluhambo lwekuvakasha kwesikolo, thishela utawukhombisa bafundzi
umbhalo lobhalwe etindzaweni lebakuto.
• Ubona emagama latayelekile endzaweni lesiphila kuyo sib.luphawu lwesitaladi, luphawu lwesitolo, emagama lakhangisako,
 sib. i-Toyota, i -OMO, i-Nandos, iVodacom njll.
• Kutfutfukisa kucala kufundza nekubhala (sib. lwati lwembhalo) ngemsebenti wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela.
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Linanincane lesikhatsi: 25-30 emaminithi ngeliviki, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi)
Nangabe thishela asebentisa linanikhulu lesikhatsi eLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta wetfula incwadzi lenkhulu
lensha nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo, evikini ngalinye, lomsebenti wentiwa malanga onkhe. Lapho thishela
asebentisa Linanincane lesikhatsi eLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta usebentisa libhuku lelifanako emavikini la-2
nobe la-3, lomsebenti wentiwa kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki. Thishela ufundzela likilasi itheksthi, akhombise emagama
babuye bacocisane ngesitfombe nangesimondzawo sendzaba. Thishela uphindze afundze indzaba emkhatsini
weliviki, abuye agcugcutele bafundzi kutsi bafundze. Itheksthi isetjentiselwa kwetfula silulumagama lesisha.
• Ulalela indzaba nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso ngalesikhatsi balandzela thishela babuye babuke titfombe.
• Ucoca ngetitfombe basebentisa Lulwimi Lwasekhaya lapho kufanele khona
• Ubona tintfo esitfombeni, sib. ‘Khombisa logwaja’ ‘Khombisa litje’.
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula ngekusitwa titfombe, sib. Ngabe logwaja unemadlebe lamadze nobe lamafisha?
• Ufundza silulumagama setemlomo, sib. sihlahla, lomdzala, indvodza, umoya, njll.
• Emva kwekufundza kanyentana, batawungenelela kumakhorasi lapho kudzingeke khona, sib. Nabaya bomake, batfwel’
 imitfwalo!
• Ulingisa indzaba basebentisa letinye tetinkhulumomphendvulwano.
• Udvweba sitfombe akhombise umcondvo lobalulekile wendzaba.
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Imphimiso nelwati lwemsindvo (1-5 emaminithi umsebenti ngamunye)
• Ushaya tandla kukhombisa emalunga emagameni latayelekile’’, sib. um-gwa-ja’, ’ti-tfu-ku-tfu-ku’
• Ubona bunyenti emagameni, sib. i-mi-fu-la, ti-ntsa-ba.
• Kufundza: (temlomo na / nobe kwenta)
• Ubona labanye bantfu, tilwane netintfo emifanekisweni lesencwadzini lenkhulu nobe emaphosta
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula yetemlomo lephatselene nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha.
• Kudvweba titfombe letikhombisa umcondvo lobalulekile wendzaba nobe itheksthi lengemaciniso
Tiphakamiso temsebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile Umsebenti we-4:
Kufundza (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ubona bunyenti emagameni, sib (sib. Liso - emehlo, inkhomo-tinkhomo)
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula lecondziswe ngco endzabeni ngetimphendvulo letimfisha.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa silulumagama lesisendzabeni ngekukhombisa tintfo letisesitfombeni kube kulandzelwa ticondziso
 tathishela, sib. khomba libhubesi, tintsaba, njll.

 ITHEMU 4
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: Alikho
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 15 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI / EMAKHONO
Kubhala ngekushiyelana (Linanincane lesikhatsi –15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Kucala kubhala eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kumele kusekelwe nguthishela
• Ngkusitwa nguthishela bhala umbhalosihumusho kumdvwebo wakho ubuye ufundza loko lokubhaliwe.
• Ngelusito lwathishela bhala luhla lolulula ngaletihloko, sib. Tintfo letiphaphako: tinyoni, lilulwane, Inyosi, njll.
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kubhala: (kubhala)
· Tsatsela umbhalosihumusho wesitfombe lowusidvwebile ubuye ufundze lokubhaliwe.
· Bhala luhla lolulula lolunesihloko.

CAPS 41
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
EMATHEKSTHI LAPHAKANYISIWE / TINSITA TEMNYAKA

KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA
• Emabhuku etindzaba (Incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza) netindzaba temlomo.
• Tincwadzi letinkhulu tetindzaba letingemaciniso.
• Emaphosta netitfombe kwesekela kufundziswa kwetindzaba nesilulumagama.
• Emashadi emibala nemashadi etinombolo.
• Tingoma, imilolotelo yeminyakato netinkondlo.
• Tintfo letihambelana nengcikitsi nobe tihloko.
• Tinsita tekulingisa indzaba sib.imaskhi, timphahla tekugcoka netintfo.
• Ema-CD, ema-DVD netinhlelo tamabona khashane.
• Iphaphethi yekungenisa Lulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO
• Titfombe nemaphosta
• Emalogo netitfo letihambelana neTendzawo, sib. tikhwama tekuphatsa, emagama emabrendi nakupakwa, njll.
• Tincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza (lokungenani tindzaba letimbili nobe letingemaciniso tematheksthi ngethemu )
• Lamanye ematheksthi lakhulusiwe, sib.tingoma, imilolotelo, njll.
• Emalebuli emafleshikhadi eliklasi nembukiso.
• Indvuku yekukhomba ematheksthi lakhulisiwe, sib.tindzaba taselubondzeni, imibukiso, njll.

KUBHALA NENDLELA YEKUBHALA NGESANDLA
• Tinsita tekubhala (sib. ipeniseli,emapeniseli emibala, emakhilayoni emafutsa,emaphepha langakabhalwa lanebukhulu
 lobehlukene (A3, A4, A5), emapheshana ekubhalela)
• Emaphepha emashadi nemakoki)
