 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 LIBANGA 2 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA
 TIDZINGO TETHEMU
 ITHEMU 1
KULALELA Nekukhuluma SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela kutsi betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama, bente lemisebenti
lengentasi. Caphela kutsi tingcikitsi letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela kumele bakhetse tingcikitsi tabo
letifanelekile kuye ngengcikitsi netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letidzingekile/ nemaphosta
lamakhulu, imilolotelo, tingoma, imidlalo netintfo mbamba letimayelana nalesihloko lesikhetsiwe.
Bothishela kumele bazame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebanikwe sona. Kumele betfule sifundvo lesisha,
kulalelwe lokukhulunywako, babuye balandzise. Thishela kumele akukhombise loku ngekutsi acale alandzise ngekwakhe
(lakwentile ngempelasontfo).Kumele abasekele bafundzi nabalandzisa ngekwabo. Kumele anikete bantfwana lokumele
bakulandzele nabakha emagama, sib, Ngemphelasontfo ngi……., nga……., ngase….., ngemuva kwaloko…….. Kumele futsi
abasite bakwati kusebentisa sikhatsi lesengcile lesilula. Bafundzi batatetayeta kulalela nekulandzisa umnyaka wonkhe. Loku
kutabanika litfuba lekusebentisa sikhatsi lesengcile nekuhlangana kwetikhatsi (sib. Kwekucala, lokulandzelako, lokutako)
Thishela kumele ahlole bafundzi ngemisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki lephakanyisiwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu..
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Lemisebenti yehluka ngebudze bayo. Thishela kumele ente munye umsebenti lomudze nobe lemibili nobe lemitsatfu
letimifisha kanye ngeliviki.
• Uchubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko letifana
 nalesi: imiva
• Ulandzela ticondziso letimfisha letilandzelanako, sib. ‘Dvweba buso lobujabulile, Nyalo dvweba buso lobujabulile.’
• Unika ticondziso letilula, sib. ‘Dvweba sitfombe semndeni wakho.’
• Uvisisa aphendvule imibuto lelula lefana nalena lelandzelako: ‘Nguyiphi…? Labani...? (Nguyiphi ingubo lenkhulu kuleti?
 Labani lelitafula?)
• Kwenta ticelo netitatimende letilula, sib. ‘Ngilambile. Ngingakutfola kudla?’
• Utfola intfo enchazelweni lelula lechazwe ngemlomo, ( sib. Intfombatane ikhubekile, Iyakhala. Bantfwama kumele
 bamatanise lokukhulunywe ngako nesitfombe.
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni aphendvule ticondziso tathishela. Sib. ‘Nginikete tintfo lotibona kulesitfombe’
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula, sib. Thishela usho lakwente ngemphelasontfo.
• Ubamba ngenhloko abuye ahaye inkondlo lelula, imilolotelo leneminyakato netingoma, sib. Katane, katane, uyaphi na?
 Katane, ngiya edolobheni, njll
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi, kucombela kutsi lomunye umuntfu uva njani ngekubuka simo lakhuluma akuso
 nangekusebentisa titfo temtimba.
Imisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma lokugcilwe kuyo (linanincane lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 1 ngeliviki, Bukhulu
besikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 2 ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba lococelwe tona nobe letifundziwe (kufundza ngekuhlanganyela)

CAPS 43
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
Thishela ufundza nobe acoce indzaba kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki ngesikhatsi lesiniketiwe. Tindzaba leticocwako
tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa lulwimi lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati. Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisukele
encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo lebonakala kahle kubo bonkhe bafundzi.
• Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, kulandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso nobe lokufundvwe etincwadzini
 letinkhulu tekufundza nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo.
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule ticondziso, sib, ‘Ngikhombise make loya edolobheni’
• Uphendvula imibuto lecondzile lelula lephatselene nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha. ‘Uhlala kuphi? Ekhaya.
• Uniketa tintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni aphendvula imibuto yathishela, sib. Yini loku? Inhlanti.
• Ulingisa lokushiwo yindzaba, asebentisa inkhulumomphendvulwano.

Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
• Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Kuchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo kanye nesilulumagama lesisengcondvweni, sib. ‘Kwetfula imiva.’
• Kucala kuvisisa nekuba nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongocndvo selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako, sibonelo. ‘Kuvisisa nekucala kusebentisa sikhatsi lesendlulile, ‘Ngihleli-ngahlala’
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki lephakanyisiwe
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
• Ukhona kutfola sitfombe salokukhulunywa ngako lokulula, sib. Umfana ujabulile, uyamoyitela
• Ulalela loko lokukhulunywako lokulula abuye aphendvule imibuto mayelana nako, sib. ‘Ucala wenteni ekuseni?’,
 ‘ulandzelise ngani?’ ‘Ngemuva kwaloko?’
Liviki 6-10
• Ulandzela ticondziso letilandzelanako, sib. Sukuma. Nyalo hleka, Kwekugcina, bamba inhloko.’
• Uniketa kulandzisa lokulula, sib. Ngelisontfo, Ngivuke nga-8 ekuseni. Ngageza. Ngemuva kwaloko, ngagcoka timphahla,
 njll.
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile loluphakanyisiwe:
Kulalela nekukhuluma (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ulalela tintfo taloko lokulula lakushoko abuye aphendvule imibuto mayelana nako, sib. ‘Wentani logogo?’, ‘Walandzelisa
 ngani?’, njll.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa sisekelo sesilulumagama setemlomo ngekukhomba bantfu labasesitfombeni nobe kwenta iminyakato
 aphendvula ticondziso tathishela, sib. Tentise kwangatsi ujabulile, utfukutsele, njll. Khomba sitfombe semuntfu lojabulile,
 njll.

 44 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 1
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Lwati lwemisindvo nemisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Bothishela babuyeketa imisindvo yabongwaca lehamba ngamunye nalefundvwe eLuLwimini Lwasekhaya.
Bachubeka betfule kupelwa kwemagama. Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-10) kumele iletse kuhlangana
kwemisebenti yeKulalela, Kukhuluma neKufundza.
• Utfola budlelwane bemisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngamunye (sib. b, c, d, f, g, h, j, k, l, m, n, p, s, t, v, w, y, z)
• Utfola imisindvo lesekucaleni kwemagama, (sib. p, s, h, l, pela, simo, hamba, lima )
• Uhlahlela emagama abe ngemalunga (sib. Sa-ndla, i-ndle-la)
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Landzela sikhatsi lesifakwe ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kufaka ekhatsi umsebenti weKulalela nekukhuluma, kubuye kufake kukhuluma ngoba
bafundzi nathishela bakhuluma ngaletheksthi. Ebangeni 2, kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kuyachubeka kodvwa
kuba yincenye yeKulalela nekukhuluma. Kufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela (Linanincane lesikhatsi 30
emaminithi nelinanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15 emaminithi ngeliviki. Bafundzi bakufundzile kufundza ngekuholwa
nguthishela ebangeni 1 eLulwimini Lwasekhaya, kumele bayitayele lendlelanchubo.
Kufundza ngemacembu ngekusitwa nguthishela
Bothishela kumele betfule kufundza ngemacembu ngekusitwa nguthishela kulethemu. Bothishela labasebentisa
Linanikhulu lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kumele bahlukanise liklasi ngemacembu lasihlanu kuye
ngelizinga labakulo bafundzi asebente nelicembu linye ngelilanga emaminithi la-15. Ngesikhatsi thishela asebenta
nalelicembu, lalamabanye batawufundza emacenjini abo nobe bafundze ngababilli, basebentisa ematheksthi lalula
letayelekile nobe imisebenti lemayelana naletheksthi. Letincwadzi tekufundza kumele tibe lula tibe nekuphindzeka
kwetakhi, silulumagama netitfombe kusekela letheksthi.
Bothishela labasebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta luniketwe emaminithi lange-30
kumele bente imisebenti yekufundza kweliklasi lokuholwa nguthishela kabili ngeliviki.
• Ufundza incwadzi yakhe yekufundza ngekuphimisela abe afundza ngekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela.
 Licembu lifundza indzaba nobe itheksthi lenemaciniso lefanako bafundze nathishela.
• Usebentisa tindlela tekufundza letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute kuvakale kahle abuye atinake kutsi ufundza
 njani, sib, kuphinyiswa kwemisindvo, njll)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekutetfula.
• Ukhomba kuvisisa kwekusetjentiswa kwetimphawu tekufundza nakufundvwa ngekuphimisela.
• Ucala kwakha silulumagama lasisusela ekufundzeni ngekusitwa, ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza ngekutimela.
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi sebafundzi sekutikhululekela nobe emakhaya)

Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe bakwati kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta ngesikhatsi
sabo lesikhululekile eklasini (sib. Uma basheshe bacedza umsebenti lebaniketwe wona), emakhaya (sib. Umsebenti
wasekhaya). Kubalulekile kutsi sonkhe sikhatsi lesivelako eklasini sisetjentiswe kutfutfukisa kufundza.
• Ufundza lokubhalwe nguye nalokubhalwe ngulabanye
• Ufundza ngekutimela tincwadzi latifundze ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngemacembu nemibhalotihumusho lelula
 nemabhukutitfombe eLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta latfolakala ekhoneni lekufundza eklasini.
• Usebentisa tichazamaga letinetitfombe tebantfwana (telulwimi lunye nobe tilimi letimbili)

CAPS 45
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki
Imisindvo (temlomo na/ na kwenta)
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile:
Imisindvo: (temlomo na/ na kwenta na/ nobe lokubhaliwe)
• Utfola budlelwane bemisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngamunye sib. b, c, d, f, g, h, j, k, l, m, n, p, s, t, v, w, y, z
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe
• Utfola imisindvo lesekucaleni kwemagama, ( sib. p, s, h, l, pela, simo, hamba, lima )
Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki:
Kufundza: (temlomo na/ na kwenta)
• Ufundza ngekushelela ngalokutfutfukile nangekutetfula.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa kuhlonishwa kwetimphawu tekufundza uma afundza ngekuphimisela
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile: 1
Kufundza (temloma na/ na kwenta)
Ufundza umbhalo lomfisha (1-2 imisho ngelikhasi) nathishela, balandzela indvuku yathishela yekukhomba.

CAPS 47
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KULALELA Nekukhuluma (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 Li-Awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE / LWATI / NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tigcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama, bente lemisebenti lengentasi.
Chaphela kutsi tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela kumele bakhetse tihloko tabo letifanelekile kuye
ngengcikitsi netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza/ emaphosta, imilolotelo,
tingoma, imidlalo netintfo mbamba letimayelana nengcikitsi lekhetsiwe.
Bothishela kumele bayente yonkhe lemisebenti, kube kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
ngekusebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 1-5
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emamini ngeLiviki)
Lemisebenti lelandzelako ayilingani. Thishela angenta munye umsebenti lomudze nalemibili kuye kulemitsatfu imisebenti
ngeliviki.
• Ucala kutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentise tingcikitsi nobe tihloko njenge ‘Simo
 selitulu’.
• Ulandzela ticondziso letimfisha letilandzelanako sib. sukuma, Hamba uye emnyango bese uyawuvula
• Uniketa ticondziso letilula, sib. ‘Vula umnyango’, ‘vala umnyango’
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula njenga: ngukuphi…? Ngubani...? (Ngubani lovule umnyango?
• Wenta ticelo letilula netitatimende, sib. ‘Kuyabandza’Ngicela kugcoka lijezi’
• Utfola intfo enchazelweni lelula lechazwe ngemlomo, sib. ‘kunemafu’
• Bafundzi batawumatanisa inchazelo kanye nesitfombe lesifanele.
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni kulandzelwe ticondziso tathishela , sib. ‘kukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni
 kulandzele tiphakamiso tathishela, sib. Ubonani esitfombeni ? Ngitjele kutsi yini longayibona esitfombeni’
• Ulalela lacocelwe kona, sib.thishela ukhuluma ngeluhambo lwekuya edamini lelakhelwe kugeza ngelilanga lelishisako.
• Ngelusito lwathishela, chaza ngetibonelo letilula mayelana neluhambo lwekuya edamini lekuhlamba.
• Faka engcondvweni bese ulingisa inkondlo lelula, wente imilolotelo yeminyakato netingoma, sib. Ngiyasha, Ngiyalalela,
 Ngiyashaya.
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi .sib. Sitinyoni tsine sitinyoni Siyasuka siyahlala sitinyoni /basebentise imidlalo lefaka timphahla
 letigcokwa nalishisa / nakubandza. Licembu lelinemagama lamanyenti ngilo leliphumelelako.
Imisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma lokugcilwe kuyo (Linanincane lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 1 ngeliviki ; Linanikhulu
lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 2 ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba lococelwe tona nobe letifundziwe (kufundza ngekuhlanganyela)
Kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki, Kuye ngebukhona besikhatsi, thishela ukhuluma nobe afundze indzaba (nobe kusho
tigameko ngekulandzelana kwato) Tindzaba leticociwe tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa timphawu temtimba netinsita
kutfutfukisa kuvusisa.
Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisuselwe encwadzini lenkhulu nobe emaphosta latawubonwa bafundzi bonkhe.

 48 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

• Ulalela indzaba lemfisha, kulandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso lacociwe nalefundziwe nobe lafundvwe
 encwadzini lenkhulu nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso.sib. Lilanga lematfonsi lamakhulu emvula, ngekujabula babuye
 bangenelele kumakhorasi ngesikhatsi lesifanele.
• Uvisisa nekulandzela ticondziso, sib. Ngikhombise emafu, Ngikhombise sibhakabhaka. Bala ematfonsi emvula lawele
 ngakuwe.
• Uphendvula imibuto lecondze ngco ngendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha, sib. Lidvume nini litulu?
• Shano tintfo letisesitjentiswe esitfombeni, uphendvule imibuto yathishela, sib. Yini lena? Ngematfonsi emvula.
• Lingisa indzaba usebentise inkhulumomphendvulwano.
• Ngelusito lwathishela, phindza usho indzaba.
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka siulumagama sasengcondvweni, sib. Simo selitulu
• Ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa nelikhono lekwati takhiwo telulwimi etingcikitsini letinenshokutsi telulwimi lolukhulunywa,
 (sib. Kuvisisa nekucala kusebentisa sento ‘kutawenteka’ kutawubandza, tiphawulo, sib. kunemoya, liyana, liyashisa,
 kufutfumele, kuyashisa)
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki:
Kulalela nekukhuluma (Temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
· Utfola sitfombe enchazelweni lelula lechazwe ngemlomo
· Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula ubuye uphendvule imibuto ngako, sib. Wentani Muzi? Yini latoyenta emva kwaloko?
Liviki 6-10
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula, sib. Sinjani simo selitulu namuhla?
• Landzisa ngemlomo ngendlela lelula, Ngelisontfo ngivuke ngelihora 8. Ngageza, Ngagcoka, Ngaya esontfweni.
Tiphakamiso Temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile:
Kulalela nekukhuluma (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ulalela lokulula lakucocelwe bese uphendvula imibuto lelula ngako, Uvuke ngasikhatsini Sipho ? Besinjani simo selitulu?
 Yini layente kucala? Yini lekayente ekugcineni?
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa tisekelo tesilulumagama setemlomo ngekukhombisa tintfo letiseklasini nobe titfombe nobe kwenta
 ngekulandzela ticondziso tathishela, sib. Ngikhombise sambulela.Unjani umbala waso? njll.

CAPS 49
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: emaminithi 45
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 Li-Awa 30 emaminithi
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo nelwati lwemisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-10 emaminithi) ihlelelwe liviki lonkhe. Ingabuye ibe nekuhlangana nemisebenti
yeKulalela, kukhuluma neKufundza.
• Ubona budlelwano bemisindvo lehamba ngamunye (b, k, l, m, t , p, s, z, c, n, d, f, g )
• Ubona imisindvo lesekucaleni kwemagama (sib. musa, liso, make)
• Wakha abuye uhlahlele emagama lalula (sib. li-so, liso; ma-ke, make; ba-be, babe, njll)
• Ubona imisindvo lenabongwac alabahamba ngababili lababili (sib. bh, hl, ns; bhala, hleka, insimbi)
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lehamba ngamibili (sib. ibhasi, hlala, insipho, njll)
• Ubona avisise imisindvo yabongwaca lehamba ngamitsatfu (sib. nhl, ndl, ngc njll)
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lehamba ngamitsatfu (sib. inhloko indlu njll)
• Wakha imisho ngemagama lakawafundzile (sib. Inhloko ibuhlungu. Indlu iyasha, njll)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Landzela sikhatsi lesifakwe tukwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kufaka ekhatsi umsebenti wekulalela nekukhuluma, kubuye kufake kukhuluma ngoba
bafundzi nathishela bacoca ngalombhalo. Ebangeni 2, kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kuyachubeka kodvwa kuba
yincenye yekulalela nekukhuluma.
Kufundza ngemacembu ngekusitwa (Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi nelinanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Bothishela labasebentisa Linanikhulu lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kumele bahlukanise liklasi
ngemacembu lasi-5 kuye ngelizinga labakulo bafundzi asebente nelicembu linye ngelilanga emaminithi langu-15.
Ngesikhatsi thishela asebenta nalelicembu, lalabanye batawufundza emacenjini abo nobe bafundze ngababilli,
basebentisa imibhalo lelula letayelekile nobe imisebenti lemayelana naletheksthi. Letincwadzi tekufundza kumele
tibe malula tibe nekuphindzeka kwetakhiwo, silulumagama netitfombe kusekela itheksthi.
Bothishela labasebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta luniketwe emaminithi lange-30
kumele bente imisebenti yekufundza kweliklasi lokuholwa nguthishela kabili ngeliviki.
• Ufundza incwadzi yakhe yekufundza ngekuphimisela abe afundza ngekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela.
 Lokusho kutsi licembu lifundza indzaba nobe itheksthi lenemaciniso lefanako bafundze nathishela.
• Usebentisa tindlela tekufundza letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute kuvakale kahle abuye atinake kutsi ufundza
 njani, sib, kuphinyiswa kwemisindvo, njll)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekutetfula.
• Ukhomba kuvisisa kwekusetjentiswa kwetimphawu tekufundza ngekuphimisela.
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama lasisusela ekufundzeni ngekusitwa, ngekuhlanganyela nangamunye.
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi sebafundzi sekutikhululekela nobe emakhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe kutsi bafundze ngekufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini Lekucala Lwekwengetwa
ngesikhatsi sabo lesikhululekile eklasini (sib. Uma basheshe bacedza umsebenti lebaniketwe wona), emakhaya (sib.
Umsebenti wasekhaya). Kubalulekile kutsi sonkhe sikhatsi lesivelako eklasini sisetjentiswe kutfutfukisa kufundza.
• Ufundza lokubhalwe nguye nalokubhalwe ngulabanye
• Ufundza ngekutimela tincwadzi latifundze ngesikhatsi sekufundza basitwa nguthishela nemibhalotitfombe lelungele Lulwimi
 Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letitfolakala ekhoneni lekufundza eklasini.
• Usebentisa tichazamaga letinetitfombe tebafundzi (telulwimi lunye nobe tilimi letimbili) kutfola inchazelo yemagama
 langawati.

 50 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 2
Imisindvo: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta na/ nobe lokubhalwako)
• Ubona budlelwano bemisindvo lehamba ngamunye (b, k, l, m, t , p, s, z, c, n, d, f, g )
• Ubona imisindvo lesekucaleni kwemagama (sib. musa, liso, make)
• Wakha abuye uhlahlele emagama lalula (sib. li-so, liso; ma-ke, make; ba-be, babe, njll)
• Ubona imisindvo lenabongwaca labahamba ngababili lababili (sib. bh, hl, ns; bhala, hleka, insimbi)
Kufundza (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Ufundza incwadzi yakhe yekufundza ngekuphimisela abe afundza ngekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela.
 Licembu lifundza indzaba yinye/lefanako
• Ukhomba kuvisisa indzaba lemfisha, (sib. Titfombe letinemcondvo lolandzelanako, kumatanisa imibhalotihumusho/ imisho
 netitfombe)
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 4:
Imisindvo: (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta na/ nobe lokubhalwako)
• Wakha emagama asebentise imisindvo lebayifundzile (sib. ibhasi, hlala, indvuku, insipho njll)
Kufundza (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
Ufundza imibhalo lesencwadzini Lenkhulu nathishela abuye aphendvule imibuto lemifisha yetemlomo lemayelana
nalendzaba.

CAPS 51
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 2
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHANYISIWE
 30 emaminithi ngelivi
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala (ka-2 nobe ka-3 ngeliviki)
Thishela kumele ente loku lokulandzelako ngesikhatsi lesibekiwe:
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala lafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya.
• Ukhetsa atsatsele imibhalotihumusho lehambelana nesitfombe
• Ngekusitwa ubhala umbhalotihumusho wesitfombe.
• Ucedzela imisho ngekugcwalisa tikhala.
• Ubhala imisho ngekusebentisa imisindvo nemagama lavamile lafundzisiwe
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (sib. Bofeleba nabongci)
• Utakhela silulumagama nesichazamagama
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile:
Kubhala: (Lokubhalwako)
Liviki 1-5
• Ucedzela imisho lemitsatfu ngekugcwalisa emagama lashodako
Liviki 6 -10
• Ubhala imisho ngekusebentisa imisindvo nemagama lavamile lafundzisiwe
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile:
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho ngekusebentisa imisindvo nemagama lavamile lafundzisiwe
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya

 52 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 3

KULALELA Nekukhuluma (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tigcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama, bente lemisebenti lengentasi.
Chaphela kutsi tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje. Bothishela kumele bakhetse tihloko tabo letifanelekile kuye
ngengcikitsi netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza/ emaphosta, imilolotelo,
tingoma, imidlalo netintfo mbamba letimayelana nengcikitsi lekhetsiwe.
Bothishela kumele bayente yonkhe lemisebenti, kube kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
ngekusebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 1-5
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela bakwati kusebentela kuloko labakwenta emavikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tisite bothishela kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Kumele tisite bothishela bakwati kuvuselela sisekelo setakhi telulwimi uma acocisana nalabanye bafundzi ngeLuwimi
Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Imisebenti ihlukana ngebudze bayo. Thishela angenta umsebenti munye lomudze nobe mibili kumbe mitsatfu lemifisha kanye
ngeliviki.
• Uchubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko letifana
 nalesi: ‘Temidlalo’
• Ulandzela kulandzelana lokufisha kweticondziso, sib., ‘Tsatsa ishoki ubhale ligama lakho ebhodini. Nyalo tsatsa idasta
 usule ibhodi.
• Uniketa ticondziso letilula, sib. ‘Khahlela ibhola.’
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula njengalena lelandzelako: Nguyiphi...? Yabani...? (Nguwuphi umdlalo
 lowutsandzako)
• Wenta ticelo netitatimende letilula, sib. ‘ngingaya kuyodlala ngephandle’
• Ukhona kutfola intfo lechazwa/leshiwo yinkhulumo, Intfombatane ikhubekile, Iyakhala. Bantfwama kumele bamatanise
 lokukhulunywe ngako nesitfombe.
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni aphendvule ticondziso tathishela, sib. ‘Ubonani kulesitfombe?
• Ulalela tintfo letilula letikhulunywako, sib. Thishela ukhuluma ngemdlalo webhola
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela uniketa kulandzisa lokulula, sib. Ucoca ngemdlalo lawubukele.
• Ubamba ngenhloko abuye ahaye inkondlo lelula, imilolotelo leneminyakato netingoma, sib. Sitimela, shuku, shuku, shuku,
 sahamba sitimela, njll.
• Kudlala imidlalo yelulwimi ngaphandle kweliklasi, sib. Pule, pule, hha, inja lencane, ayinamazeze, lenkhulu ke, inemazeze,
 njll.
Imisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma lokugcilwe kuyo (linanincane lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 1x ngeliviki, Linanikhulu
lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi x 2 ngeliviki)
Kulalela tindzaba leticociwe naletifundziwe (Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ufundza nobe acoce indzaba kanye nobe kabili ngeliviki ngesikhatsi lesiniketiwe. Tindzaba leticocwako
tingalingiswa ngekusebentisa lulwimi lwemtimba netinsita kwenanela lwati. Tindzaba letifundziwe kumele tisukele
encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo lebonakala kahle kubo bonkhe bafundzi.
• Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, lokucocwako nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso nobe lokufundvwe etincwadzini letinkhulu
 tekufundza nobe emaphosta lanemidvwebo, (Umdlalo welibhola letinyawo) ngekutijabulisa nekungenelela emakhorasi
 lapho kufanele khona.
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule ticondziso, sib. ‘Bala badlali labasenkhundleni.’
• Uphendvula imibuto lecondzile lelula lemayelana nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha, sib. ‘Ngubani loshaye ligoli.’
• Uniketa tintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni aphendvule imibuto yathishela, sib. ‘Yini loku?’ Yibhola.
• Uphendvula imibuto lecondzile lelula lemayelana nendzaba ngetimphendvulo letimfisha, sib. ‘Ngubani loshaye ligoli.’

CAPS 53
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
• Uniketa tintfo letitfolakala esitfombeni aphendvule imibuto yathishela, sib. ‘Yini loku?’ Yibhola.
• Uma kuyindzaba ulingisa lokushiwo yindzaba, asebentisa inkhulumomphendvulwano.
• Ngelusito lwathishela,uphindze acoce indzaba lacocelwe yona nobe afinyete umbhalo
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza kulemisebenti lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo kanye nelwati lwesilulumagama, sib. Tinkhombandlela-sandla sekudla/
 sandla sesancele.
• Ucala kuvisisa nekuba nelwati lwekusebentisa takhi telulwimi letilula kusimongcondvo selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako, sib. Sikhatsi sanyalo lesichubekako: ‘Udlala ibhola,’ ‘tiphawulo, ’ ‘lomkhulu’, ‘lomncane’
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki:
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula lenemcondvo lemayelana nendzaba/netheksthi
• Ukhuluma indzaba/ufinyeta itheksithi ngekuyikhuluma (ngemisho lemitsatfu nobe lemine)
• Liviki 6-10
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula, sib. Nguluphi luhlobo lwemdlalo lolutsandza kakhulu?
• Ukhona kutfola titfombe leti-3 kulokuchazwe ngemlomo, sib. Lendvodza idlala libhola letinyawo/Lendvodza idlala ithenisi.
 Lendvodza idlala ikhilikithi.
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 3:
Kulalela nekukhuluma (temlomo na / nobe kwenta)
• Ulalela lokukhulunywako lokulula nekuphendvula imibuto lelula ngako, sib. Nguliphi licembu lelifake ligoli kucala? Nguliphi
 licembu lelifake ligoli kwesibili? Nguliphi licembu leliwinile?
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa sisekelo sesilulumagama setemlomo ngekukhomba bantfu labasesitfombeni nobe kwenta iminyakato
 aphendvula ticondziso tathishela, sib. Ngikhombise lijezi lekhilikithi, Ngikhombise libhola letinyawo, njll.
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile:
Kulalela nekukhuluma (temlomo na / nobe kwenta)
• Usho lokukhulunyiwe lokulula, sib. Kusho lokwenteke emdlalweni welibhola letinyawo.
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa sisekelo sesilulumagama setemlomo ngekukhomba bantfu labasesitfombeni nobe kwenta iminyakato
 aphendvula ticondziso tathishela, sib. ‘Ngikhombe umceceshi walelicembu.’ ‘Ngikhombe umdlali lovimba emapalini.’ Njll.

 54 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 3
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKAMISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 Li-Awa 30 emaminithi
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo nelwati lwemisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Bothishela bachubeka nekwakha emagama lamafisha latayelekile. Kumele bazame kusebentisa emagama
labawatiko bantfwana bese bawasebentisa emishweni lelula. Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-10 emaminithi)
ihlelelwe liviki lonkhe. Ingabuye ibe nekuhlangana nemisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma.
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lalula lanemisindvo lehamba ngamunye (sib. Be-ka, su-sa, la-mu-la, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamibili (sib. Bha-le-la, sho-sho-lo-za, she-le-la. kho-
 khel-a, li-phe-pha, li-dze-dze, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamitsatfu sib. I-ntse-tse, ndzi-ndza, i-ndzi-ma, njll)
• Wakha imisho ngemisindvo layifundzile
• Kuhlela emagama latayelekile abe ngemagama lahlobene (sib. uhambile, ulambile, njll).
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela (Landzela sikhatsi lesifakwe ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kufaka ekhatsi umsebenti wekulalela nekukhuluma, kubuye kufake kukhuluma ngoba
bafundzi nathishela bacoca ngalombhalo. Ebangeni 2, kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kuyachubeka kodvwa kuba
yincenye yekulalela nekukhuluma.
Kufundza ngemacembu ngekusitwa (Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi nelinanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Bothishela labasebentisa Linanikhulu lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kumele bahlukanise liklasi
ngemacembu la-5 kuye ngelizinga labakulo bafundzi asebente nelicembu linye ngelilanga emaminithi la-15.
Ngesikhatsi thishela asebenta nalelicembu, lalabanye batawufundza emacenjini abo nobe bafundze ngababili,
basebentisa imibhalo lelula letayelekile nobe imisebenti lemayelana nalomibhalo. Letincwadzi tekufundza kumele
tibe malula tibe nekuphindzeka kwetakhiwo, silulumagama netitfombe kusekela letheksthi
Bothishela labasebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi eLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta luniketwe emaminithi langu-30
kumele bente imisebenti yekufundza kweliklasi lokuholwa nguthishela kabili ngeliviki.
• Ufundza incwadzi yakhe yekufundza ngekuphimisela abe afundza ngekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela.
 Lokusho kutsi licembu lifundza indzaba nobe itheksthi lenemaciniso lefanako bafundze nathishela.
• Usebentisa tindlela tekufundza letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute kuvakale kahle abuye atinake kutsi ufundza
 njani, sib, kuphinyiswa kwemisindvo, njll)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekutetfula.
• Ukhomba kuvisisa kwekusetjentiswa kwetimphawu tekufundza nakufundvwa ngekuphimisela.
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama lasisusela ekufundzeni ngekusitwa, ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza ngekutimela.
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi sebafundzi sekutikhululekela nobe emakhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe bakwati kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini Lekucala Lwekwengetwa ngesikhatsi
sabo lesikhululekile eklasini (sib. Uma basheshe bacedza umsebenti lebaniketwe wona), emakhaya (sib. Umsebenti
wasekhaya). Kubalulekile kutsi sonkhe sikhatsi lesivelako eklasini sisetjentiswe kutfutfukisa kufundza.
• Ufundza lokubhalwe nguye nalokubhalwe ngulabanye
• Ufundza ngekutimela tincwadzi latifundze ngesikhatsi sekufundza basitwa nguthishela nemibhalotitfombe lelungele Lulwimi
 Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letitfolakala ekhoneni lekufundza eklasini.
• Usebentisa tichazamaga letinetitfombe tebafundzi (telulwimi lunye nobe tilimi letimbili) kutfola incazelo yemagama
 langawati.

• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lalula lanemisindvo lehamba ngamunye (sib. Be-ka, su-sa, la-mu-la, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamibili (sib. Bha-le-la, sho-sho-lo-za, she-le-la. kho-
 khel-a, li-phe-pha, li-dze-dze, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamitsatfu sib. I-ntse-tse, ndzi-ndza, i-ndzi-ma, njll)
• Kuhlela emagama latayelekile abe ngemagama lahlobene (sib. uhambile, ulambile, njll).

• Ufundza incwadzi yakhe yekufundza ngekuphimisela abe afundza ngekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela.
 Licembu lifundza indzaba yinye/lefanako.
• Ukhomba kuvisisa indzaba lemfisha, sib. Titfombe letinemcondvo lelandzelanako, kumatanisa imibhalotihumusho/ imisho
 netitfombe

 khel-a, li-phe-pha, li-dze-dze, njll)

Kufundza (temlomo na/ nobe kwenta)
• Ufundza imibhalo lemifisha nathishela abuye aphendvule imibuto lemifisha yetemlomo lemayelana nalendzaba.

 56 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 3
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHANYISIWE
 30 emaminithi ngelivi
LOKUCUKETFWE/LWATI/NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala (ka-2 nobe ka-3 ngeliviki)
Thishela kumele ante loku lokulandzelako ngesikhatsi lesibekiwe:
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala lafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya.
• Ubhala imibhalotihukusho lehambelana netitfombe
• Ucedzela imisho ngekugcwalisa tikhala.
• Ubhala imisho ngekusebentisa imisindvo nemagama lavamile lafundzisiwe.
• Ubhala umusho longakapheleli asebentisa luhlaka, sib. Ngitsandza____________. Angitsandzi_________.
• Ubhala emagama latayelekile nemisho ngesibitelo.
• Ubhala imibhalo lelula, lemifisha lefundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya, umlayeto losekhadini lokufisela kululama.
• Usebentisa emabito(sib. Muzi, Thembi, sihlahla, umfula) netabito (sib. yena, sona, wona, tona, bona, njll)
• Upela kahle emagama lasuselwa engcondvweni, uhambile, dlala, imoto nobe asebentise lwati lwemisindvo, sib. Tindzebe,
 imphumulo, njll
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebafundzi uma kunesidzingo.
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala letifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (sib. Bofeleba nabongci)
• Utakhela silulumagama nesichazamagama
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungahleleki we- 3:
KUBHALA: (LOKUBHALWAKO)
• Ubhala umbhalotihumusho ahumushe sitfombe, sib. Inkhomo idla tjani
• Ubhala imisho lengakapheleli asebentise luhlaka, sib. Ngitsandza______. Angitsandzi________
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 4:
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho ngesibitelo, afake timphawu tekubhala
• Ubhala abuye afake nemidvwebo ekhadini lekufisela kululama.

CAPS 57
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 4
KULALELA Nekukhuluma (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
Lokucuketfwe/lwati/nemakhono
Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitakwenta kutsi bakwati kungenisa kanye nekujikeletisa silulumagama babuye
bente imisebenti lebalwe lapha ngentasi. Yati kutsi letingcikitsi/tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje kuphela.
Bothishela kumele batikhetsele tabo tingcikitsi letifanele kuye ngesimo kanye netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise
kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza/emaphosta, tinkondlo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo letiphatsekako letifanele
tengcikitsi lebayikhetsile. Kumele betame kwenta yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kwenteka.
Thishela kumele ahlole bafundzi asebentise imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letinsha letitakwenta bakwati kwakhela kuloko labakwente livikini ekucala lasihlanu.
Tingcikitsi kumele tibente bakwati kungenisa silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama lesifundzisiwe.
Tingcikitsi titakwenta thishela akwati kuvuselela kalula, takhi telulwimi kanye nesilulumagama ngesikhatsi achumana
nebafundzi ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta.
Bothishela kumele bente yonkhe imisebenti, kanyentana uma kungenteka. Kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa
imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lephakanyiselwe evikini 6 -10. Bothishela kumele babhalise bafundzi Imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Imisebenti iyashiyena ngebudze. Thishela kumele ente munye umsebenti lomudze nobe lemibili kumbe lemitsatfu lemifisha
ngeliviki.
• Uyachubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko
 letinjengaleti ‘Sikhatsi semnyaka’
• Ulandzela ticondziso letimfisha (sib. ‘Wota utohlala embi kweliklasi’)
• Unika ticondziso letilula (sib. ‘Shaya tandla.’)
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula (sib. ‘Nguyiphi …?’ ‘Yabani …?’ ‘Kuphi’? (Ngusiphi sikhatsi semnyaka lositsandza
 kakhulu? Lihlobo)
• Ucela abuye ente titatimende letilula (sib. ‘Ngingatipaka yini tincwadzi tami?’)
• Ubona intfo lechazwe ngemlomo ngalokulula (sib.‘Lihlobo. Lilanga liyashisa. Timbali tichakatile. Bafundzi bagcoke tikhindzi
 nemasandali.’ Bafundzi kumele bacondzanise lenchazelo nesitfombe lesifanele)
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela ticondziso latinikwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Yini loyibona esitfombeni? Ngitjele
 ngaloko lokubona esitfombeni.’)
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula (sib. thishela acoca ngaloko lakwente ngesikhatsi atsetse luhambo lwekuyewutsenga)
• Asitwa nguthishela, wenta kulandzisa lokulula (sib. bafundzi batjela thishela ngaloko labakwentile)
• Ubamba ngenhloko aphindze ahaye tinkondlo letilula, imilolotelo lenemnyakato kanye netingoma (sib. Lilanga lasehlobo
 liyashisa)
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi (sib. Ngibona ngemehlo ami umsindvo locala nga …..
Imisebenti lekugcilwe kuyo yekulalela nekukhuluma (linanincane lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi ngeliviki, linanikhulu
lesikhatsi 15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona naletifundziwe (Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ukhuluma nobe afundze indzaba kabili ngeliviki (kulandzisa ngetehlakalo) kuye ngesikhatsi lesikhona.
Tindzaba leticocwako tingentiwa samdlalo kusetjentiswa kunyakata kwemtimba kanye netintfo letesekela inchazelo.
Tindzaba tifundvwa encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso lapho bonkhe bafundzi
bakwati kubona khona titfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, ematheksthi lalandzisa ngaye nobe lacuketse emaciniso lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu
yekufundza (sib. busika) ngenhloso yekutitfokotisa abuye angenelele kumakhorasi ngesikhatsi lesifanele
Uvisisa abuye aphendvule ticondziso (sib. ‘Ngikhombise tihlahla. Ingabe tinemacembe? Ngusiphi sikhatsi semnyaka?’)
Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lemayelana netheksthi lenetimphendvulo letimfisha (sib. Kwentekani etihlahleni ebusika?’)

 58 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

Usho letinye tetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela imibuto lebutwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Kuyini loku? ‘Sitfwatfwa’)
• Uma kuyindzaba lingisa lokushiwo yindzaba usebentise inkhulumomphendvulwano
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela, uphindze acoce indzaba nobe anike sifinyeto setheksthi lelula
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama kanye netakhi telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza emisebentini lengenhla:
• uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulu magama (sib. libhayisikili-tikhatsi
 temnyaka)
• ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa abuye abe nelikhono lekusebentisa takhi selulwimi lesilula esimeni selulwimi lolukhulunywako
 loluvakalako (sib. uvisisa abuye acale kusebentisa sento: kusehlobo, siphawulo, kuyabandza, sandziso: lokutsambile)
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki:
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lemayelana nendzaba/netheksthi
• Uphindze acoce indzaba/ente tifinyeto tetheksthi ngemlomo (imisho lemitsatfu nobe lemine)
Liviki 6-10
• Uphendvula imibuto lelula (sib. Ngusiphi sikhatsi semnyaka lositsandzako?’)
• Ubona titfombe letintsatfu kuleto letichazwe ngemlomo (sib. ‘Lihlobo, Lilanga liyashisa, Busika, Akunamacembe
 etihlahleni./Yintfwasahlobo. Inyoni yakha sidleke sayo.’
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile loluphakanyisiwe 5:
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula (sib. Emaholide ami asehlobo, abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula lemayelana nelihlobo
 lenjengaleti: Thandi uye kuphi ngamaholide? Wentani nakafika lapho? Wentani emva kwaloko? njll)
• Wenta kulandzisa lokulula asebentisa luhlaka emishweni lemitsatfu nobe lemine
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa silulumagama setemlomo ngekutsi akhombe tintfo letiseklasini nobe esitfombeni lapho aphendvula
 imibuto/ticondziso letivela kuthishela (sib. ‘Ngikhombise irakhethi yethenisi/ibhethi/libhola, njll)
• Uvisisa lokungenani emagama la-500 uma kusetjentiswe linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
 nekufinyelela emagameni layi-1, 000 uma kusetjentiswe linanikhulu lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
 (sebentisa luhla lwemagama eSigabeni se-3 kute likusite)

CAPS 59
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 4
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 45 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
Lokucuketfwe/lwati/nemakhono
Lwati lwemisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Thishela ubuyeketa imisindvo lefundvwe ethemini yesitsatfu. Nakangenisa ligama lelisha, ucinisekisa kutsi bafundzi
bayayivisisa inchazelo yalo babuye balisebentise emshweni. Lemisebenti kumele ibe ifinyetwe (5-10 emaminithi)
yentiwe liviki lonkhe. Lemisebenti ingaphindze ihlanganiswe nemisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma kanye
neKufundza.
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemsindvo lohamba ngamunye (sib. su-sa, la-mu-la, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamibili (sib. kho-khel-a, li-phe-pha, li-dze-dze, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamitstfu (sib. ndzi-ndza, i-ndzi-ma, njll)
• Ukhona kubona emagama lanetijobelelo. (sib. sandlana, indlwana, njll)
• Kuhlela emagama latayelekile abe ngemagama lahlobene (sib. uhlobile, ulambile, njll).
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela (kwabelwe sikhatsi ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kungumsebenti wekufundza kanye nekulalela; ubuye uhlanganise nekukhuluma ngobe
bafundzi bakhuluma nathishela wabo ngetheksthi. EBangeni 2, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kutawuchubeka,
kodvwa kutawuchubeka njangencenye yekulalela nekukhuluma.
Kufundza Ngemacembu basitwa Nguthishela (linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi, linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Bothishela labasebentisa linanikhulu lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta kumele bahlukanise liklasi
labo ngemacembu ekufundza lasihlanu alabanemakhono lafanako basebente nelicembu linye emalanga onkhe
lokutsatsa emaminithi la-15. Ngesikhatsi thishela asebenta nalelicembu lalamanye emacembu atawufundza
ngekuhlanganyela nobe bafundze ngababili basebentisa ematheksthi lalula nalatayelekile nobe bente imisebenti
lehambisana netheksthi lefundvwako. Tincwadzi kumele kube nguletilula tibe netakhi lesiphindzaphindvwako kanye
nesilulumagama netitfombe tekwesekela itheksthi.
Bothishela labasebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta lapho kunesikhatsi
lesingemaminithi lange-30 kuphela lesikhona, kumele bente umsebenti wekufundza kwebafundzi bonkhe kabili
ngeliviki.
• Ufundza nathishela ngekuphimisela encwadzini yakhe ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela. Loku kusho kutsi
 lonkhe licembu lifundza nathishela indzaba lefanako nobe itheksthi lenemaciniso
• Usebentisa emasu lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute akhe umcondvo abuye aticaphele nakafundza (imisindvo,
 tinkhombandlela tesimongcondvo, kuhlatiya takhi, emagama lavamile)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekuphimisa lokuya ngekukhula
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa timphawu tekufundza nakafundza ngekuphimisa
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngekusitwa, ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza
 ngekutimela
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi bafundzi labakhululeke ngaso esikolweni kanye nasekhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe bakwati kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
nabanesikhatsi eklasini, kwenta sibonelo, nasebacedze umsebenti ngaphambi kwesikhatsi kanye nasekhaya
nabenta umsebenti wasekhaya. Kubalulekile kutsi kusetjentiswe sonkhe sikhatsi eklasini kute kutfutfukiswe
kufundza kwabo.
• Ufundza umbhalo wakhe kanye newalabanye
• Ufundza tincwadzi ngekutimela ngetikhatsi tekufundza basitwa nguthishela kanye nemibhalotihumusho kanye
 netindzabatitfombe ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letisekhoneni lekufundza eklasini
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebafundzi lesinetitfombe (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili) kute atfole inchazelo
 yemagama langawati

 60 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lokuhlelekile:
Imisindvo: (temlomo, kwenta na/nobe lokubhalwako)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemsindvo lohamba ngamunye (sib. su-sa, la-mu-la, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamibili (sib. kho-khel-a, li-phe-pha, li-dze-dze, njll)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamitsatfu (sib. ndzi-ndza, i-ndzi-ma, njll)
• Ukhona kubona emagama lanetijobelelo. (sib. sandlana, indlwana, njll)
• Kuhlela emagama latayelekile abe ngemagama lahlobene (sib. uhlobile, ulambile, njll).
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki:
Kufundza: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Uphindze acoce incenye yendzaba ngekusitwa nguthishela (imisho lemibili kuya kulemitsatfu)
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile lophakanyisiwe 5:
Imisindvo: (temlomo, kwenta na/nobe lokubhalwako)
• Wakha abuye ahlahlele emagama lanemisindvo lehamba ngamitsatfu (sib. ndzi-ndza, i-ndzi-ma, njll)
• Ukhona kubona emagama lanetijobelelo. (sib. sandlana, indlwana, njll)
• Kuhlela emagama latayelekile abe ngemagama lahlobene (sib. uhlobile, ulambile, njll).
Kufundza: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
• Wakha umcondvo wendzaba lebhaliwe lemfisha lenetitfombe (sib. ngekulandzelanisa titfombe nobe kucondzanisa
 umbhalotihumusho/imisho kanye nesitfombe
• Ufundza nathishela itheksthi lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe itheksthi lekhulusiwe), asebentisa sihloko kute
 acombele abuye aphendvule imibuto lemifisha neyemlomo lemayelana nendzaba
• Ufundza encwadzini yakhe ngekuphimisa ekufundzeni ngelicembu basitwa nguthishela. Licembu lonkhe lifundza indzaba
 lefanako

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 4
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 30 emaminithi
Lokucuketfwe/lwati/nemakhono
Imisebenti yekubhala (kabili nobe katsatfu ngeliviki)
Thishela kumele etame kwenta lokunyenti kwaloku lokulandzelako ngendlela langakhona ngayo:
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala ngesandla lawafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
• Ubhala imisho asebentisa emagama lacuketse imisindvo kanye nemagama latayelekile lasavele afundzisiwe
• Ulandzelanisa kahle imisho lehlangahlanganisiwe kute ente indzima bese utsatsela kuyo
• Ubhala indzima lokungenani lenemisho lemitsatfu ngesihloko lasetayele
• Uhlelembisa imininingwane lehleleke ngekwegrafu asebentisa emakhono lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (lishadi nobe
 umugcasikhatsi)
• Usebentisa emabito netabito (sib. umfana, libhola, wena, yena, bona, njll)
• Usebentisa sikhatsi sanyalo, sikhatsi sanyalo lesichubekako, lesengcile nakabhala
• Usebentisa bunyenti bemagama latayelekile nakabhala
• Upela ngendlela lefanele emagama lasenhloko yakhe (sib. uhambile, kudlala, imoti nobe asebentise lwati lwemisindvo;
 baba, mama)
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebafundzi nakunesidzingo
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala lesetivele tifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (bofeleba nabongci)
• Utakhela silulumagama nesichazamagama sakhe
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo lolungakahleleki loluphakanyisiwe:
Kubhala: (lokubhaliwe)
• Ubhala imisho lesihlanu labitelwe wona asebentise timphawu tekubhala letifanele
• Ulandzelanisa kahle imisho lehlangahlanganisiwe kute ente indzima bese utsatsela kuyo
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile loluphakanyisiwe 5:
Kubhala: (lokubhaliwe)
• Ubhala indzima lokungenani lenemisho lemitsatfu ngesihloko lasetayele
• Uhlelembisa imininingwane lehleleke ngekwegrafu (sib. lishadi nobe umugcasikhatsi

 62 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
EMATHEKSTHI LAPHAKANYISIWE / TINSITA TEMNYAKA

KULALELA Nekukhuluma
• Titfombe nemaphosta
• Tintfo letihambelana nengcikitsi netihloko, emaphaphethi, imask nalokunye
• Titfombe tekulandzelanisa
• Timphahla tekulingisa kanye nekulingisa indzaba
• Emadiski nobe emathephu lanetindzaba (letifundziwe nobe lebacocelwe tona), tinkondlo, imilolotelo kanye netingoma,
 sicophamavi, kanye nemathephu evidiyo
• Tincwadzi tetindzaba kanye netindzaba temlomo
• Tinkondlo, tingoma nemilolotelo
• Imidlalo yelulwimi (temlomo)
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO
• Titfombe nemaphosta
• Tincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza-letinemaciniso naletingenamaciniso
• Luhlelo lwekufundza loluphakanyisiwe (tindzaba leti 7-5)
• Lamanye ematheksthi lakhulisiwe (tinkondlo, tingoma, imilolotelo, njll)
• Tincwadzititfombe letinemibhalotihumusho (tincwadzi temibhalotihumusho)
• Tincwadzi letimfisha ‘letihlekisako’ letinemisho lemi 1-2 ekhasini kute tibekwe ekhoneni lekufundza
• Emafleshikhadi etintfo taseklasini, ukhombisa emagama lavamile
• Lishadi lema-alfabethi
• Indvuku yekukhomba ematheksthi lakhulisiwe (sib. tindzaba taselubondzeni, imibukiso, njll)
• Lamanye ematheksthi (sib. tingoma, imilolotelo, njll)
• Emalebuli emafleshikhadi elikilasi nemibukiso.
• Indvuku yekukhomba ematheksthi lakhulisiwe (sib. tindzaba taselubondzeni, imibukiso, njll)
• Tichazamagama tebafundzi letinetitfombe (letinelulwimi lunye netilwimi letimbili)
KUBHALA NEndlela yeKUBHALA NGESANDLA
• Tinsita tekubhala (sib. ipenseli, emapenseli emibala, emakhilayoni emafutsa, emaphepha langakabhalwa lanebukhulu
 lobehlukene (A3, A4, A5), irula, ijotha lengakabhalwa lutfo)
• Emaphepha emashadi nemakoki

CAPS 63
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 LIBANGA 3 SISWATI LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LOLWENGETIWE
 TIDZINGO TETHEMU NGAYINYE
 ITHEMU 1
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 li-awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1 - 5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela kutsi betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama bente imisebenti
lebalwe lapha ngentasi. Yati kutsi letingcikitsi/tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje kuphela. Bothishela kumele
batikhetsele tabo tingcikitsi kuye ngesimo kanye netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu
tekufundza/emaphosta, tinkondlo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo mbamba letifanele ingcikitsi labayikhetsile.
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6 - 10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letinsha letitabavumela kutsi bakwati kwakhela kuloko labakwente emavikini ekucala
lasihlanu. Letingcikitsi kumele tibavumele bakwati kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama
lesifundzisiwe. Letingcikitsi kumele tivumele thishela akwati kuvuselela takhi telulwimi letilula ngalesikhatsi achumana
nebafundzi ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentise Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 6-10. Kumele bachubeke bente imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Imisebenti iyashiyana ngebudze. Thishela kumele ente munye umsebenti lomudze nobe lemibili kumbe lemitsatfu lemifisha
ngeliviki.
• Uyachubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko
 letinjengaleti ‘Tilwane Tasendle’
• Ulandzela ticondziso (sib. ‘Khipha incwadzi yakho nemapenseli. Bhala lusuku etulu ekhoneni lelisesandleni sekudla
 ekhasini.’)
• Unika ticondziso letilula (sib. ‘Bamba sandla sami)’
• Uvisisa abuye ahendvule imibuto lelula (sib. ‘Nini …?’ ‘Kungani …?’ ‘Kuphi’? (Uyibone kuphi indlovu? Uyibone nini?
 Ehlatsini. Kulomnyaka lowengcile)
• Ucela kucaciselwa (sib. ‘Angivisisi. Ngicela uphindze usho.’)
• Uphendvula aphindze acele (sib. Ngingasisebentisa yini sichazamagama sakho, ngiyacela nebakitsi?’
• Ubamba lichaza etingcocweni letimfisha letimayelana nesihloko lesetayelekile (sib. simo selitulu, umndemi wami, njll)
• Ubona intfo letichazwe ngemlomo (sib. ‘Ngisilwane lesikhulu kakhulu. Ngimphunga ngelibala. Sikhumba ami siyahedletela.
 Nginemboko lomudze. Ngingubani mine?’
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela ticondziso latinikwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Yini loyibona esitfombeni? Ngitjele
 ngaloko lokubona esitfombeni).’
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula (sib. thishela acoca ngeluhambo lwekuya e-zoo)
• Asitwa nguthishela, wenta kulandzisa lokulula (sib. ucocela thishela ngaloko lakwentile)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise takhi telulwimi ngalokufanele (sib. sikhatsi lesengcile “Ngibone indlulamitsi.”)
• Ubeka enhloko aphindze ahaye tinkondlo letilula, imilolotelo lenemnyakato kanye netingoma (sib. Tihamba kancane
 kancane)
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi (sib. Cagela kutsi ngingubani mine …...
Imisebenti lekugcilwe kuyo yekulalela nekukhuluma (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona kanye naletifundziwe (Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ucoca nobe afundze indzaba kabili ngeliviki (kulandzisa ngetehlakalo). Tindzaba leticocwako tingentiwa
samdlalo kusetjentiswa kunyakata kwemtimba kanye netintfo letesekela inchazelo. Tindzaba tifundvwa encwadzini
lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso lapho bonkhe bantfwana bakwati kubona khona titfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, kulalandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso (sib. tindzaba letinemaciniso,
ticondziso, imibiko lecuketse lwati) lacocelwe yona nobe lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta
lanemifanekiso ngenhloso yekutitfokotisa
• Ulalela tindzaba (sib. Indlulamitsi yayitfola kanjani intsamo yayo lendze, kulandzisa ngemphilo abuye aphendvule imibuto
 lekhombisa kuvisisa njengekutsi ‘Yayitfola njani intsamo yayo lendze indlulamitsi?’)

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

• Ucombela lokutawulandzela endzabeni nobe alandzise ngaye (sib. Ucabanga kutsi kutakwentekani nyalo kundlulamitsi?)

 indzawo letihlala kuyo, njll)

 etihlahleni./Yintfwasahlobo. Inyoni yakha sidleke sayo
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 1:
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)

 yekuvisisa

CAPS 65
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 1
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Thishela ubuyeketa imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngababili nalabo labahamba ngabatsatfu, asebentisa
emagama bafundzi lasebavele bawati nobe emagama lasetjentiswa kakhulu. Nakangenisa ligama lelisha, ucinisekisa
kutsi bafundzi bayayivisisa inchazelo yalo babuye balisebentise emshweni. Wenta imisebenti yekuva ngetindlebe
ledzinga kutsi bafundzi bakhombe imisindvo yemagama labawatiko eLulwimini Lwekucala Lolwengetiwe.
Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-10 emaminithi) yentiwe liviki lonkhe. Lemisebenti ingaphindze ihlanganiswe
nemisebenti yekulalela, kukhuluma neKufundza.
Liviki 1 - 5
• Ukhomba budlelwane bemisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngamunye, abona umehluko lokhona ekuphimisweni
 kwemisindvo eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kanye naseLulwimini Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, sib. Sicoco - cat
• Ubona bongwaca labahamba ngababili abuye akhe emagama (sib. ‘sh’, ‘kh’, ‘ts’) (sib. sh-aya, kh-iya, ts-ela)
• Ubona emagama lanetijobelelo, sib. Malumekati, sitjana
• Ubona bongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. nhl, nts, ntj, ndl, njll)
• Wakha abuye aphimise emagama lamafisha lanabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib.
 intsaba, inhlanhla, indlondlo, njll)
Liviki 6 - 10
• Ubona bongwaca labahamba ngababili abuye akhe emagama (sib. ‘ph’, ‘ch’, ‘hl’; phela; chela; hlula)
• Wakha abuye aphimise emagama lamafisha lanabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib.
 inhlanhla, kuntjontja, intsambo njll)
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela (kwabelwe sikhatsi ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kungumsebenti wekufundza kanye nekulalela; ubuye uhlanganise nekukhuluma ngobe
bafundzi bakhuluma nathishela wabo ngetheksthi. EBangeni 3, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kutawuchubeka,
kodvwa kutawuchubeka njangencenye yekulalela nekukhuluma
• Ufundza nathishela itheksthi lebhaliwe lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe lenye itheksthi lekhulisiwe),
 asebentisa sihloko kucagela
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lemayelana nendzaba (sib. ‘Wenteni umfana esikolweni?’)
• Uchaza kutsi indzaba imente wativa njani, angagucukela kulolunye lulwimi nakunesidzingo
Kufundza Ngemacembu basitwa Nguthishela (Linanincane lesikhatsi 45 emaminithi, linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa
15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Uma bothishela basebentisa linanikhulu lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bahlukanise liklasi
labo ngemacembu ekufundza lasihlanu alabanemakhono lafanako. Kumele basebentise indlela yekufundza lehlelwe
ngetigaba bacinisekise kutsi licembu ngalinye lisebenta ngetheksthi lesezingeni labo lekufundza (kubona emagama
ngaphandle kwemaphutsa lokusemkhatsini wa 90%-95%). Thishela kumele asebente nelicembu linye lusuku
ngalinye lokutsatsa emaminithi la-15. Ngesikhatsi asebenta nelicembu, lalabanye bafundzi batawufundza ngababili
nobe bafundze ngekutimela nobe bente imisebente lephatselene netheksthi yekufundza.
Uma bothishela basebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bente umsebenti
wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela lowentiwa liklasi lonkhe katsatfu ngeliviki.
• Ufundza nathishela ngekuphimisela encwadziniyakhe ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela. Loku kusho kutsi
 lonkhe licembu lifundza nathishela indzaba lefanako nobe itheksthi lenemaciniso
• Usebentisa emasu lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute akhe umcondvo abuye aticaphele nakafundza (imisindvo,
 tinkhombandlela tesimongcondvo, kuhlatiya sakhiwo, emagama lavamile)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekuphimisa lokuya ngekukhula
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa timphawu tekufundza nakafundza ngekuphimisa
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngekusitwa, ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza
 ngekutimela
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi bafundzi labakhululeke ngaso esikolweni kanye nasekhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe bakwati kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
nabanesikhatsi eklasini, kwenta sibonelo, ngesikhatsi thishela asita licembu ngefundza, nobe uma bacedze
umsebenti ngaphambi kwesikhatsi. Kubalulekile kutsi litfuba lebalitfolako eklasini balisebentisele kutfutfukisa
kufundza kwabo. Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe futsi kufundza ekhaya njengemsebenti wasekhaya.

 nemibhalotihumusho kanye netindzabatitfombe ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letisekhoneni lekufundza eklasini

• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana lesinetitfombe (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili) kute atfole inchazelo
 yemagama langawati

Imisindvo: temlomo, kwenta na/nobe lokubhalwako)

Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela
• Ufundza indzaba lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe lenye itheksthi lekhulisiwe ) nathishela, asebentisa sihloko
 kucagela
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lemayelana nendzaba (sib. ‘Kwentekeni ngalesikhatsi indlulamitsi icala kunatsa emanti?’)
Kufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa Nguthishela
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa nekushelela nakafundza nathishela itheksthi lesezingeni lakhe
Kufundza Ngekutimela
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa nekushelela nakafundza ngekutimela
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 1:
Kufundza: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela
• Uphindze acoce indzaba nobe afinyete itheksthi lenemaciniso
Kufundza Ngemacembu Basitwa Nguthishela
• Ufundza tindzaba nathishela ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela ngalokusezingeni lakhe. Lonkhe licembu
 lifundza indzaba lefanako
• Usebentisa emagama lavamile, imisindvo kanye nemakhono ekuvisisa kute akhe inchazelo

 ITHEMU 1
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
KUSETJENTISWA KWELULWIMI Kubhala:
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki

LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi1 li-awa ngeliviki)
Munye nobe mibili imisebenti lapho kusetjentiswa khona Linanikhulu lesikhatsi kanye nemsebenti munye lapho
kusetjentiswa khona Linanincane lesikhatsi
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala ngesandla lawafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
• Ubhala tinhla letiya ngekuba lukhuni letinetihloko (sib. Tilwane Tasendle: tindlovu, tindlulamitsi, emabhubesi nemadvuba
• Ubhala itheksthi lelula (sib. likhadi lelusuku lwekutalwa)
• Ngekusitwa, ubhala lisethi lelilula leticondziso (sib. iresiphi)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lemine kuya kulesitfupha ngesihloko lasetayele
• Usebentisa luhlelo lwekubhala (kubhala luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala, kuhlela bunjalo bemibhalo nekushicilela)
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala lesetivele tifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (bongci, bokhefana, bomabuta, tibabato
 nabokhulumile)
• Upela emagama latayelekile ngendlela lefanele abuye etame kupela emagama langakatayeleki asebentisa lwati lwakhe
 lwemisindvo
• Utakhela lwatimagama kanye nesichazamagama sakhe
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili)
Imisebenti yekusetjentiswa kwelulwimi (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
 Kulemisebenti bafundzi batawucala kugcila eluhlelweni lwelulwimi nasekusetjentisweni kwalo. Luhlelo lwelulwimi
kumele lufundziswe kusimongcondvo lesivakalako.
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise sikhatsi sanyalo lesilula
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise bunye nebunyenti (sib. ‘Kunencwadzi/kunetincwadzi etafuleni)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise buniyo (sib. lingephandle lencwadzi )
• Ubuyeketa luhlelo lwelulwimi lolwentiwe ngalokungakahleleki emaBangeni R - 2
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ngekusitwa, ubhala lisethi lelilula leticondziso
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 1
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lemine kuya kulesitfupha ngesihloko lesetayelekile

Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imibhalotihumusho yetitfombe asebentisa luhlaka: Kune ________. Kuneti ________.
• Ubhala imisho levakalako asebentisa buniyo ngaphandle kwekwenta emaphutsa

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 2
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 li-awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1 - 5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela kutsi betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama bente imisebenti
lebalwe lapha ngentasi. Yati kutsi letingcikitsi/tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje kuphela. Bothishela kumele
batikhetsele tabo tingcikitsi kuye ngesimo kanye netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi letinkhulu
tekufundza/emaphosta, tinkondlo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo mbamba letifanele ingcikitsi labayikhetsile.
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6 - 10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letinsha letitabavumela kutsi bakwati kwakhela kuloko labakwente emavikini ekucala
lasihlanu. Letingcikitsi kumele tibavumele bakwati kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama
lesifundzisiwe. Letingcikitsi kumele tivumele thishela akwati kuvuselela takhi telulwimi letilula ngalesikhatsi achumana
nebafundzi ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentise Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 6-10. Kumele bachubeke bente imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Imisebenti iyashiyana ngebudze. Thishela kumele ente munye umsebenti lomudze nobe lemibili nobe lemitsatfu lemifisha
ngeliviki.
• Uyachubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko
 letinjengaleti ‘Kuhlanyela Tintfo’
• Ulandzela ticondziso (sib. Gcwalisa umhlabatsi ebhodweni. Ubuye ufake inhlanyelo. Vala inhlanyelo ngemhlabatsi
 lomncanyana. Nisela inhlanyelo.’)
• Unika ticondziso letilula (sib. ‘Nyalo nisela inhlanyelo.’)
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula (sib. ‘Nini …?’ ‘Kungani …?’ ‘Ini’? (Ucabanga kutsi inhlanyelo itawukhula ibe yini)
• Ucela kucaciselwa (sib. ‘Angivisisi. Ngicela uphindze uchaze futsi.’)
• Uphendvula aphindze acele lokutsite (sib. Ngingasisebentisa yini sichazamagama sakho, ngiyacela nebakitsi?’
• Ubamba lichaza etingcocweni letimfisha letiphatselene nesihloko lesetayelekile (sib. Bangani babo kanye naloko
 labakwentako)
• Ubona intfo lechazwe ngemlomo (sib. ‘Ngimncane futsi ngicinile. Nawunginika umhlabatsi nemanti, ngikhula ngibe
 sitfombo. Ngiyini mine?’
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela ticondziso latinikwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Yini loyibona esitfombeni? Ngitjele
 kutsi ubonani esitfombeni).’
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula (sib. thishela ucoca ngesitfombo lesimilako. Ngicala ngokugcwalisa umhlabatsi ebhodweni.
 Bese ngifaka inhlanyelo ebhodweni ivalwe ngumhlabatsi. Ngemuva kwaloko nginisele inhlanyelo. Ngase ngiyibukela
 ikhula)
• Ulandzisa tehlakalo letilula ngemlomo (sib. ucoca ngekuhlanyela inhlanyelo nekuhlanyela sitjalo)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise takhi telulwimi letikusimongcondvo (sib. sikhatsi lesengcile “Nginisele sitjalo; bondzaweni:
 Ngifaka inhlanyelo ebhodweni.”)
• Ubamba ngenhloko aphindze ahaye tinkondlo letilula, imilolotelo lenemnyakato kanye netingoma (sib. Sitawuhlanyela
 emabhontjisi engadzini yetfu)
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi (sib. Ngibona ngeliso lami lelincane)
Kulalela nekukhuluma lokugcilwe kuko (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona naletifundziwe (Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ucoca nobe afundze indzaba kabili ngeliviki (nobe kulandzisa ngetehlakalo) kuye ngesikhatsi lesikhona.
Tindzaba leticocwako tingentiwa samdlalo kusetjentiswa kunyakata kwemtimba kanye netintfo letesekela inchazelo.
Tindzaba tifundvwa encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso lapho bonkhe bantfwana
batawukwati kubona titfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, kulandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso (sib. kulandzisa ngemaciniso, ticondziso,
imibiko lecuketse lwati lacocelwe lona nobe lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso
ngenhloso yekutitfokotisa

• Ulalela tindzaba (sib. Lizimu nemntfwana), alandzise ngaye abuye aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa njengekutsi
 ‘Lamtfola njani lizimu umntfwana?’)
• Ucombela lokutawulandzela endzabeni nobe alandzise ngaye (sib. Ucabanga kutsi kutakwentekani nyalo?)
• Uveta imiva yakhe lephatselene nendzaba (sib. Ingabe uyitsandzile yini indzaba? Kungani? Kungani ungakayitsandzi?)
• Uphindze acoce indzaba
• Ulalela itheksthi lengemaciniso njengekulandzisa lokunemaciniso nobe umbiko locuketse lwati, sib. Titjalo letikhulako
 abuye aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa, sib. Yini loyidzingako kute uhlanyele sitfombo?)
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela, ufinyeta itheksthi lelula lengemaciniso
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaaz emisebentini lengenhla:
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama (sib. Kuchaza inchubo –
 umjikeleto wemphilo)
• ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa abuye abe nelikhono lekusebentisa sakhi selulwimi lesilula kusimongcondvo selulwimi
 lolukhulunywako loluvakalako (sib. emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki ‘letinjengenhlanyelo’/’emanti’; tandziso-
 ekhatsi ebhodweni)

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

 ITHEMU 2
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Thishela ubuyeketa imisindvo lenetinhlavu letintsatfu, asebentisa emagama bafundzi labawatiko. Nakangenisa
ligama lelisha, ucinisekisa kutsi bafundzi bayayivisisa inchazelo yalo babuye balisebentise emshweni. Ubanika
imisebenti lebatayilalela ledzinga bafundzi bakhombe imisindvo lenemvulelwano. Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-
10 emaminithi) yentiwe liviki lonkhe. Lemisebenti ingaphindze ihlanganiswe nemisebenti yekulalela, yeKukhuluma
neKufundza.
Liviki 1 - 5
• Ubona imisindvo lenabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. ntf, nkh, nhl, ndl, ndv; intfombatane, inkhosi, inhlanhla,
 indlulamitsi, indvuna)
• Wakha abuye aphimise emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib. hlela, dlala, intfombatane, inkhosi)
• Wakha imisho asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib. Uhlela umsebenti wakhe, Intfombatane iyagijima; Tintsetse
 tigcwele esigangeni)
Liviki 6 -10
• Ubona emagama lanemvumelwano etingomeni, kumilolotelo netinkodlo ( sib.
• Wakha emagama asebentisa imisindvo lenabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. intfo, inkhomo, inhlama, indlela,
 indvodza;)
• Uhlukanisa ngemalunga emagama lanemisindvo lenabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. i-ntf-o, intfo; i-ndv-odza,
 indvodza)
• Wakha imisho asebentisa emagama lanemisindvo lefundziwe (sib. Intfombi yakhe ikhona; Uhamba nendvodza yakhe)
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela ( kwabelwe sikhatsi ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma )
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kungumsebenti wekufundza kanye nekulalela; ubuye uhlanganise nekukhuluma ngobe
bafundzi bakhuluma nathishela wabo ngetheksthi. EBangeni 3, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kutawuchubeka,
kodvwa kutawuchubeka njengencenye yekulalela nekukhuluma
• Ufundza nathishela itheksthi lebhaliwe lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe lenye itheksthi lekhulisiwe),
 asebentisa sihloko kucagela
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lemayelana nendzaba (sib. ‘Ngubani logijimise inja?’)
• Uchaza kutsi indzaba imente wativa njani, angantjintja lulwimi nakunesidzingo
Kufundza Ngemacembu basitwa Nguthishela (Linanincane lesikhatsi 45 emaminithi, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Uma bothishela basebentisa Linanikhulu lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bahlukanise liklasi
labo ngemacembu ekufundza lasihlanu alabanemakhono lafanako. Kumele basebentise indlela yekufundza lehlelwe
ngetigaba bacinisekise kutsi licembu ngalinye lisebenta ngetheksthi lesezingeni labo lekufundza (kubona emagama
ngaphandle kwemaphutsa lokusemkhatsini wa 90%-95%). Thishela kumele asebente nelicembu linye lilanga
ngalinye lokutsatsa emaminithi la-15. Ngesikhatsi asebenta nelicembu, lalabanye bafundzi batawufundza ngababili
nobe bafundze ngekutimela nobe bente imisebenti lephatselene netheksthi yekufundza.
Uma bothishela basebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bente umsebenti
wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela lowentiwa liklasi lonkhe katsatfu ngeliviki.
• Ufundza nathishela ngekuphimisela encwadzini ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela. Loku kusho kutsi lonkhe
 licembu lifundza nathishela indzaba lefanako nobe itheksthi lengemaciniso
• Usebentisa emasu lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute akhe umcondvo abuye aticaphele nakafundza (imisindvo,
 tinkomba tesimongcondvo, kuhlatiya sakhiwo, emagama lavamile)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekuphimisa lokuya ngekukhula
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa timphawu tekufundza nakafundza ngekuphimisa
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama lesivamile ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela,
 ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza ngekutimela
Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi bafundzi labakhululeke ngaso esikolweni kanye nasekhaya)

nabanesikhatsi eklasini, kwenta sibonelo, ngesikhatsi thishela asita licembu ngefundza, nobe uma bacedze
umsebenti ngaphambi kwesikhatsi. Kubalulekile kutsi litfuba lebalitfolako eklasini balisebentisele kutfutfukisa
kufundza kwabo. Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe futsi kufundza ekhaya njengemsebenti wasekhaya.

 nemibhalotihumusho kanye netindzabatitfombe ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letisekhoneni lekufundza eklasini

 yemagama langawati
