 ITHEMU 2
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
KUSETJENTISWA KWELULWIMI Kubhala:
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala ( Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa ngeliviki )
Munye nobe mibili imisebenti lapho kusetjentiswa khona Linanikhulu lesikhatsi kanye nemsebenti munye lapho
kusetjentiswa khona Linanincane lesikhatsi
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala ngesandla lawafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
• Ubhala tinhla letiya ngekuba lukhuni letinetihloko (sib. Tintfo letidzingwa titjalo: emanti, umhlabatsi, umoya, lilanga
• Ubhala itheksthi lelula (sib. likhadi lelusuku lwekutalwa)
• Ngekusitwa, ubhala lisethi leticondziso (sib. iresiphi)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lemine kuya kulesitfupha ngesihloko lasetayele
• Uhlelembisa lwati eshadini, ethebulini nobe kubhagrafu (sib. Umjikeleto wemphilo yesitjalo)
• Usebentisa luhlelo lwekubhala (kubhala luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala, kuhlela bunjalo bembhalo kanye nekushicilela)
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala lesetivele tifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (bongci, bokhefana, bomabuta, tibabato
 nabokhulumile)
• Upela emagama latayelekile ngendlela lefanele abuye etame kupela emagama langakatayeleki asebentisa lwati lwakhe
 lwemisindvo
• Utakhela silulumagama kanye nesichazamagama sakhe
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili)
Imisebenti yekusetjentiswa kwelulwimi (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Kulemisebenti bafundzi batawucala kugcila eluhlelweni lwelulwimi nasekusetjentisweni kwalo. Luhlelo lwelulwimi
kumele lufundziswe kusimongcondvo lesivakalako.
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise sikhatsi sanyalo lesichubekako
• Emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki (sib. Emabhodlela/emanti)
• Ubuyeketa luhlelo lwelulwimi lolwentiwe ngalokungakahleleki emaBangeni R kuya ku 2
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Uhlelembisa lwati eshadini, ethebulini nobe kubhagrafu
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo loluhlelekile 2
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lesitfupha kuya kulesiphohlongo ngesihloko lasetayele
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 3
• Ngekusitwa, ubhala lisethi leticondziso letilula (sib. Iresiphi)
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho levakalako asebentisa emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki ngalokungenamaphutsa
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 2
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ucedzela imisho asebentisa sikhatsi sanyalo lesichubekako
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 3
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho levakalako lesesikhatsini sanyalo lesichubekako ngalokungenamaphutsa

CAPS 73
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 3
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 li-awa ngeliviki

LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1 - 5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela kutsi betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama babuye bente
imisebenti lebalwe lapha ngentasi. Yati kutsi letingcikitsi/tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje kuphela. Bothishela
kumele batikhetsele tabo tingcikitsi kuye ngesimo kanye netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi
letinkhulu tekufundza/emaphosta, tinkondlo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo mbamba letifanele ingcikitsi labayikhetsile.
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona
Bothishela kumele bahlole bafundzi basebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6 - 10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letinsha letitabavumela kutsi bakwati kwakhela kuloko labakwentile emavikini ekucala
lasihlanu. Letingcikitsi kumele tibavumele bakwati kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama
lesifundzisiwe. Letingcikitsi kumele tivumele thishela akwati kuvuselela takhi telulwimi letilula ngalesikhatsi achumana
nebafundzi ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentise Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 6-10. Kumele bachubeke bente imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
• Uyachubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko
 letinjengaleti ‘Kwenta Tintfo’
• Ulandzela ticondziso (sib. ‘Udzinga emaphepha, emakokipeni, sikelo kanye netsambo yekubopha imaskhi. Cala
 ngekudvweba simo sebuso bakho. Landzela …. njll)
• Unika ticondziso letilula (sib. ‘Yipende ngembala lomtfubi)’
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula (sib. ‘Nini …?’ ‘Kungani …?’ ‘Uyenta njani’? (Uyenta njani imaskhi letfusako?)
• Ucela kucaciselwa (sib. ‘Angivisisi. Ngicela ungisite.’)
• Ubamba lichaza etingcocweni letimfisha letiphatselene nesihloko lesetayelekile (sib. kwentekani emmangweni)
• Ubona intfo lechazwe ngemlomo (sib. ubeka titfombe ngekulandzelana ngemuva kwekulandzela ticondziso tekwenta
 imaskhi)
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela ticondziso latinikwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Yini loyibona esitfombeni? Ngitjele
 kutsi ubonani esitfombeni.’)
• Ulalela kulandzisa lokulula (sib. thishela ukhuluma ngekutsi yentiwa njani imaskhi: Ngicale ngekudvweba simo sebuso
 bami. Ngase ngidvweba emehlo, imphumulo kanye nemlomo, njll)
• Ulandzisa ngetehlakalo letilula (sib. ukhuluma ngekwenta imaskhi)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise takhi telulwimi ngendlela lolusetjentiswe ngayo (sib. ukhombisa sabito sekukhomba: ‘Nginike
 leya nyama’)
• Ubeamba ngenhloko aphindze ahaye tinkondlo letilula, imilolotelo lenemnyakato kanye netingoma (sib. ‘Kunesipoko
 endlini’)
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi (sib. Ngibuka ngeliso lami lelincane …...
Imisebenti lekugcilwe kuyo yekulalela nekukhuluma (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona kanye naletifundziwe (Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ucoca nobe afundze indzaba kabili ngeliviki (kulandzisa ngetehlakalo). Tindzaba leticocwako tingentiwa
samdlalo kusetjentiswa kunyakata kwemtimba kanye netintfo letesekela inchazelo. Tindzaba tifundvwa encwadzini
lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso lapho bonkhe bantfwana batawukwati kubona titfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, kulandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso (sib. tindzaba letinemaciniso, ticondziso,
imibiko lecuketse lwati) lacocelwe yona nobe lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso
ngenhloso yekutitfokotisa
• Ulalela tindzaba (sib. Sipoko lesinebungani, nekulandzisa ngemphilo abuye aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa
 njengekutsi ‘Bantfu bangasibona yini sipoko lesinebungani?’)
• Ucombela lokutawulandzela endzabeni nobe alandzise ngaye (sib. Ucabanga kutsi kutawulandzelani?)
• Uveta imiva yakhe lephatselene nendzaba (sib. Ingabe uyitsandzile yini indzaba? Kungani? Kungani ungakayitsandzi?)
• Uphindze acoce indzaba

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

• Ulalela itheksthi lengemaciniso njengetheksthi leveta kulandzelana kwetehlakalo (sib. yentiwa njani imaskhi abuye
 aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa. Udzingani kute wente imaskhi)
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela, unika sifinyeto setheksthi lelula lenemaciniso
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati nesilulumagama
Ngekubamba lichaza emisebentini lengenhla:
• uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama (sib. kuchaza inchubo, kwenta
 tintfo)
• Ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa abuye abe nelikhono lekusebentisa sakhi selulwimi lesilula kusimongcondvo
 selulwimi lolukhulunywako loluvakalako (sib. sikhatsi lesengcile; emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki:
 ‘emamaskhi’/’liphepha’; tintfo
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kulalela nekukhuluma: (temlomo na/nobe kwenta)
Liviki 1-5

Liviki 6-10
• Ulalela kulandzisa ngemlomo lokufisha
• Unika kulandzisa ngemlomo lokufisha

• Uphindze acoce indzaba

CAPS 75
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 3
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Thishela ubuyeketa imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngababili nalabahamba ngabatsatfu. Nakangenisa ligama
lelisha, ucinisekisa kutsi bafundzi bayayivisisa inchazelo yalo babuye balisebentise emshweni. Wenta imisebenti
labatayilalela ledzinga kutsi bafundzi babone umehluko emkhatsini wekupela nekuphimisa emagama elulwimini
lwasekhaya naselulwimini lwekwengeta. Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-10 emaminithi) yentiwe liviki lonkhe.
Lemisebenti ingaphindze ihlanganiswe nemisebenti yekulalela yekukhuluma kanye neKufundza.
Liviki 1 - 5
• Ubona imisindvo yabongwaca lesekucaleni kweligama (sib. Themba, Phakamani)
• Ubona abuye akhe emagama asebentisa imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngababili (sib. hl, dl, kh, bh, tf; hlela, dlala,
 khipha, lutfo, njll)
• Ubona imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. ntf, nkh, nhl, ndl, ndv; inhlanyelo, indlulamitsi, tindvonga)
• Ubona umehluko emkhatsini webudlelwane bemisindvo nekupela emagama eLulwimini Lwasekhaya naseLulwimini
 Lwekwengeta ( Pheka – Philip)
• Wakha imisho asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib. Uhlela umsebenti wakhe, Udlala libhola; Intfombatane iyagijima;
 Sipho unenhlanhla)
Liviki 6 - 10
• Ubona imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu (sib. ntf, nkh, nhl, ndl, ndv; intfombatane, inkhosi, inhlanhla,
 indlulamitsi, indvuna)
• Wakha abuye aphimise emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe (sib. hlela, dlala, intfombatane, inkhosi)
• Uhlahlela emagama abe ngemalunga (sib.i –ndza-wo, i-ndle-la)
• Wakha imisho asebentisa emagama lanemisindvo lefundziwe (sib. Bomake belitsango bavunulile; Themba uhlala
 eMgobodzi )
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela (kwabelwe sikhatsi ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kungumsebenti wekufundza kanye nekulalela; ubuye uhlanganise nekukhuluma ngobe
bafundzi bakhuluma nathishela wabo ngetheksthi. Ebangeni le -3, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kutawuchubeka,
kodvwa kutawuchubeka njengencenye yekulalela nekukhuluma
• Ufundza nathishela itheksthi lebhaliwe lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe lenye itheksthi lekhulisiwe),
 asebentisa sihloko kucagela
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lephatselene nendzaba (sib. ‘Bantfu bangasibona yini sipoko lesinebungani?’)
• Uchaza kutsi indzaba imente wativa njani, angantjintja lulwimi nakunesidzingo
Kufundza Ngemacembu basitwa Nguthishela ( Linanincane lesikhatsi 45 emaminithi, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Uma bothishela basebentisa Linanikhulu lesikhatsiseLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bahlukanise liklasi
labo ngemacembu ekufundza lasihlanu alabanemakhono lafanako. Kumele basebentise indlela yekufundza lehlelwe
ngetigaba bacinisekise kutsi licembu ngalinye lisebenta ngetheksthi lesezingeni labo lekufundza (kubona emagama
ngaphandle kwemaphutsa lokusemkhatsini wa 90%-95%). Thishela kumele asebente nelicembu linye lilanga
ngalinye lokutsatsa emaminithi la -15. Ngesikhatsi asebenta nelicembu, lalabanye bafundzi batawufundza ngababili
nobe bafundze ngekutimela nobe bente imisebenti lephatselene netheksthi yekufundza.
Uma bothishela basebentisa Linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bente umsebenti
wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela lowentiwa liklasi lonkhe katsatfu ngeliviki.
• Ufundza nathishela ngekuphimisela encwadzini yakhe ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela. Loku kusho kutsi
 lonkhe licembu lifundza nathishela indzaba lefanako nobe itheksthi lengemaciniso
• Usebentisa emasu lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute akhe umcondvo abuye aticaphele nakafundza (imisindvo,
 tinkhombandlela tesimongcondvo, kuhlatiya sakhiwo, emagama lavamile)
• Ufundza ngekushelela nangekuphimisa lokuya ngekukhula
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa timphawu tekufundza nakafundza ngekuphimisa
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama lesivamile ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela,
 ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza ngekutimela.

Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi bafundzi labakhululeke ngaso esikolweni kanye nasekhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe bakwati kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
nabanesikhatsi eklasini, kwenta sibonelo, ngesikhatsi thishela asita licembu ngefundza, nobe uma bacedze
umsebenti ngaphambi kwesikhatsi. Kubalulekile kutsi litfuba lebalitfolako eklasini balisebentisele kutfutfukisa
kufundza kwabo. Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe futsi kufundza ekhaya njengemsebenti wasekhaya.
• Ufundza umbhalo wakhe kanye newalabanye
• Ufundza tincwadzi ngekutimela ngetikhatsi tekufundza basitwa nguthishela kanye nemabhuku lanetitfombe
 nemibhalotihumusho kanye netindzabatitfombe ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letisekhoneni lekufundza eklasini
• Ufundza tinkondlo nemilolotelo letayelekile
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana lesinetitfombe (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili) kute atfole inchazelo
 yemagama langawati

 ITHEMU 3
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
KUSETJENTISWA KWELULWIMI Kubhala:
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala (Linanincane lesikhatsi 30 emaminithi, Linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa ngeliviki)
Munye nobe mibili imisebenti lapho kusetjentiswa khona Linanikhulu lesikhatsi kanye nemsebenti munye lapho
kusetjentiswa khona Linanincane lesikhatsi
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala ngesandla lawafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
• Ubhala tinhla letiya ngekuba lukhuni letinetihloko (sib. Tintfo lotidzingako kute wente imaskhi: liphepha, emakokipeni, sikelo
 nentsambo)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lemine kuya kulesitfupha ngesihloko lasetayelekile
• Uhlelembisa lwati eshadini, ethebulini nobe kubhagrafu (sib. indzaba yekwenta imaskhi)
• Ngekusitwa, ubhala alandzise ngemphilo yakhe (sib. ‘Ngente imaskhi itolo. Ngicale ngeku …… Ngalandzela nge ……
 Kwalandzela …… Ngagcina nge ….)
• Usebentisa luhlelo lwekubhala (kubhala luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala nekuhlela bunjalo bembhalo)
• Usebentisa timphawu tekubhala lesetivele tifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (bongci, bokhefana, bomabuta, tibabato
 nabokhulumile)
• Upela emagama latayelekile ngendlela lefanele abuye etame kupela emagama langakatayeleki asebentisa lwati
 lwemisindvo
• Usebentisa sikhatsi lesengcile ngalokungenamaphutsa
• Utakhela sichazamagama sakhe
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili)
Imisebenti yekusetjentiswa kwelulwimi (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
 Kulemisebenti bafundzi batawucala kugcila eluhlelweni lwelulwimi nasekusetjentisweni kwalo. Luhlelo lwelulwimi
kumele lufundziswe kusimongcondvo lesivakalako.
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise sikhatsi lesengcile lesilula (sib. ‘Itolo ngente imaskhi’)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki (buyeketa)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise tibanjalo (sib. umuntfu/ngumuntfu, umntfwana/ngumntfwana)
• Ubuyeketa luhlelo lwelulwimi lolwentiwe ngalokungakahleleki emaBangeni R kuya ku 2

lokuhlelekile we-5

Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho levakalako asebentisa emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki ngalokungenamaphutsa
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile:4
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ucedzela imisho lesesikhatsini lesengcile
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile:5
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho levakalako lesesikhatsini lesengcile ngalokungenamaphutsa

CAPS 79
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 4
KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA (TEMLOMO) SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 1 li-awa ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Liviki 1-5
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letitabavumela kutsi betfule babuye bavuselele silulumagama babuye bente
imisebenti lebalwe lapha ngentasi. Yati kutsi letingcikitsi/tihloko letiphakanyisiwe titiphakamiso nje kuphela. Bothishela
kumele batikhetsele tabo tingcikitsi kuye ngesimo kanye netinsita letikhona. Kumele bacinisekise kutsi banetincwadzi
letinkhulu tekufundza/emaphosta, tinkondlo, tingoma, imidlalo kanye netintfo mbamba letifanele ingcikitsi labayikhetsile.
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentisa Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 1-5.
Liviki 6-10
Bothishela bakhetsa tingcikitsi letimbili letinsha letitabavumela kutsi bakwati kwakhela kuloko labakwentile emavikini ekucala
lasihlanu. Letingcikitsi kumele tibavumele bakwati kwetfula silulumagama lesisha babuye bahlanganise silulumagama
lesifundzisiwe. Letingcikitsi kumele tivumele thishela akwati kuvuselela takhi telulwimi letilula ngalesikhatsi achumana
nebafundzi ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta
Bothishela kumele betame kwenta imisebenti leminyenti ngalesikhatsi lebaniketwe sona. Kumele bahlole bafundzi
basebentise Imisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki lokuphakanyiselwe evikini 6-10. Kumele bachubeke bente imisebentiluhlolo
Loluhlelekile ekupheleni kwethemu.
Imisebenti yemalanga onkhe (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Imisebenti iyashiyana ngebudze. Thishela kumele ente munye umsebenti lomudze nobe lemibili nobe lemitsatfu lemifisha
ngeliviki.
• Uyachubeka nekutfutfukisa silulumagama setemlomo (kulalela nekukhuluma) asebentisa tingcikitsi nobe tihloko
 letinjengaleti ‘Titfutsi’
• Ulandzela abuye anike ticondziso
• Uvisisa abuye aphendvule imibuto lelula (sib. ‘Nini …?’ ‘Kungani …?’ ‘njani …’? (Uhamba ngani kuya esikolweni)
• Ucela kucaciselwa (sib. Tichazani ‘tekutfutsa?’)
• Uphendvula aphindze acele (sib. Ngingayisebentisa yini irula yakho, ngiyacela nebakitsi?’
• Ubamba lichaza etingcocweni letimfisha letiphatselene nesihloko lesetayelekile (sib. luhambo lwabo loluya esikolweni)
• Ubona intfo lechazwe ngemlomo (sib. ‘Yimoto. Yinkhulu futsi yindze. Itfwala tintfo kusukela embonini kuya esuphamakethe.
 Iyini?’)
• Ukhuluma ngetintfo letisesitfombeni alandzela ticondziso latinikwe nguthishela (sib. ‘Uyasibona yini sitimela esitfombeni?
 Ngikhombise sitimela.')
• Ulalela abuye anike kulandzisa lokulula ngemlomo (sib. luhambo lwami loluya esikolweni lamuhla)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise takhi telulwimi kusimongcondvo
• Ubamba ngenhloko aphindze ahaye tinkondlo letilula, imilolotelo lenemnyakato kanye netingoma (sib. Emasondvo ebhasi
 ayajikeleta)
• Udlala imidlalo yelulwimi (sib. Umdlalo weliketani – thishela uphimisa umsindvo bese ukhomba umntfwana lokumele
 acabange ngeligama lelicala ngaloyo msindvo; umntfwana usho lelo ligama, thishela ukhomba lomunye umntfwana, njll)
Kulalela nekukhuluma lokugcilwe kuko (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Ulalela tindzaba lacocelwe tona naletifundziwe (Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela)
Thishela ucoca nobe afundze indzaba kabili ngeliviki (nobe kulandzisa ngetehlakalo) kuye ngesikhatsi lesikhona.
Tindzaba leticocwako tingentiwa samdlalo kusetjentiswa kunyakata kwemtimba kanye netintfo letesekela inchazelo.
Tindzaba tifundvwa encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso lapho bonkhe bantfwana
bakwati kubona khona titfombe.
Ulalela tindzaba letimfisha, kulandzisa ngemphilo nobe ematheksthi langemaciniso (sib. tindzaba letinemaciniso, ticondziso,
imibiko lecuketse lwati) lacocelwe yona nobe lafundvwe encwadzini lenkhulu yekufundza nobe kumaphosta lanemifanekiso
ngenhloso yekutitfokotisa
• Ulalela tindzaba (sib. Indlovu nentfutfwane), nekulandzisa ngemphilo abuye aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa
 njengekutsi ‘Beyitfukutseliswe yini indlovu?’)
• Ucombela lokutawulandzela endzabeni nobe alandzise ngaye (sib. Ucabanga kutsi kutawulandzelani?)

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3

• Uveta imiva yakhe lephatselene nendzaba (sib. Ingabe uyitsandzile yini indzaba? Kungani? Kungani ungakayitsandzi?)
• Uphindze acoce indzaba
• Ulalela itheksthi lengemaciniso (sib. ‘Titfutsi’ ) abuye aphendvule imibuto lekhombisa kuvisisa. Bala tinhlobo tetitfutsi
 letintsatfu
• Ngekusitwa nguthishela, ufinyeta itheksthi lelula lengemaciniso
Kutfutfukiswa kwelwati, silulumagama netakhi telulwimi
Ngekubamba lichaza emisebentini lengenhla:
• uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama setemlomo, lokufaka ekhatsi lwati lwesilulumagama (sib. kucatsanisa)
• ucala kutfutfukisa kuvisisa abuye abe nelwati lekusebentisa sakhi selulwimi lesilula kusimongcondvo selulwimi
 lolukhulunywako loluvakalako (sib. sikhatsi sanyalo nesikhatsi lesengcile; emagama etintfo letibalekako naletingabaleki
 ‘letinjengetimoti’/’titfutsi’; takhi: bondzaweni, edolobheni, tiphawulo leticatsanisako: kakhulu, kakhudlwana)

CAPS 81
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU 4
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisindvo (15 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Thishela ubuketa imisindvo lenetinhlavu letintsatfu abuye etfule imisindvo lelula lenetinhlavu letine. Nakangenisa
ligama lelisha, ucinisekisa kutsi bafundzi bayayivisisa inchazelo yalo babuye balisebentise emshweni. Ematheksthi
eKufundza Ngekuhlanganyela asetjentiselwa kukhomba tijobelelo letehlukene. Lemisebenti kumele ifinyetwe (5-
10 emaminithi) yentiwe liviki lonkhe. Lemisebenti ingaphindze ihlanganiswe nemisebenti yekulalela nekukhuluma
kanye neKufundza.
Liviki 1-5
• Ubuketa imisindvo yabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu lefundvwe ethemini yesitsatfu (sib. ndl, ndv, ngw, dlw, khw, njw,
 nsw, nch, nkh, nsh, ncw, njll)
• Uhlahlela emagama lanabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu abe ngemalunga (sib. i-ndl-ela, indlela; i-ngw-e, ingwe; um-
 dlw-ane, umdlwane)
• Ubona bongwaca labahamba ngabane(sib. nftw, nkhw, ndlw, ntjw; intfwala, sinkhwa, indlwana, lintjwele)
• Uhlahlela emagama lanabongwaca labane abe ngemalunga (sib. i-intfw-ala, intfwala; si-nkhw-a, sinkhwa; i-ndlw-ana,
 indlwana; li-ntjw-ele, lintjwele)
• Wakha imisho ngemagama lanemisindvo lefundziwe (sib. Uhamba endleleni, Ingwe inemabala; Ulunywa yintfwala,
 Ngidle sinkhwa ebusuku)
Liviki 6-10
• Ubona emagama lahlobene (sib. Uhambile, usebentile)
• Ubona abuye asebentise tijobelelo (sib. Indlovukati, babekati, indlwana)
• Wakha abuye aphimise emagama asebentisa imisindvo lefundziwe
Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela (kwabelwe sikhatsi ngaphasi kwekulalela nekukhuluma)
Kufundza ngekuhlanganyela kungumsebenti wekufundza kanye nekulalela; ubuye uhlanganise nekukhuluma ngobe
bafundzi bakhuluma nathishela wabo ngetheksthi. Ebangeni le -3, Kufundza Ngekuhlanganyela kutawuchubeka,
kodvwa kutawuchubeka njengencenye yekulalela nekukhuluma
• Ufundza nathishela itheksthi lebhaliwe lemfisha (incwadzi lenkhulu yekufundza nobe lenye itheksthi lekhulisiwe),
 asebentisa sihloko kucagela
• Uphendvula imibuto lesebaleni lephatselene nendzaba (sib. ‘Bewunjani umbala wesikhumba sendlovu?’)
• Uchaza kutsi indzaba imente wativa njani, angantjintja lulwimi nakunesidzingo
Kufundza Ngemacembu basitwa Nguthishela ( Linanincane lesikhatsi 45 emaminithi, linanikhulu lesikhatsi 1 li-awa 15
emaminithi ngeliviki)
Uma bothishela basebentisa Linanikhulu lesikhatsiseLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bahlukanise liklasi
labo ngemacembu ekufundza lasihlanu alabanemakhono lafanako. Kumele basebentise indlela yekufundza lehlelwe
ngetigaba bacinisekise kutsi licembu ngalinye lisebenta ngetheksthi lesezingeni labo lekufundza (kubona emagama
ngaphandle kwemaphutsa lokusemkhatsini wa 90%-95%). Thishela kumele asebente nelicembu linye lilanga
ngalinye lokutsatsa emaminithi la-15. Ngesikhatsi asebenta nelicembu, lalabanye bafundzi batawufundza ngababili
nobe bafundze ngekutimela nobe bente imisebenti lephatselene netheksthi yekufundza.
Uma bothishela basebentisa linanincane lesikhatsi seLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta, kumele bente umsebenti
wekufundza ngekuhlanganyela lowentiwa liklasi lonkhe katsatfu ngeliviki.
• Ufundza nathishela ngekuphimisela encwadzini ekufundzeni ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela. Loku kusho kutsi lonkhe
 licembu lifundza nathishela indzaba lefanako nobe itheksthi lengemaciniso
• Usebentisa emasu lafundvwe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya kute akhe umcondvo abuye aticaphele nakafundza (imisindvo,
 tinkhomba tesimongcondvo, kuhlatiya sakhiwo, emagama lavamile)
• Ufundza ngekushelela lokungetekako, akhombisa kuvisisa
• Ukhombisa kuvisisa timphawu tekufundza nakafundza ngekuphimisa
• Uchubeka nekwakha silulumagama lesivamile ngesikhatsi sekufundza ngemacembu basitwa nguthishela,
 ngekuhlanganyela nekufundza ngekutimela.

Kufundza ngekutimela (ngesikhatsi bafundzi labakhululeke ngaso esikolweni kanye nasekhaya)
Bafundzi kumele bakhutsatwe kufundza ngekutimela eLulwimini lwabo Lwekucala Lwekwengeta nabanesikhatsi
eklasini, kwenta sibonelo, ngesikhatsi thishela asita licembu ngefundza, nobe uma bacedze umsebenti ngaphambi
kwesikhatsi. Kubalulekile kutsi litfuba lebalitfolako eklasini balisebentisele kutfutfukisa kufundza kwabo. Bafundzi
kumele bakhutsatwe futsi kufundza ekhaya njengemsebenti wasekhaya.
• Ufundza umbhalo wakhe kanye newalabanye
• Ufundza tincwadzi ngekutimela ngetikhatsi tekufundza basitwa nguthishela kanye nemabhuku lanetitfombe
 nemibhalotihumusho kanye netindzabatitfombe ngeLulwimi Lwekucala Lwekwengeta letisekhoneni lekufundza eklasini
• Ufundza tinkondlo nemilolotelo letayelekile
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana lesinetitfombe (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili) kute atfole inchazelo
 yemagama langawati

 nsw, nch, nkh, nsh, ncw, njll)
• Uhlahlela emagama lanabongwaca labahamba ngabatsatfu abe ngemalunga (sib. i-ndl-ela, indlela; i-ngw-e, ingwe; um-

• Ubona bongwaca labahamba ngabane (sib. nftw, nkhw, ndlw, ntjw; intfwala, sinkhwa, indlwana, lintjwele)

CAPS 83
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
 ITHEMU
KUBHALA SIKHATSI LESIPHAKANYISIWE
KUSETJENTISWA KWELULWIMI Kubhala:
 Linanincane lesikhatsi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
 Linanikhulu lesikhatsi: 1 li-awa ngeliviki
 Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: 30 emaminithi ngeliviki
LOKUCUKETFWE/ LWATI /NEMAKHONO
Imisebenti yekubhala (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Munye nobe mibili imisebenti yekubhala lebalulekile ngeliviki lapho kusetjentiswa khona linanikhulu lesikhatsi
besikhatsi kanye nemsebenti munye lapho kusetjentiswa khona linanincane lesikhatsi
Thishela kumele angenise luhlobo lolusha lwetheksthi – kubhala indzaba. Bafundzi badzinga kusitwa kantsi thishela
kumele acale ngekubhala indzaba kanye neliklasi. Ucala ngekucoca indzaba lelula bafundzi labayatiko. Ubuta
bafundzi ngebalingisi, kutsi indzaba yenteka kuphi nekutsi kwentekani endzabeni. Ubuta kutsi tehlakalo tendzaba
tikusiphi sikhatsi. Ucela bafundzi baticabangele ngebalingisi bendzaba yabo, basho nekutsi yenteka kuphi indzaba
nekutsi kwentekani kuyo. Bafundzi bakha indzaba ngekubambisana nathishela, lokunguye lobhala ebhodini.
Nakasacedzile thishela, ucela bafundzi labambalwa kutsi bafundze indzaba ngekuphimisa. Ngemuva kwaloko
bayitsatsela etincwadzini tabo tekubhala.
• Usebentisa emakhono ekubhala ngesandla lawafundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya
• Ubhala tinhla letiya ngekuba lukhuni letinetihloko (sib. Tinhlobo letahlukene tetitfutsi: libhayisikili, imoto, sitimela,
 sithuthuthu, lithekisi, ibhasi, umkhumbi, indiza)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lesitfupha kuya kulesiphohlongo ngesihloko lasetayele
• Uhlelembisa lwati eshadini, ethebulini nobe kubhagrafu (sib. indzaba yekwenta imaskhi)
• Ulandzisa ngalokwenteke emphilweni yakhe (sib. ‘Kuleliviki leliphelile ngi …. Ngicale nga ….. njll)
• Ubhala nathishela indzaba lelula bese ayitsatsela (Kubhala Ngekuhlanganyela)
• Usebentisa luhlelo lwekubhala (kubhala luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala, kuhlela bunjalo bembhalo kanye nekushicilela)
• Usebentisa timphawu lesetivele tifundziswe eLulwimini Lwasekhaya (bongci, bokhefana, bomabuta, tibabato
 nabokhulumile)
• Upela emagama latayelekile ngendlela lefanele abuye etame kupela emagama langakatayeleki asebentisa lwati lwakhe
 lwemisindvo
• Usebentisa sikhatsi lesengcile ngalokungenamaphutsa
• Utakhela sichazamagama sakhe
• Usebentisa sichazamagama sebantfwana (selulwimi lunye nesetilwimi letimbili)
Imisebenti yekusetjentiswa kwelulwimi (30 emaminithi ngeliviki)
Kulemisebenti bafundzi batawucala kugcila eluhlelweni lwelulwimi nasekusetjentisweni kwalo. Luhlelo lwelulwimi
kumele lufundziswe kusimongcondvo lesivakalako.
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise sikhatsi lesitako (sib. Ngitakuya edolobheni kusasa)
• Uvisisa abuye asebentise siphawulo lesicatsanisako (sib. Imoti igijima kakhulu kwengca libhayisikili. Indiza igijima kakhulu)
• Ubuyeketa luhlelo lwelulwimi lolwentiwe ngalokungakahleleki emaBangeni R kuya ku- 2
LUHLOLO
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala indzaba lelula ngekusitwa nguthishela
• Usebentisa luhlelo lwekubhala (kubhala luhlaka lwembhalo, kubhala, kuhlela bunjalo bembhalo kanye nekushicilela)
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile we-6
Kubhala: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala indzima lenemisho lemine kuya kulesitfupha ngesihloko lesetayelekile
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Lolungakahleleki:
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ucedzela imisho asebentisa tiphawulo leticatsanisako
Tiphakamiso temisebentiluhlolo Loluhlelekile 6
Kusetjentiswa kwelulwimi: (lokubhalwako)
• Ubhala imisho levakalako asebentisa tiphawulo leticatsanisako

 84 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
EMATHEKSTHI LAPHAKANYISIWE / TINSITA TEMNYAKA

KULALELA NEKUKHULUMA
• Titfombe nemaphosta
• Tintfo letiphatsekako letihambisana netingcikitsi, emaphaphethi, emamaskhi,njll
• Titfombe tekulandzelanisa
• Timphahla tekulingisa kanye nekulingisa indzaba
• Ema-CD nobe emathephu etindzaba (letifundziwe nobe lebacocelwe tona), tinkondlo, imilolotelo kanye netingoma,
 sidlalima-CD, sicophamavi, mabonakudze kanye nemavidiyo
• Tincwadzi tetindzaba kanye netindzaba temlomo
• Tinkondlo, tingoma nemilolotelo
• Imidlalo yelulwimi (temlomo)
KUFUNDZA NEMISINDVO
• Titfombe nemaphosta
• Tincwadzi letinkhulu tekufundza – letingasito emaciniso naletingenamaciniso
• Luhlelo lwekufundza loluphakanyisiwe (buncane betindzaba 10 / tincwadzi ngethemu)
• Lamanye ematheksthi lakhulisiwe (tinkondlo, tingoma, imilolotelo; lakhicitwe ekhaya nasesitolo)
• Tincwadzi letimfisha ‘letihlekisako’ letinemisho lemi 1-2 ekhasini kute tibekwe ekhoneni lekufundza
• Emafleshikhadi etintfo letisetjentiswa eklasini, umbukiso wemagama lavamile
• Lishadi lema-alfabhethi
• Indvuku letawusetjentiselwa kukhomba ematheksthi lakhulisiwe (sib. tindzaba taselubondzeni, imibukiso, njll)
• Sichazamagama sebantfwana lesinetitfombe (lesinelulwimi lunye nalesinetilwimi letimbili)
KUBHALA NENDLELA YEKUBHALA NGESANDLA
• Tinsita tekubhala (sib. ipenseli, emapenseli emibala, emakhilayoni emafutsa, emaphepha langakabhalwa lanebukhulu
 lobehlukene (A3, A4, A5), emapheshana ekubhalela)
• Emaphepha emashadi nemakoki
• Sichazamagama sebantfwana lesinetitfombe (lesinelulwimi lunye nalesinetilwimi letimbili)

CAPS 85
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
SIGABA 4: EMAGAMA LAVAMILE

Ngentasi kuneluhla lwemagama lavamile. Lamanye emagama asuselwa etihlokweni letisetincwadzini tebantfwana.
Tindzaba ticocwa tibe sesikhatsini lesengcile kungako nje tento tiba sesikhatsini lesngcile.

Loluhla lufakelwe kunika bothishela umcondvo welwatimagama bafundzi lokumele balutfole eLulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta. Batawufundza lamagama ngekulalela lokukhulunywako babuye bafundze ngelulwimi lweSiswati,
bawente incenye yesilulumagama sabo ngeKukhuluma nekubhala. Lamagama akumelanga afundziswe ngaphandle
kwesimongcondvo.

Emagama lafundvwa bantfwana kumele ahambisane netincwadzi letisetjentiswa bantfwana. Lulwimi Lwekucala
Lwekwengeta lufundvwa ngekulalela nekufundza, kumele bafundzi baniketwe imisebenti leminyenti lesesikhatsini
sanyalo

Luhla lwemagama

 1. mine 21.sona 41.sonkhe 61. leyo 81. bami
 2. yena 22. kona 42. lowo 62. leti 82. betfu
 3. bona 23. leto 43. lowa 63. sabo 83. bonkhe
 4. yona 24. tonkhe 44. leta 64. sobabili 84. bobane
 5. tona 25. tabo 45. tenu 65. sinabo 85.babo
 6. tsine 26. tetfu 46. nabo 66. sona 86. nami

Letinhla tiniketwe njengenkombandlela. Bothishela nabo kumele bagcine lirekhodi lemagama bafundzi labataweva
babuye bawafundze. Nangabe bafundzi sebakwati kufundza ngeLulwimi Lwekwengeta, thishela angacala
kufaka emagama elubondzeni lwaseklasini abuye akhutsate bafundzi basebentise sichazamagama sebantfwana
lesilula. Nasebakwati bafundzi kubhala, thishela angabakhutsata babhale emagama lamasha labahlangana nawo
kusichazamagama sabo ( nobe ebhukwini lesichazamaga). baniketwe emagama latabasita kwengeta silulumagama
sabo. Anganika bafundzi silulumagama nemidlalomibuto yesipelingi.

 3. busika 12. dzadzewetfu 21.timbali 30. umfana 39. sinkhwa
 4. likwindla 13. mnaketfu 22. titselo 31. imfene 40. libhodo

TINHLOBO TEMATHEKSTHI LAFUNDVWA ESIGABENI SABOKHEWANE

EsiGabeni saboKhewane, bafundzi batawulalela letinhlobo tematheksthi lalandzelako:

• kulandzisa ngaye nekulandzisa ngetintfo letingemaciniso

• kulandzelanisa (ticondziso)

• imibiko lenelwati

• tindzaba letilandzisako

Ebangeni le-3, batawulindzeleka kunika tilandziso temlomo letilula kanye neticondziso.

Ngalesikhatsi bacala kufundza nekubhala ngelulwimi lwabo Lwekwengeta, bafundzi batawufundza kulandzisa
lokulula, tilandziso, inchubo nembiko loletsa lwati. Ebangeni le-3, batawuphindze babhale tilandziso letilula, inchubo
netindzaba letilandzisako ngekusitwa nguthishela. Elulwimini Lwekwengeta, akukavumeleki kwetfula sikhatsi
lesengcile ekucaleni kwelibanga lekucala, tindzaba tingacocwa kusetjentiswe sikhatsi sanyalo.

Lapha ngaphasi kunetibonelo tetinhlobo tetheksthi letisetjentiswe esiGabeni saboKhewane.

 Ngubani? Ini? Nini?
libhontjisi ngalinye engilazini kute silibone likhula. Sambonya

tinkhudlwana, sabuye sabona sicu lesincane.

safaka libhontjisi ebhodweni.

CAPS 89
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
LuhlA lwemagama lasetjentisiwe

budlelwane bemisindvo – budlelwane lobukhona emkhatsini wemisindvo nekupelwa kwayo

emagama labonwa njalo – ngemagama bafundzi labawabona bangakahleli kepha bawabone ngalokutentakalelako

emagama lavame embhalweni – lawa ngemagama lavela kanyenti embhalweni

emakhadimagama – ngemakhadi lokubhalwa kuwo emagama latsite

emakhadimibhalo – ngemakhadi nobe emapheshana lacuketse emagama

emakhadimisho – ngemakhadi lokubhalwa kuwo imisho lemifisha

emakhono ekuchumanisa titfo – kusebentisa imiva yakho yekubona nekunyakata kwesandla (sib. kubhala)

emakhono ekuhlela emalunga – indlela yekuhumusha emagama latiwako nalabonwako lokufaka ekhatsi imisindvo,
kwakhiwa kwemagama kanye nekuhlatiywa kwembhalo lokwentiwa ngumfundzi wembhalo

emakhono emisipha – kusetjentiswa kwemisipha lemincane yesandla, usebentisa iminwe nemehlo (sib. kufaka
umtiya enyalitsini, kubhala)

imibhalo lengemaciniso – loku kusho imibhalo letsintsa imphilo letayelekile lengemaciniso (sib. liphephabhuku,
liphephandzaba, njll)

imibhalo lengesiwo emaciniso – loku kusho imibhalo letsintsa imphilo lengakatayeleki lengesiwo emaciniso

imibhalotihumusho – emagama labhalwe ngaphasi kwesitfombe, achaza bunjalo besitfombe

imibuto lesezingeni leliphakeme – imibuto ledzinga kutsi bafundzi babutse lwati etincenyeni letihlukene tembhalo

imibuto levalekile – imibuto ledzinga imphendvulo letsite (sib. “Ufuna litiya?” Imphendvulo kutakuba,ngu- “Yebo”)

imibuto levulekile – lengaphendvulwa ngetindlela letehlukene. Umfundzi angasebentisa emagama akhe

imibuto yekutikhumbuta - imibuto lefuna bafundzi bakhumbule tintfo letingemaciniso endzabeni

imidvwebotindvonga – umbhalo lonelwati lolwetfulwe ngemidvwebo, emagrafu nalokunye

imisebenti lekugcilwe kuyo – kusho imisebenti lokutawukhulunywa ngayo kumbe lokutawugcilwa kuyo kuleso
sifundvo

imvumelwane – ngemagama nobe imigca enkondlweni legcina ngemsindvo nobe ngankhamisa lofanako

indlela yekucaphela – umfundzi ucaphelisisa indlela lekacabanga ngayo lefaka ekhatsi kutfutfukisa emasu
ekufundza nekubhala

indlela yekutiphatsa nawufundza – nguloko lokwentiwa bafundzi nabafundza kute bahumushe emagama batfole
kutsi itheksthi ikhuluma ngani

indlela yekuvala – lisu lekukhipha emagama esiceshini lesitsite

indzabasitfobme – titfombe letilandzelanako letakha indzaba

 90 Sitatimende seNchubomgomo yeKharikhulamu neKuhlola
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
iphazili yetincetu – sitfombe lesijutjwe saba tincentu letinyenti umfundzi latama kutihlanganisa tibe sitfombe
lesiphelele

iplastisini – lubumba lwekudlala lolusetjentiswa bafundzi kubumba lokutsite

kubhala basitwa nguthishela – kufaka ekhatsi umfundzi ngamunye nobe licembu lelibhala tinhlobo letehlukene
tembhalo ngemuva kwekutsi thishela abafundzise tindlela letitsite tekubhala, njengeluhlaka lwekubhala, tiphumuti,
lulwimi kanye nesipelingi

kubhala lokuhlanganisile – indlela yekubhala lapho tinhlavu letinyenti temagama tihlanganiswe ndzawonye kute
umfundzi atewukwati kubhala ngekushesha

kubhala ngekuhlanganisa – indlela yekufundzisa kubhala ngesandla ube ubhala ngekuhlanganisa usebentisa
ipenseli

kubuka incwadzi ungenanhloso – kubuka incwadzi ngaphandle kwenhloso letsite lekwenta kutsi uyibuke kepha
ekugcineni kube khona lokutsite lokubonako

kucalisa kufundza nekubhala – kukhula kwelwati lwemfundzi lwemagama labhaliwe. Bafundzi babona lokubhaliwe
bese bacala kuvisisa inhloso yako

kucaphelisisa imisindvo – likhono lekwati kuhlukanisa imisindvo yemagama elulwimini

kucoca ngembhalo – kucoca ngembhalo kute uvisiseke

kucoca ngesitfombe – kukhuluma ngesitfombe nobe imifanekiso lesencwadzini kute kunike umcondvo waso

kudlala ngekukhululeka - sikhatsi lesivumela bafundzi kutsi bakhetse kutsi batawudlala kuphi, kanjani, batawudlala
ngani

kufundza kwelicembu basitwa nguthishela – sikhatsi sekufundza ngemacembu lapho bafundzi bonkhe basezingeni
linye lekufundza babe basitwa nguthishela

kufundza lokungakahlelwa – kufundza lokwenteka ngaphandle kwekufundza lokuhlosiwe

kufundza ngekuhlanganyela – kufundza ngekuhlanganyela umsebenti lapho bafundzi bafundza kanye nathishela
imibhalo lekhulisiwe

kufundza ngekutimela – lizinga lapho umfundzi angafundza itheksthi kahle afike ezingeni lelinge- 95%. Kuba lula
kufundza itheksthi kulelizinga lekufundza

kufundza ngekwenta – kufundza ngekutsi bafundzi babe benta lokutsite

kufundzisa kubhala ngesandla – loku kusho indlela yekufundzisa kubhala ngesandla, kungaba kubhala
ngekuhlukanisa nobe kubhala ngekuhlanganisa

kuhlahlela sakhiwo semusho – loku kufaka ekhatsi kuhlatiya tincenye teligama (sib. ticalo netiphetfo)

kuhlanganisa tinhlavu – kuhlanganisa tinhlavu letimbili nobe letintsatfu kwenta umsindvo lotsite (sib. bh- bhala,
tfw-tfwala)

CAPS 91
 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
kuhlela bunjalo bembhalo – kuhlela itheksthi ngendlela letawunika umcondvo longiwo nalophelele ngekutsi
ulungiswe ngendlela letawuvisiseka

kuhlola – kuhlola lokuhlelekile nalokuchubekako lokubutsela ndzawonye lwati ngekusebenta kwemfundzi

kuhlola lokuchubekako – kuhlola lokuchubekako lokuba yincenye yekufundza nekufundzisa kanye nekutfutfukisa
lwati lwemfundzi

kuhlola lokusisekelo – kuhlola kwekucala lokusetjentiswa kutfola kutsi nguluphi lwati lasavele analo umfundzi
angakacali kufundziswa

kuhumusha ligama – likhono lekuhumusha ligama lelibhaliwe bese ukhuluma ngalo, esikhatsini lesinyenti
kusetjentiswa lwati lwebudlelwane lobukhona emkhatsini wemisindvo.

kusebentisa imiva – kufundza ngekuticabangela kwenteka ngekusebentisa titfo tekubona nekulalela; loku kufaka
ekhatsi emakhono etibonwa newekuva

kushicilela – kungesikhatsi bafundzi bashicilela umsebenti wabo; benta kutsi watiwe ngulabanye (sib. ngekuwunika
thishela, bawubeke etindvongeni tendlu yekufundzela nobe ebhodini yetatiso)

kuvundla emkhatsini wemtimba – sento sekuvundla emkhatsini wemtimba emgceni longabonakali kusukela
enhloko kuya etintwaneni

kwakhiwa kwemisho – indlela lokuhlelwa ngayo emagama kute kwakhiwe imisho nobe emabintana, kungaba
yimitsetfo lelawulwa kuhlelwa kwelulwimi

likhona lekucabanga – likhona lelisendlini yekufundzela lapho bafundzi bangatibandzakanya emidlalweni
yemifanekisomcondvo

likhono lenkhombaphi – loku kusho kucala kubhala esandleni sesancele, uye esandleni sekudla

lisu – lisu umfundzi latibandzakanya kulo ekucabangeni ekufundzeni, nasekubhaleni kanye netemlomo kute
kufundza kuvisiseke

lizinga lekufundza kahle – lizinga lapho umfundzi angafundza itheksthi kahle afike ezingeni lelinge- 90%. Itheksthi
lokumele ifundvwe inebumatima kodvwa iyafundzeka

luchungechunge lwetincwadzi tekufundza - tincwadzi tekufundza letihlukene ngekwemazinga. Lamazinga
acala kulalula aye kulalukhuni. Bafundzi bafundza tincwadzi letinyenti kulelo lizinga badzimate bakwati kufundza
ngalokushelelako

mabitwafanana – nangabe libito/ligama linetinshokutsi letehlukene kepha liphinyiswa nobe lipelwa ngalokufanako

mcondvophika – ligama lelinemcondvo lophikisana newalelinye ligama aphilisanako

ngekulalela – kusho kulalela nobe kuva ngetindlebe

sikhanyisamcondvo – lokuveta simo lesingembi kwesigameko lesitsite

sipelingi sekutakhela – ngumtamo wekupela ligama kuye ngelwati umfundzi lanalo lebudlelwane emkhatsini
wetinhlavu nemisindvo

 LULWIMI LWEKUCALA LWEKWENGETA EMABANGA 1-3
ticondziso – loku kusho ticondziso lonikwa tona lokumele utilandzele

tincwadzititfombe – tincwadzi lokusetjentiswe kuto titfombe kute takhe indzaba letsite

umsebenti wekuhlola – umsebenti lohlukaniswe tigaba letihlukene lonikwa bafundzi nabahlolwa

umsindvo lomncane – incenye lencane yemsindvo weligama lelikhulunyiwe
